Antipasto Squares

Ingredients

  • 2 cans Crescent dinner rolls
  • ¼ lb thinly sliced Baked Ham
  • ¼ lb thinly sliced Provolone Cheese
  • ¼ lb thinly sliced Swiss Cheese
  • ¼ lb thinly sliced Genoa Salami
  • ¼ lb thinly sliced Pepperoni
  • 1 cup Roasted Red Peppers, drained
  • 3 eggs
  • 3 TB grated Parmesan Cheese
  • ½ tsp Black Pepper

Instructions

Preheat oven to 375 degrees.
Unroll one package of crescent roll dough, and cover the bottom of a 9x13 pan. Layer the Ham, Provolone cheese, Salami, Swiss cheese, Pepperoni and Red Peppers on top of the dough. In a bowl, beat the eggs lightly and stir in the Parmesan cheese and black pepper. Pour ¾ of this mixture over the peppers. Unroll the second package of dough and place over the top of the peppers. Brush with the remaining egg mixture. Cover with aluminum foil. Bake for 25 minutes. Remove foil and bake another 15-20 minutes or until dough is golden brown. Cut into squares. Serve warm or at room temperature. Enjoy!

"Fasten your hearts to the love of God and receive the mercy of our Lord Jesus Christ, who gives us eternal life"
Jude 1:21 TPT

Appetizers |

Artichoke Couscous Salad

Ingredients

  • 10 ounce box Near East Couscous
  • 15 ounce can artichoke hearts, chopped
  • 1 cup slivered almonds
  • 1 cup sun dried tomatoes
  • 1 cup fresh shredded or grated Parmesan Cheese
  • 1/4 cup Italian dressing of choice *you can use premade or make the sweet/sour Italian dressing on this website
  • 1/2 teaspoon kosher salt
  • 1/4 teaspoon freshly ground black pepper

Makes 8 servings

Instructions

  1. Cook couscous according to package directions. Toss to fluff and chill until cooled.
  2. Add the rest of the ingredients, stirring to combine. Serve or chill until ready to serve.

** sweet/sour Italian dressing recipe
1 cup sugar
1 1/2 cups apple cider vinegar
2 packets Good Seasons Italian Dressing Mix
2 ice cubes
1/4 cup vegetable oil

Bring vinegar to a boil.
2. Remove from heat.
3. Add sugar and stir until dissolved.
4. Add dressing mix, then ice.
5. Stir until the ice melts, then add oil.
6. Store in a covered container in the refrigerator.
7. Shake well before serving

this will last 14 days in refrigerator.

"Whoever gives thought to the word will discover good, and blessed is he who trusts in the Lord"
Proverbs 16:20 ESV

Appetizers |

Bacon Candy

Ingredients

  • Package of lean bacon (the leanest Walmart brand seems to be the best - "hickory smoked bacon" by Great Value)
  • 2 cups brown sugar
  • 1 Tbsp cinnamon

Instructions

  1. Cover a cookie sheet with aluminum foil and bake the bacon at 400 degrees for 10 minutes. The bacon can overlap on the tray because it is just being partially cooked to get some of the grease out of it.

  2. While the bacon is cooking, combine the cinnamon and sugar in a bowl.

  3. Line a cookie sheet with edges completely covered with foil.

  4. Place a cookie cooling rack on top of the foiled cookie sheet. Spray with Pam.

  5. Cut partially cooked bacon into squares about 1 inch x 1 inch. Dredge the cut bacon squares in the sugar mixture and place on the cookie cooling rack touching each other.

  6. Broil at 500 degrees, watching constantly for about 5-10 minutes. It is important to carefully watch the bacon because it can easily burn.

  7. Remove the caramelized bacon while it is still hot and let them cool separately on foil or a plastic plate so that they do not stick together.

  8. Caramelized bacon freezes well in plastic bags. When reheating, be sure to lay them out separately so that they do not stick together.

"As for God - His way is perfect; The word of the Lord proves true; He is a shield for all those who take refuge in Him." 2 Samuel 22:31

Appetizers |

Bacon Wrapped Smokies

Ingredients

  • 1 pound bacon, cut into thirds
  • 1 pound smokies
  • 1 stick butter
  • 2 cups brown sugar

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 375°F.

  2. Cut the bacon into thirds and wrap each smokie. Place all the wrapped smokies in a single layer in a baking dish.

  3. Melt the stick of butter and combine with 1 cup of brown sugar, stirring until mixed well.

  4. Pour the butter and brown sugar mixture over the smokies and bacon.

  5. Take the remaining cup of brown sugar and sprinkle evenly over the smokies.

  6. Bake for about 15-20 minutes, then increase the heat to 400°F for about 5 minutes or until the bacon becomes crispy.

Breadstick Dipping Sauce

Ingredients

  • 1 (6 ounce) can tomato paste
  • 1 (8 ounce) can tomato sauce
  • 1/4 tsp dried oregano or 1/2 tsp minced fresh oregano
  • 1/4 tsp dried basil or 1/2 tsp minced fresh basil
  • 1/4 teaspoon white sugar
  • 1 teaspoon minced garlic
  • 1/2 teaspoon garlic salt
  • 1/4 teaspoon minced fresh parsley

Instructions

In a medium bowl, combine all ingredients. Serve with easy breadsticks and enjoy!

Can be heated in saucepan if you prefer warm dipping sauce. Heat on medium low while breadsticks are baking then serve warm.

Surely your goodness and love will follow me all the days of my life, and I will dwell in the house of the LORD forever. Psalm 23:6

Appetizers |

Bruschetta

Ingredients

  • 6 roma (plum) tomatoes, chopped
  • 1/2 cup sun-dried tomatoes, packed in oil
  • 3 cloves minced garlic
  • 1/4 cup olive oil
  • 2 tablespoons balsamic vinegar
  • 1/4 cup fresh basil, stems removed
  • 1/4 red onion, chopped
  • 1/4 teaspoon salt
  • 1/4 teaspoon ground black pepper
  • 1 French baguette
  • 2 cups shredded mozzarella cheese

Instructions

  1. Preheat the oven on broiler setting.

  2. Chop tomatoes, basil, and red onion. Mince garlic.

  3. In a large bowl, combine the roma tomatoes, sun-dried tomatoes, garlic, olive oil, vinegar, basil, salt, and pepper. Allow the mixture to sit for 10 minutes.

  4. Cut the baguette into 3/4-inch slices.

  5. On a baking sheet, arrange the baguette slices in a single layer.

  6. Broil for 1 to 2 minutes, until slightly brown.

  7. Divide the tomato mixture evenly over the baguette slices.

  8. Top the slices with mozzarella cheese.

  9. Broil for 5 minutes, or until the cheese is melted.

"And we know that all things work together for good to those who love God, to those who are the called according to His purpose." Romans 8:28

Appetizers |

Buffalo Chicken Bites

Ingredients

  • 3 cups shredded cooked chicken (use 2 rotisserie chickens)
  • 1/4 cup to 1/2 cup hot sauce (depends how hot you like it)
  • 3 1/2 oz cream cheese, softened
  • 8 oz pkg sharp cheddar cheese, grated
  • 1 bunch green onions, chopped
  • 1 cup all-purpose flour
  • 4 eggs, lightly beaten
  • 1 box corn flakes, crushed

Instructions

Preheat oven to 350°F.

Line a large baking sheet with parchment paper.

In a large bowl, combine chicken, cream cheese, hot sauce, cheddar cheese, and green onions.

Roll a heaping tablespoon of mixture into a 1½-inch ball and place onto a plate or separate baking sheet.

Repeat with the remaining mixture.

Place flour in a shallow dish.

In a second shallow dish, place eggs.

In a third shallow dish, place corn flakes.

Dip each chicken ball first into the flour, then the egg, and ending with the corn flakes.

Place on the prepared baking sheet and bake for 20 to 25 minutes.

Serve warm with ranch or blue cheese dressing.

"Take delight in the LORD, and he will give you the desires of your heart."
Psalm 37:4 NIV

Appetizers |

Cheesy Sweet Potato Crisp

Ingredients

  • 2 Sweet Potatoes, peeled
  • 2 Egg Whites
  • 1 cup Parmesan Cheese, grated
  • 1/2 tsp Rosemary
  • 1/2 tsp Pepper

Yogurt Dip:

  • 1 cup Plain Greek Yogurt
  • 1/4 tsp garlic powder
  • 1/4 tsp paprika
  • 1/4 tsp cumin

Instructions

Preheat oven to 425.

Spray a cookie sheet with non-stick spray.

Grate peeled sweet potatoes into a bowl.

Squeeze all the moisture from them - this takes several times and minutes.

In a large bowl, combine grated sweet potatoes, egg whites, cheese, pepper, and rosemary. Mix well.

Scoop out 1 TB of the mixture onto the cookie sheet.

Press down as thin as possible.

Bake for 20-30 minutes or until potatoes are dark around the edges and crispy.

While potatoes are baking, prepare yogurt dip.

In a medium mixing bowl, combine yogurt, garlic powder, paprika, and cumin.

Refrigerate until ready to use.

When potato crisps are finished baking, serve with yogurt dip and enjoy!

"Search me, God, and know my heart:
Test me and know my anxious thoughts...
See if there is any offensive way in me,
and lead me in the way everlasting."
Psalm 139:23-24 NIV

Appetizers |

Chicken Flautas with White Queso

Ingredients

  • Chicken Flautas:

    • 4-6 Boneless cooked chicken breasts, shredded
    • 8 oz Picante Sauce
    • 8 oz Monterey Jack Cheese, shredded
    • ¼ tsp Cumin
    • 24 Corn Tortillas
    • Pam spray

  • White Queso:

    • 16 oz Velveeta White Queso Cheese
    • 8 oz Cream Cheese, softened
    • Small can chopped Green Chilis
    • Milk as needed to thin queso

Instructions

Chicken Flautas:

  1. Preheat oven to 350 degrees.
  2. In a large mixing bowl, combine shredded chicken, picante sauce, cumin, and cheese.
  3. Spray Pam in a small skillet and heat skillet over medium-high heat.
  4. Place a corn tortilla in the pan for 2-4 seconds on each side to soften.
  5. Place 1-2 tablespoons of chicken mixture in the center of the tortilla and roll up.
  6. Place tortilla on a lightly greased cookie sheet, seam side down.
  7. Repeat with all the tortillas until chicken mixture is gone.
  8. Bake tortillas in the oven for 15-25 minutes or until tortillas are a little crispy and golden.
  9. Serve with white queso and enjoy!

White Queso:

  1. In a medium saucepan, melt Velveeta pepper jack cheese, cream cheese, and green chilis.
  2. Add milk if necessary to thin.
  3. Serve with flautas or chips and enjoy!

“One thing I ask of the LORD, this is what I seek: that I may dwell in the house of the LORD all the days of my life, to gaze upon the beauty of the LORD and to seek him in his temple.” Psalm 27:4

Appetizers |

Chicken Puffs

Ingredients

  • 4 skinless boneless chicken breasts, cooked
  • 4 TB butter
  • 8 oz cream cheese, cubed
  • 1 tsp salt
  • 1/2 tsp pepper
  • 1/4 cup milk
  • 2 TB minced onions
  • 3 cans crescent rolls

Instructions

  1. Cut or shred chicken. Set aside.
  2. Melt butter and cream cheese in skillet over medium heat, stirring often and watching not to burn cheese.
  3. To butter and cream cheese mixture, add salt, pepper, milk, onions, and chicken. Stir until all mixed together. Remove from heat.
  4. Open crescent roll cans and separate dough. Place 1 TB of chicken mixture in the center of each dough piece and roll, pinching sides to close.
  5. Place on greased cookie sheet. Bake at 350 degrees for 15 minutes.
  6. Enjoy!

Appetizer size: Cut each crescent roll dough into thirds. Place 1 tsp of chicken mixture in the middle and roll up.

"Commit your work to the Lord, and your plans will be established" Proverbs 16:3 ESV

Appetizers |

Cold Spinach Artichoke Dip

Ingredients

  • 14 oz can artichoke hearts, drained and chopped
  • 8 oz container sour cream
  • 1 cup mayonnaise
  • 3 tbsp chopped onion
  • 1 tsp lemon zest
  • 1 tbsp fresh lemon juice
  • 1 envelope vegetable soup mix
  • 10 oz package frozen chopped spinach, thawed and drained

Ingredients for Homemade Tortilla Chips:

  • 1 package flour or corn tortillas
  • Cooking spray
  • Seasoning – salt, garlic salt, or seasoned salt (flavor is up to you)

Instructions

  1. In a large mixing bowl, combine all ingredients. Cover and refrigerate for 30 minutes to 2 days. Serve with fresh vegetables or homemade tortilla chips. Enjoy!

Directions for Homemade Tortilla Chips:

  1. Preheat oven to 350 degrees.
  2. Cut tortillas into triangles or use cookie cutters to make shapes.
  3. Lay tortillas onto a cookie sheet lined with parchment paper.
  4. Spray with cooking spray and season with the seasoning of your choice.
  5. Bake for 8-15 minutes or until golden.
  6. Turn over, spray, and season again.
  7. Return to oven for 3-5 minutes.
  8. Store in a ziplock bag. Enjoy!

"Create in me a pure heart, O God, and renew a steadfast spirit within me."
Psalm 51:10

Appetizers |

Crab Cakes

Ingredients

  • 1 cup seasoned bread crumbs
  • 1 egg, beaten
  • 1/3 cup milk
  • 2 cans crab meat, drained
  • 2 tsp crab boil seasoning
  • 1 TB baking powder
  • 1 TB chopped parsley
  • 1/2 red bell pepper, chopped
  • 1 tsp Worcestershire sauce
  • 1 tsp hot pepper sauce
  • 2 TB tartar sauce
  • 1 tsp horseradish
  • 1 Jiffy cornbread mix
  • Vegetable oil for frying

Instructions

  1. Combine bread crumbs, egg, and milk.

  2. Add crab meat, crab boil seasoning, baking powder, parsley, red pepper, Worcestershire sauce, hot pepper sauce, tartar sauce, and horseradish. Mix all ingredients well and form into patties.

  3. Coat patties with cornbread mix and set aside.

  4. Add 1/2 inch of vegetable oil to a skillet and heat to medium to medium-high temperature.

  5. Fry cakes for 3 minutes on each side.

  6. Remove and drain on paper towels.

  7. Serve with red sauce, tartar sauce, or enjoy just the way they are!

"Do not judge, or you too will be judged. For in the same way you judge others, you will be judged, and with the measure you use, it will be measured to you." Matthew 7:1-2

Creamy Cilantro Sauce

Ingredients

  • 8 oz Cream Cheese, Softened
  • 1 TB Sour Cream
  • 7 oz jar of Salsa
  • 1 tsp Black Pepper
  • 1 tsp Celery Salt
  • ½ tsp Cumin
  • 2 tsp Garlic Powder
  • 1 cup Chopped Cilantro
  • 1 TB fresh Lime Juice

Instructions

Preparation:
Chop Cilantro.
In a medium mixing bowl, combine all ingredients with a mixer.
Blend until smooth and creamy.
Serve with Southwestern Egg Rolls and enjoy!

Appetizers |

Cupcake Filling

Ingredients

  • 3/4 of 16 oz jar marshmallow creme
  • 1/2 cup softened butter
  • 1/4 tsp vanilla
  • 4 tbsp whipping cream
  • 1 1/2 cups powdered sugar

Instructions

  1. Beat marshmallow creme, butter, vanilla, and whipping cream until smooth.

  2. Slowly add powdered sugar and increase speed to medium high. Beat for 1 minute.

  3. Refrigerate until ready to use.

  4. Keep filled cupcakes refrigerated.

"In the beginning, God created the heavens and the earth" Genesis 1:1 ESV

Appetizers |

Deviled Eggs

Ingredients

  • 6 hard-boiled eggs
  • 1/2 tsp mustard
  • 2 TB mayonnaise
  • 1/4 cup chopped onion
  • 2 dill pickles, chopped
    • or
  • 2 TB pickle relish
  • salt and pepper, to taste
  • paprika for topping

Instructions

To boil an egg:

  1. Place eggs in a single layer in a saucepan, covering with water.
  2. Place lid on pan and bring water to a boil.
  3. Remove pan from heat, leave lid on, and let sit for 15 minutes.
  4. Remove from water and rinse in cold water until cool.
  5. Peel under running water.

To make Deviled Eggs:

  1. Peel eggs and slice in half.
  2. Remove yolk and place in a large mixing bowl.
  3. Add mustard, mayonnaise, onion, and pickles.
  4. Mix together until creamy.
  5. Season with salt and pepper.
  6. Spoon mixture into egg whites and sprinkle with paprika.

Serve immediately or refrigerate until ready to serve. Enjoy!

Blessed is the man who trusts in the Lord, whose confidence is in Him.
Jeremiah 17:7

Appetizers |

Egg Roll

Ingredients

See Below.

Instructions

Brown ground pork with chopped onion. Add salt, garlic salt, pepper, and soy sauce (to taste). When completely brown, add 1 pkg cole slaw mix and cook until tender. Drain mixture of all juice (Kathy uses a colander to drain). While meat is draining, make a paste.

In the same skillet (you don’t have to wash it), mix 1/4 cup water, 2 tsp corn starch, and 2 TB soy sauce. Cook on low heat till thick. Place meat mixture in a large bowl, add paste and 1 egg. Mix well. (Let cool for about 30 minutes)

Now You're Ready to Roll:

Mix 1/2 cup water and 2 TB corn starch. This will be the glue to seal the wrapper. Place about 3 TB of mixture on egg roll wrapper, wrapper points up. Seal wrapper with glue. Fry until golden brown. Enjoy!

Serve with fried or steamed rice, stir fry vegetables and wontons.

"O Lord, in the morning you hear my voice; in the morning I prepare a sacrifice for you and watch" Psalm 5:3

Appetizers |

Fiesta Cheesecake

Ingredients

  • 1 1/2 cups finely crushed tortilla chips
  • 1/4 cup butter, melted
  • 2 (8 oz) pkgs. cream cheese, softened
  • 1 (3 oz) pkg. cream cheese, softened
  • 2 large eggs
  • 2 1/2 cups shredded pepper jack cheese
  • 1 can chopped green chilies, drained
  • 1/4 tsp ground red pepper
  • 8 oz sour cream

These items can be used to decorate the top for Christmas

  • 1/2 cup chopped green pepper
  • 1/2 cup chopped yellow pepper
  • 1/2 cup chopped red pepper
  • 1/2 cup chopped green onion
  • 2 tablespoons finely chopped olives
  • 2 bunches fresh parsley or cilantro (optional)

Instructions

This recipe needs to be made the night before to allow time to cool and chill.

Preheat oven to 325 degrees.

Combine tortilla chips and butter.

Press onto bottom of a lightly greased 9" springform pan.

Bake for 15 minutes. Cool on wire rack.

Beat cream cheese at medium speed for 3 minutes or until fluffy; add eggs, one at a time, beating after each addition. Stir in cheese, chilies, and ground red pepper.

Pour into prepared pan, and bake at 325 degrees for 30 minutes.

Cool for 10 minutes on a wire rack.

Gently run a knife around edge of pan to release sides and let cool completely.

Spread sour cream evenly over top; cover and chill.

To serve:
Place on top of a bed of fresh cilantro or parsley.
Serve with crackers or tortilla chips. Enjoy!

*** You can decorate this cheesecake for any holiday.
Christmas:
arrange sliced bell peppers in the shape of a Christmas tree. Use red and yellow bell peppers for oranaments and olives for the trunk.
Halloween:
arrange carrot slices in the shape of a pumpkin. Olives for the face, slice of green bell pepper for the stem
Valentines:
arrange sliced red bell peppers in the shape of a heart or cut small heart shapes out of a red bell pepper
Spring:
Create flowers on top using sliced green bell peppers for the stem, and red, yellow and orange bell peppers for the flowers.

"While the earth remains, seedtime and harvest, cold and heat, summer and winter, day and night, shall not cease." Genesis 8:22 ESV

Appetizers |

Jalapeno Jelly

Ingredients

  • 4 fresh jalapeno peppers, seeded and chopped
  • 1/2 green bell pepper, seeded and chopped
  • 1/2 red bell pepper, seeded and chopped
  • 1/2 cup cider vinegar (5% acidity)
  • 1/2 (3 oz) pkg liquid pectin
  • 2 tbsp lime juice

Instructions

Wear gloves when working with the jalapeno peppers. The oil from these peppers will stay on your hands for days and can burn your skin, eyes, mouth, or anything your hands come in contact with.

  1. Process jalapeno pepper and bell peppers in a food processor until smooth, stopping several times to scrape down the sides.

  2. Combine pepper puree, sugar, and cider vinegar in a large saucepan. Bring to a boil over medium-high heat, stirring constantly. Once it starts to boil, continue boiling for 3 minutes; stir in pectin and lime juice. Boil for 1 minute, stirring constantly. Remove from heat, and skim off foam with a metal spoon.

  3. Pour into hot sterilized jars, filling to 1/4 inch from the top. Wipe jar rims. Cover at once with metal lids and screw on bands; cool.

  4. Store in refrigerator. Serve over cream cheese with crackers or toast. Makes 3 half-pint jars.

Lea Ann's Ham Sandwiches

Ingredients

  • 12 mini croissants
  • 1 cup butter, room temperature
  • 1/4 cup mustard
  • 1/2 tsp poppy seeds
  • 2 tbsp minced onion
  • 2 lbs shaved ham
  • 12 slices Swiss cheese, cut to fit shape

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 325 degrees.

  2. Slice croissants in half.

  3. Combine butter, mustard, poppy seeds, and onion.

  4. Spread on half of each croissant.

  5. Top with 2 1/2 oz of ham and a slice of cheese.

  6. Put halves together.

  7. Wrap each sandwich in foil and place in oven for 15 minutes.

  8. These sandwiches may be frozen in foil and warmed, unwrapped at 325 degrees for 25-30 minutes.

Appetizers |

Lemon Chicken Tenders

Ingredients

  • Paprika
  • Salt
  • Pepper
  • 4 boneless, skinless chicken breasts, cut into 2-inch strips
  • 2 eggs, slightly beaten
  • 1 cup seasoned bread crumbs (or panko crumbs)
  • 1/2 cup sugar
  • 1/2 cup fresh squeezed lemon juice (about 2-3 lemons)
  • 1 1/2 teaspoons curry powder

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 400 degrees F (200 degrees C).

  2. Spray a baking sheet with cooking spray.

  3. Season chicken strips with paprika, salt, and pepper.

  4. Dip strips in egg, then dredge in bread crumbs.

  5. Arrange chicken strips in a single layer on prepared baking sheet.

  6. Bake in preheated oven for 15 minutes, turning once.

  7. While chicken is cooking, in a small saucepan over medium-low heat, stir together sugar, lemon juice, and curry powder, stirring until sugar dissolves, about 5 minutes.

  8. Drizzle lemon sauce over chicken, return to oven, and bake 5 minutes more.

  9. Serve as appetizers or with pasta or rice for main course. Enjoy!

Appetizers |

Mini Quiche

Ingredients

  • 1 pkg refrigerated pie crusts (2 crusts)
  • 1/2 cup Half & Half
  • 2 eggs
  • Dash of salt and pepper
  • 1/2 cup shredded cheese
  • 1/2 cup chopped or crumbled precooked meat
  • Your choice of vegetables, chopped (e.g., mushrooms, bell pepper, green onion, broccoli, zucchini, etc.)

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 375 degrees.

  2. Let pie crusts stand at room temperature for 15 minutes.

  3. Lightly spray mini-muffin pan with vegetable oil.

  4. In a small bowl, whisk milk and eggs together. Set aside.

  5. Cut pastry circles from each pie crust with a biscuit cutter or juice glass. Roll into balls and press one into each greased muffin cup using the mini-tart shaper. Be careful not to puncture the bottom of crusts in cups.

  6. Sprinkle your choice of meat, vegetables, and/or cheese into mini crusts. Fill crusts with egg mixture, careful not to overfill.

  7. Bake 17-20 minutes or until crusts are light golden brown. Cool in pan 2 minutes.

  8. Carefully remove mini quiches from pan. Serve warm. May be baked ahead of time and frozen until ready to use.

Makes 24 mini quiches.

Pepperoni Cheese Loaf

Ingredients

  • 1 can Pillsbury Crusty French Bread
  • 1 egg, beaten
  • 1/2 cup grated Parmesan Cheese
  • 8 oz sliced Pepperoni
  • 2 cups shredded Mozzarella Cheese
  • 1/2 tsp Oregano

Instructions

Appetizers |

Pizza Pinwheels

Ingredients

  • 1 can crescent roll dough (preferably the full sheet can)
  • 2 cups shredded Mozzarella cheese
  • 24 slices of pepperoni, chopped
  • 14 oz can or jar of pizza sauce
  • Italian Seasoning

Instructions

Preheat oven to 375 degrees.

Place dough on parchment paper and cut roll in half.

Roll out one half of dough into a rectangle shape.

Spread desired amount of pizza sauce over the dough, followed by half of the mozzarella cheese, then half of the pepperoni.

Starting at the long end of the dough, start rolling tightly until dough is rolled into an 8-inch log.

Repeat with the second rectangle. Place in the freezer until firm, about 15 minutes.

Remove from freezer and cut each log into 6 to 8 pieces and place on a baking sheet covered with parchment paper.

Sprinkle evenly with Italian seasoning (you can freeze them at this point if you want to bake them later).

Bake in the preheated oven until golden brown, about 12 minutes. Serve with the remaining pizza sauce for dipping.

Appetizers |

Potato Skins

Ingredients

  • 5 lb bag of potatoes (these are smaller and better bite size)
  • 1 stick butter, melted
  • 1 tsp salt
  • 1 tsp garlic powder
  • 1/2 tsp paprika
  • 1 tsp pepper
  • 2 cups Cheddar/Jack cheese, grated

Optional Toppings:

  • 8 oz sour cream
  • 4 green onions, chopped
  • 7 strips of bacon, cooked crisp and crumbled

Instructions

  1. Bake potatoes until done. (I use my microwave - it's faster)

  2. Cut potatoes in half, scoop out center, leaving a 1/4 inch shell.

  3. Place potato skins on a greased baking sheet.

  4. In a small mixing bowl, combine butter and seasonings.

  5. Brush over both sides of potato skins.

  6. Bake in preheated oven at 475 degrees for about 7 minutes.

  7. Turn over and bake on the other side until crisp, about 7 more minutes.

  8. Remove from oven and sprinkle grated cheese and bacon on top of skins.

  9. Turn oven to broil and broil until cheese is bubbly and melted.

  10. Place on serving dish. Top with sour cream and onions.

Enjoy!

Ranch Crackers

Ingredients

  • 1 stick melted butter
  • 1 pkg. Ranch dressing mix
  • 1/4 cup grated Parmesan (the jar kind)

Instructions

  1. In a large bowl, combine first 5 ingredients.

Appetizers |

Restaurant-style Salsa

Ingredients

  • 1 can (28 ounce) whole tomatoes with juice
  • 2 cans (10 ounce) Rotel (diced tomatoes and green chilies)
  • 1/4 cup chopped onion
  • 1 clove garlic, minced
  • 1 whole jalapeno, quartered and sliced thin
  • 1/4 teaspoon sugar
  • 1/4 teaspoon salt
  • 1/4 teaspoon ground cumin
  • 1/2 whole lime juice

Instructions

Note: This is a very large batch. Recommend using a 12-cup food processor, or you can process the ingredients in batches and then mix everything together in a large mixing bowl.

Combine whole tomatoes, Rotel, onion, jalapeno, garlic, sugar, salt, cumin, lime juice, and cilantro in a blender or food processor. Pulse until you get the salsa to the consistency you'd like — I do about 10 to 15 pulses. Test seasonings with a tortilla chip and adjust as needed.

Refrigerate salsa for at least an hour. Serve with tortilla chips or cheese nachos.

Appetizers |

Sassy Crackers

Ingredients

  • 1 cup vegetable oil
  • 1 teaspoon garlic powder
  • 1 teaspoon onion powder
  • 1/2 teaspoon black pepper
  • 1 (1 oz) envelope Italian dressing
  • 1 (1 oz) envelope ranch dressing mix
  • 3 tablespoons crushed red pepper flakes
  • 2 packages oyster crackers

Instructions

Directions

  1. Combine the vegetable oil, garlic powder, onion powder, black pepper, ranch dressing mix, and crushed red pepper flakes in a large mixing bowl and whisk together.
  2. Add oyster crackers to the bowl and stir to toss and coat well.
  3. Place the crackers into a 2-gallon ziplock bag and seal it.
  4. Let the bag sit for about 1 hour, then turn it over. Repeat several more times until the crackers are well-coated with spice mix, and allow the bag to sit overnight.
  5. Remove crackers and serve.

Appetizers |

Sausage pinwheels

Ingredients

  • 1 can crescent rolls
  • 1 lb roll of sausage
  • 1 (8 oz) pkg cream cheese, softened

Instructions

Preheat oven to 375 degrees.

  1. In a small skillet, brown sausage.

  2. In a medium-size mixing bowl, mix sausage and cream cheese.

  3. Open crescent rolls. On an ungreased cookie sheet, unroll the 2 sections and press seams together.

  4. To make sausage pinwheels:

    • Smooth a thin layer of the cream cheese and sausage mixture over crescent rolls.
    • Roll into a log and slice. Place on a cookie sheet.

  5. Bake at 375 degrees for 15 minutes or until golden brown.

  6. Serve immediately!

these can be great for brunch too!

South of the Border Mix

Ingredients

  • 6 TB butter
  • 1 pkg dry taco seasoning
  • 1 TB Worcestershire sauce
  • 1 tsp hot pepper sauce
  • 1 cup peanuts
  • 8 cups Rice, Corn or Wheat Chex
  • 1 cup broken pretzel pieces
  • 1 cup Cheez-Its
  • 2 TB cheese powder (optional)

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 250 degrees.

  2. In a large glass bowl, melt butter in microwave.

  3. Stir in taco seasoning, Worcestershire sauce, and pepper sauce.

  4. Gradually add Chex, pretzels, peanuts, and Cheez-Its; stirring to coat evenly.

  5. Pour onto a large baking sheet.

  6. Bake for 1 hour, stirring every 15 minutes.

  7. Spread on absorbent paper to cool.

  8. Sprinkle with cheese powder, if desired.

  9. Store in an airtight container.

Enjoy!

Appetizers |

Southwestern Egg Rolls

Ingredients

  • 1 chicken breast, cooked
  • 2 tbsp vegetable oil
  • 4 green onions, minced
  • 1/2 red bell pepper, minced
  • 1/2 cup corn, drained
  • 1/2 cup black beans, drained
  • 2 tbsp frozen spinach, thawed and drained
  • 1/2 jalapeno pepper, minced
  • 1/2 tsp cumin
  • 1/2 tsp chili powder
  • 1/2 tsp salt
  • Pinch of red pepper
  • 4 oz Monterey Jack cheese, shredded
  • 8 egg roll wrappers

Instructions

Preparation:

  1. Preheat oven to 450 degrees.
  2. Chop and mince green onions, red bell pepper, chicken, and jalapeno pepper. Keep them separate.
  3. Shred Monterey Jack cheese.

Directions:

  1. In a large frying pan, heat oil over medium heat.
  2. Stir in green onions and red bell pepper. Cook until tender, about 5 minutes.
  3. Add chicken and mix well.
  4. Add corn, black beans, spinach, jalapeno pepper, cumin, chili powder, salt, and red pepper.
  5. Cook and stir for about 5 more minutes until well blended and tender.
  6. Remove from heat and stir in cheese. Mix well until melted.
  7. Spoon 2 tbsp of mixture onto egg roll wrapper. Wrap, sealing edges with water.
  8. Place on greased cookie sheet and spray with cooking spray.
  9. Bake at 450 degrees for 15-20 minutes, turning over after 10 minutes. Cook until golden brown.
  10. Serve with cilantro sauce and enjoy!

Creamy Cilantro Sauce
Ingredients:

  • 8 oz Cream Cheese, Softened
  • 1 TB Sour Cream
  • 7 oz jar of Salsa
  • 1 tsp Black Pepper
  • 1 tsp Celery Salt
  • ½ tsp Cumin
  • 2 tsp Garlic Powder
  • 1 cup Chopped Cilantro
  • 1 TB fresh Lime Juice

Preparation:
Chop Cilantro.
In a medium mixing bowl, combine all ingredients with a mixer.
Blend until smooth and creamy.
Serve with Southwestern Egg Rolls and enjoy!

Sun-Dried Tomato Dip

Ingredients

  • 2 (8 oz) pkgs cream cheese, softened
  • 2 cups mayonnaise
  • 1/4 cup onion, chopped
  • 4 tsp garlic, minced
  • 7 oz jar oil-packed sun-dried tomatoes, drained and chopped
  • 2/3 cup chopped roasted red peppers
  • 2 cups (8 oz) mozzarella cheese, shredded
  • 2 cups (8 oz) Italian cheese blend, shredded
  • 1 cup Parmesan cheese, shredded and divided

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 350 degrees.

  2. In a large bowl, combine the cream cheese, mayonnaise, onion, and garlic until blended.

  3. Stir in tomatoes and red peppers.

  4. Stir in mozzarella cheese, Italian cheese, and 1/2 cup Parmesan cheese.

  5. Pour into greased 9x13 baking dish.

  6. Sprinkle with remaining Parmesan cheese.

  7. Bake, uncovered, for 18-22 minutes or until edges are bubbly and light brown.

  8. Serve with crackers and enjoy!

'No wisdom, no understanding, no counsel can avail against the Lord" Proverbs 21:30 ESV

Appetizers |

Tailgate Sandwiches

Ingredients

  • 1 pkg King Hawaiian hoagie rolls (12 ct)
  • 1 lb shaved Black Forest Ham
  • 12 slices of Gruyere Cheese
  • 1/2 TB Dried Onion Flakes
  • 1 TB Worcestershire Sauce
  • 1/2 Cup Butter, melted
  • 1/4 cup Parmesan Cheese, grated
  • 8 oz Chive and Onion Cream Cheese, softened

Instructions

Preheat oven to 350 degrees.

Cut all rolls in half and put bottom halves in a 9x13 pan.

Place equal amounts of ham on each roll, then top with a slice of Gruyere Cheese.

Spread cream cheese inside each of the roll tops.

Place the roll tops over the bottoms, creating a sandwich.

In a separate bowl, mix together butter, Worcestershire sauce, onion flakes, and Parmesan cheese.

Pour over sandwiches and let sit for at least 20 minutes. (Can be made ahead of time and placed in refrigerator overnight).

Cover sandwiches in foil and place in oven.

Bake for 20 minutes or until warmed all the way through.

Enjoy!!

Appetizers |

Taquitos

Ingredients

  • 1 tablespoon vegetable oil, plus some for brushing on the tortillas

  • 1/2 medium yellow onion, finely chopped

  • 2 teaspoons minced garlic

  • 1 pound lean ground beef

  • 1 cup salsa

  • 2 teaspoons chili powder

  • 1/2 teaspoon salt

  • 1/2 teaspoon black pepper

  • 10 flour tortillas

  • 1 1/2 cups shredded cheddar cheese

  • Sour cream, queso and/or salsa to serve with taquitos

Instructions

  1. Heat the oven to 400 degrees.

  2. In a large skillet, heat 1 tablespoon vegetable oil over medium heat.

  3. Add the chopped onion and garlic; cook for 3 minutes, stirring often. (Be careful not to let the garlic burn)

  4. Add the ground beef, and use a wooden spoon to break it up while it cooks, until it is no longer red, about 5 minutes. Add salt and pepper while cooking.

  5. Stir in 1 cup salsa and 2 teaspoons chili powder.

  6. Cook over medium-high heat, stirring occasionally, until the mixture thickens, about 7-10 minutes.

  7. Top each tortilla with 1/4 cup of beef, spreading not quite to the edges of the tortilla.

  8. Sprinkle about 2 tablespoons shredded cheese on top.

  9. Roll up tortillas and place them seam-side down on a cookie sheet lined with parchment paper.

  10. Brush lightly with vegetable oil.

  11. Bake until tortillas are lightly browned, about 8-12 minutes.

  12. Serve with sour cream, queso, and/or salsa.

Enjoy!

"if you lie down, you will not be afraid; when you lie down, your sleep will be sweet" Proverbs 3:24 ESV

Appetizers |

Veggie Bites

Ingredients

  • 2 cans Crescent Rolls
  • 1 Cup Mayonnaise
  • 8 oz pkg Cream Cheese, softened
  • 1 pkg Ranch Dressing mix
  • 1 cup Broccoli, chopped
  • 1/2 Red Bell Pepper, chopped
  • 2 Carrots, chopped
  • 2 cups Mozzarella Cheese, grated

Instructions

  1. On an ungreased cookie sheet, spread out crescent roll dough into a rectangle, pressing all seams together. Bake at 375 degrees for 12-15 minutes or until golden brown. Cool completely.

  2. In a bowl, combine mayonnaise, cream cheese, and ranch dressing mix. Spread evenly over cooled crust.

  3. In a large bowl, combine chopped broccoli, carrots, red pepper, and mozzarella cheese. Sprinkle on top of cream cheese mixture, pressing down. Refrigerate until ready to serve.

  4. When serving, you can cut them into squares or shapes using a cookie cutter. Enjoy!

Appetizers |

White Pizza Dip

Ingredients

  • 1 pint grape tomatoes, cut in half
  • 1 tbsp olive oil
  • 1/4 tsp salt
  • 1/4 tsp pepper
  • 12 oz (1 1/2 blocks) cream cheese, softened
  • 8 oz mozzarella cheese, grated
  • 8 oz provolone cheese, grated
  • 1/4 cup Parmesan cheese (the Kraft brand that's grated)
  • 4 garlic cloves, minced
  • 1/4 cup fresh basil, chopped (or 2 1/2 tbsp dried basil)
  • 2 tbsp fresh thyme, chopped (or 2 tsp dried thyme)
  • 1/2 tbsp fresh oregano, chopped (or 1/2 tsp dried oregano)

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 400 degrees.

  2. Line a baking sheet with aluminum foil and place tomatoes on top.

  3. Sprinkle with olive oil, salt, and pepper.

  4. Toss by hand to coat.

  5. Roast in oven for 20-25 minutes, until bursting. Set aside.

  6. While tomatoes are roasting, grate mozzarella and provolone cheese.

  7. In a large mixing bowl, beat cream cheese till creamy.

  8. Add grated mozzarella and provolone cheese (leave out about 1/2 cup of each to place on top).

  9. Add Parmesan cheese and mix well.

  10. Stir in fresh herbs, garlic, and roasted tomatoes, mixing well to combine.

  11. Pour into a baking dish.

  12. Sprinkle with remaining mozzarella and provolone cheese.

  13. Bake for 25-30 minutes, or until top is golden and bubbly.

  14. Serve with crackers or chips and enjoy!

Isaiah 43:10-11 NIV

Chai Tea Mix

Ingredients

  • 1 cup nonfat dry milk powder
  • 1 cup powdered non-dairy creamer
  • 1 cup French Vanilla flavored powdered creamer
  • 2 ½ cups sugar
  • ¼ cup cocoa
  • 1 ½ cups unsweetened instant tea
  • 2 tsp ginger
  • 2 tsp cinnamon
  • 1 tsp cloves
  • ½ tsp white pepper
  • ½ tsp cardamom

Instructions

In a large bowl, combine all ingredients. Mix well.

In a blender or food processor, blend 1 cup at a time, until the mixture is the consistency of a fine powder.

Store in an air-tight container.

To serve, stir 2 tablespoons of chai tea mixture into a cup of hot water or milk.

Enjoy!

Chocolate Café Au Lait

Ingredients

  • 1/2 cup powdered non-dairy creamer
  • 1/2 cup butter mints, crushed
  • 1 cup powdered sugar
  • 2 cups powdered milk
  • 2/3 cup instant coffee crystals
  • 3/4 cup Nestle Quik drink mix

Instructions

In a large bowl, combine creamer, butter mints, powdered sugar, powdered milk, coffee crystals, and Nestle Quik. Store in an airtight container.

To make: Dissolve 1/4 cup of mix in a cup of hot water. Enjoy!

Beverages |

Crunch Punch

Ingredients

  • 9 cups hot tap water
  • 3 (3 oz) pkgs jello
  • 4 cups sugar
  • 4 cups water
  • 16 oz bottle ReaLemon Juice
  • 2 (46 oz) cans Pineapple Juice
  • 2-4 bottles Ginger Ale

Instructions

In a large container, dissolve Jell-O in 9 cups of hot tap water. Set aside.

In a large saucepan, bring 4 cups of water and 4 cups of sugar to a rolling boil.

Add to Jell-O and cool slightly.

Add lemon juice and pineapple juice to Jell-O mixture. Cool completely.

Place in 1-gallon hefty ziplock freezer bags and place in freezer.

When ready to use, thaw in microwave for 5 minutes. Place in punch bowl and add ginger ale. To get the crunchy taste, add ginger ale slowly and crunch up punch with a knife or spatula.

Hot Cocoa Mix

Ingredients

  • 1 3/4 cup powdered milk
  • 1/3 cup chocolate drink mix
  • 1/3 cup non-dairy creamer
  • 1 1/8 cup powdered sugar
  • 1/8 cup cocoa powder

Instructions

In a large bowl, combine powdered milk, chocolate drink mix, creamer, powdered sugar, and cocoa.

Store in an airtight container.

To make: Dissolve 2 heaping tablespoons in a cup of hot water or hot milk. (I like to use boiling water and add cream to cool and make it richer in flavor)
Serve with whipped cream, marshmallows and/or chocolate sprinkles on top and enjoy!

This makes a great gift. Just attach a card with the recipe and instructions on how to make it.

"The name of the Lord is a strong tower; the righteous man runs into it and is safe."
Proverbs 18:10 ESV

Ingredients

  • Red Hots

PW's Cherry Limeade

Ingredients

  • 2-liter bottle lemon-lime soda (the cheapest brand works great!)
  • 1 cup fresh squeezed lime juice
  • 1 cup sugar
  • One 10-ounce jar maraschino cherries, with juice
  • Thin lime slices

Instructions

  1. Chill all ingredients before mixing.

  2. When cold, combine the lemon-lime soda, lime juice, sugar, cherries with their juice, and lime slices in a large pitcher and stir.

  3. Refrigerate for 30 minutes.

The perfect summertime drink!

Pumpkin Spice Coffee Syrup

Ingredients

  • 4 cups water
  • 3 cups brown sugar
  • 1/4 cup pumpkin puree
  • 2 tsp pumpkin pie spice

Instructions

In a medium saucepan, combine water and sugar.

Bring to a boil and stir until sugar is dissolved.

Whisk in pumpkin puree and spices.

Drain over a small sieve or cheesecloth.

Pour into bottles or canning jar.

Let cool, seal top, and keep refrigerated for up to 2 weeks.

To enjoy - add 2 tablespoons to your hot drink of choice and enjoy!

Sparkling Strawberry Lemonade

Ingredients

  • Yield: 6-7 cups

For the strawberry syrup:

  • 1 heaping cup chopped strawberries
  • 2 tbsp. sugar
  • 1 tsp. lemon juice
  • 2 tbsp. cold water
  • Pinch of salt

For the lemonade:

  • 1 cup boiling water
  • 2/3 cup sugar
  • 1¼ cups freshly squeezed lemon juice
  • 4½ cups cold sparkling water or seltzer

Instructions

Directions:

To make the strawberry syrup, combine the strawberries, sugar, lemon juice, water, and salt in the bowl of a food processor or blender. Puree until smooth. Pour the mixture through a fine mesh strainer to remove the seeds, pressing down to extract as much liquid as possible.

To make the lemonade,
Boil 1 cup of water, add sugar and stir until dissolved.
Pour into pitcher and add lemon juice and sparkling water.
Stir in the strawberry syrup.

To serve, rub a wedge of lemon or one of the spent lemon halves around the rim of a glass and then dip in sugar. Fill with ice if desired and then lemonade. Garnish with sliced lemon or strawberries. Serve immediately.

Apple Carrot Muffins

Ingredients

  • 1 3/4 cup Raisin Bran Cereal
  • 1 1/4 cup Flour
  • 3/4 cup Sugar
  • 1 1/4 tsp Baking Soda
  • 1 tsp Ground Cinnamon
  • 1/4 tsp Salt
  • 1 egg
  • 3/4 cup Buttermilk*
  • 1/4 cup Canola Oil**
  • 1 Granny Smith Apple, peeled, cored, and finely chopped
  • 1 Carrot, grated (should equal 3/4 cup)
  • 1/4 cup Chopped Walnuts

*If you don't have buttermilk, add 1 tsp white vinegar to 1 cup of milk. Let sit for a few minutes, then measure out what you need.

**Substitute 1/4 cup applesauce for oil to make these healthier.

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 400 degrees.

  2. Peel, core and chop apple. Grate carrots. Place in a bowl and set aside.

  3. In a large mixing bowl, combine cereal, flour, sugar, baking soda, cinnamon, and salt.

  4. In a small mixing bowl, beat the egg, buttermilk, and oil.

  5. Stir the wet ingredients into the dry ingredients until just moistened.

  6. Fold in the apple, carrots, and walnuts.

  7. Place paper liners in muffin cups or coat muffin cups with non-stick cooking spray.

  8. Fill cups 3/4 full.

  9. Bake at 400 degrees for 18-23 minutes or until a toothpick comes out clean.

  10. Cool for 5 minutes before removing from pan to wire rack.

Serve warm and enjoy!

"Lean on, trust in, and be confident in the Lord with all your heart and mind and do not rely on your own insight or understanding."
Proverbs 3:5 (Amplified)

Breads |

Apple Cider Mini Muffins

Ingredients

  • 2 cups all-purpose flour
  • 1 1/2 tsp baking powder
  • 1 1/2 tsp baking soda
  • 1/2 tsp salt
  • 2 tsp cinnamon
  • 1 large egg (room temperature)
  • 2/3 cup brown sugar
  • 1/2 cup apple butter
  • 1 tsp vanilla
  • 1/3 cup honey
  • 1/3 cup apple cider
  • 1/3 cup plain yogurt
  • 2 TB canola or vegetable oil
  • Cooking spray

Cinnamon Sugar Topping:

  • 2 TB melted butter
  • 1/4 cup sugar
  • 1 TB cinnamon

Instructions

Preheat oven to 400 degrees.

Spray a mini muffin pan with cooking spray.

In a large mixing bowl, combine the flour, baking powder, baking soda, salt, and 2 tsp cinnamon.

In another large mixing bowl, whisk together the egg, brown sugar, apple butter, vanilla, honey, apple cider, yogurt, and oil.

Pour the wet ingredients into the dry ingredients and stir until combined. Don't overmix - a few lumps are ok :)

Using a cookie scoop, put the batter into the pan filling each hole 3/4 full.

Bake for 10-12 minutes or until center is done. Cool a couple of minutes.

While muffins are baking, combine the 1/4 cup sugar with the 1 TB cinnamon.

Brush the top of the still warm muffin with the melted butter then roll in the cinnamon sugar mixture to coat.

Serve warm or room temperature.

You can dust with powdered sugar if you prefer.

Enjoy!!

"Lead me in your truth and teach me, for you are the God of my salvation; for you I wait all the day long" Psalm 25:5 ESV

Breads |

Apple Cinnamon Scones

Ingredients

  • 2 3/4 cups all-purpose flour
  • 1/3 cup granulated sugar
  • 3/4 teaspoon salt
  • 1 tablespoon baking powder
  • 1 teaspoon ground cinnamon
  • 1/2 cup (8 tablespoons) cold butter
  • 1 chopped fresh apple, leave the skin on, if you like - I prefer no skin
  • 3/4 cup cinnamon chips
  • 2 large eggs
  • 1 teaspoon vanilla extract
  • 1/2 cup unsweetened applesauce

Topping

  • 3 tablespoons coarse white sparkling sugar
  • 1/2 teaspoon ground cinnamon
  • Milk to brush on top of scones

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 425 degrees.
    In a large mixing bowl, whisk together the flour, sugar, salt, baking powder, and spice.

  2. Cut the butter into cubes. Using a pastry cutter, work in the butter just until the mixture is unevenly crumbly; it's OK for some larger chunks of butter to remain unincorporated.

  3. Stir in the chopped apple and cinnamon chips.

  4. In a separate mixing bowl, whisk together the eggs, vanilla, and applesauce.

  5. Add the liquid ingredients to the dry ingredients and stir until all is moistened and holds together.

  6. Gather dough together and place on parchment paper dusted with flour. Gently pat dough into a rectangle.

  7. To make the topping: Stir together the coarse sugar and cinnamon. Brush each circle with milk, and sprinkle with the topping.

  8. Using a knife or cookie cutter, cut dough into shapes and place on a cookie sheet lined with parchment paper.

  9. Brush each shape with milk and sprinkle with sugar topping.

  10. Place scones in the oven and bake for 18-22 minutes or until golden brown.

  11. Remove the scones from the oven, and cool briefly on the pan. Serve warm. When they're completely cool, wrap in plastic and store at room temperature for up to several days.

  12. Scones can be made in advance and refrigerated before baking.

Yield: 12 scones.

"The Lord is my light and my salvation: whom shall I fear? The Lord is the stronghold of my life; of whom shall I be afraid? Psalm 27:1 ESV

Breads |

Basic Bread Dough

Ingredients

  • 1 cup milk

  • 1 1/2 tsp salt

  • 1/2 cup sugar

  • 3/4 cup butter

  • 1 pkg rapid-rise yeast

  • 1/4 cup HOT tap water

  • 1 tsp sugar

  • 3 eggs

  • 4 cups all-purpose flour

  • 1 stick of butter

For cinnamon rolls:

  • 1 cup brown sugar
  • 2 cups sugar
  • 3 tbsp cinnamon

Instructions

Rinse medium saucepan with water. On medium heat, cook 1 cup of milk until a skim forms on top. This is called scalding. DO NOT bring milk to a boil. Remove from heat once skin is formed. Add 1 1/2 tsp salt, 1/2 cup sugar, and 3/4 cup butter. Stir well. Set aside.

Mix with a fork 1/4 cup HOT tap water, 1 tsp sugar, and 1 pkg of yeast in a medium-sized glass. Set aside until yeast bubbles up.

Beat 3 eggs in a small bowl.

Once the milk mixture has cooled and the butter is completely melted, in a large mixing bowl, add risen yeast. Mix well. Add beaten eggs. Mix well. Add 2 cups flour, mixing well. Add 1 more cup of flour. Mix well. Add the final cup of flour. Mix well. Cover bowl with saran wrap and let rise. The dough will double in size. This takes from 4 to 6 hours.

Once the dough has risen, remove saran wrap and stir dough about 3 times. Replace saran wrap and refrigerate overnight (at least 6 hours).

Remove dough from refrigerator. Melt 1 stick of butter in the microwave. Divide dough into 3 parts. On a floured surface, roll out dough like a pie. With a pastry brush, spread melted butter over dough. Cut into 8 pieces with a pizza cutter.

Roll dough into crescent shape and place on a greased cookie sheet. Cover with a tea towel and let rise till doubled in size (about 2-3 hours).

Bake at 350 degrees for 12-15 minutes. Remove from oven when golden brown. Brush with melted butter. Serve immediately or let cool and place in a Ziplock freezer bag. Rolls can be frozen for up to 1 month. To use after freezing – let rolls thaw at room temperature in the bag. Heat on cookie sheet at 300 degrees for 4-8 minutes.

This is usually a 2-day process. You can make the bread the night before the day you want it. Let it rise that evening and refrigerate it overnight. It can stay in the refrigerator for up to 18 hours. You can make it that next morning or wait until about 3 hours before dinner.

Tips...

This bread freezes great. When finished baking, let cool completely and place in Ziplock freezer bags. Freeze for up to 1 month. To reheat, remove from freezer and let thaw at room temperature. Place thawed rolls on a cookie sheet and reheat at 300 degrees for 5-10 minutes.

But Jesus told him, "No! The scriptures say, 'People do not live by bread alone, but by every word that comes from the mouth of God." Matthew 4:4 NLT

Beer Bread

Ingredients

  • 1 can Beer (not light - room temperature)
  • 3 Cups Self-rising Flour
  • 3 TB Sugar
  • 3 TB melted Butter

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 375 degrees.
  2. Spray a loaf pan with Pam.
  3. In a large mixing bowl, combine self-rising flour and sugar. Stir in beer; mix well.
  4. Pour into greased loaf pan. Pour 3 TB melted butter on top.
  5. Cover with saran wrap and let rise 20 minutes.
  6. Bake at 375 for 45-60 minutes or until golden brown.
  7. Serve and enjoy! (It is easier to cut after it is cool)

Ingredients

  • 1 box Golden Butter Cake Mix
  • 1 small box French Vanilla pudding
  • 1 (8 oz) ctn Cream Cheese, softened
  • 2 cups Frozen Blueberries
  • 3/4 cup Oil
  • 1 tsp Vanilla
  • 4 Eggs

Instructions

Fold in frozen blueberries (if using fresh blueberries,

Using mini or regular muffin tins, spray with vegetable oil spray and fill tins 1/2 full with batter.

Carrot Cake Muffins

Ingredients

  • 2 cups flour
  • 3/4 cup sugar
  • 2 tsp baking soda
  • 1 tsp cinnamon
  • 1 tsp salt
  • 8 oz can crushed pineapple, drained
  • 1/4 cup vegetable oil
  • 2 large eggs
  • 2 egg whites
  • 1 tbsp vanilla
  • 3 cups grated carrots
  • 1/2 cup chopped pecans or walnuts
  • 1/2 cup golden raisins

If glaze is desired: combine powdered sugar with milk till you get the consistency you desire. Drizzle over warm muffins.

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 350 degrees.

  2. Combine flour, sugar, baking soda, cinnamon, and salt in a large bowl.

  3. Make a well in the center and whisk in pineapple, vegetable oil, eggs, egg whites, and vanilla. Mix until dry ingredients are just moist.

  4. Fold in carrots, pecans or walnuts, and raisins.

  5. Fill 18 muffin tins with paper liners or spray with nonstick baking spray.

  6. Spoon batter into baking cups, filling about 2/3 full.

  7. Bake for 22-25 minutes or until a wooden toothpick inserted in center comes out clean.

  8. Cool in pan for 10 minutes.

  9. Serve warm and enjoy!

Cheesy Biscuits

Ingredients

  • 4 cups Bisquick
  • 1 1/3 cups Milk
  • 1 cup grated cheese
  • 1/2 cup melted butter
  • 1 TB parsley flakes
  • 1 tsp garlic powder
  • 1 tsp Italian seasonings

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 450 degrees.

  2. Stir together Bisquick, milk, and cheese until a soft dough forms.

  3. Drop by spoonfuls onto a lightly greased baking sheet.

  4. Bake for 8–10 minutes.

  5. In a small bowl, mix butter, parsley, Italian seasoning, and garlic powder.

  6. Brush over warm biscuits. Enjoy!

Cinnamon Bread

Ingredients

  • Batter:

    • 1 cup butter, softened
    • 2 cups sugar
    • 2 eggs
    • 2 cups buttermilk or 2 cups milk plus 2 tablespoons vinegar or lemon juice
    • 4 cups flour
    • 2 teaspoons baking soda

  • Cinnamon/sugar mixture:

    • 2/3 cup sugar
    • 2 teaspoons cinnamon

Instructions

  1. Cream together butter, 2 cups of sugar, and eggs. Add milk, flour, and baking soda.

  2. Put 1/2 of batter (or a little less) into greased loaf pans (1/4 in each pan).

  3. Mix in a separate bowl the 2/3 cup sugar and cinnamon. Sprinkle 3/4 of the cinnamon mixture on top of the 1/2 batter in each pan.

  4. Add remaining batter to pans; sprinkle with the last of the cinnamon topping. Swirl with a knife.

  5. Bake at 350 degrees for 45-50 minutes or until toothpick tester comes clean.

  6. Cool in pan for 20 minutes before removing from pan.

Cinnamon Rolls (using our basic bread dough recipe)

Ingredients

Refer to 'Basic Bread Dough' Recipe for ingredients and instructions on making dough.

Instructions

To make dough into cinnamon rolls:

  1. Remove dough from refrigerator. Melt 1 stick of butter. Mix together in medium size bowl 1 cup brown sugar, 2 cups sugar, and 3 tablespoons of cinnamon. Set aside.

  2. Divide dough into 3 parts. Roll dough into a rectangle shape on a floured surface. Brush melted butter onto dough. Sprinkle 1 cup of sugar/cinnamon mixture on top of dough. Roll dough into a tube shape.

  3. Slice dough into 1-inch pieces and place into greased 9x13 pan. Press down. Brush melted butter on top of rolls.

  4. Cover with towel and let rise till doubled in size.

  5. Bake at 350 degrees for 15-20 minutes or until golden brown. Remove from oven and turn out rolls onto tray.

Breads |

Cranberry Scones

Ingredients

  • 4 cups plus 1 TB flour

  • 2 TB baking powder

  • 2 TB sugar

  • 2 tsp salt

  • 3 sticks butter, cold and diced

  • 4 eggs, lightly beaten

  • 1 cup whipping cream

  • 1 pkg craisins

  • 1 egg with 2 TB milk or water for egg wash

Instructions

  1. Using an electric mixer with a paddle, combine 4 cups flour, 2 TB sugar, baking powder, and salt. Blend in cold butter at the lowest speed until mixture is crumbly.
  2. In a small bowl, combine eggs and cream. Add to flour mixture. Combine until just blended.
  3. In another small bowl, combine dried cranberries with 1 TB flour. Add to dough and mix quickly.
  4. Dump dough onto floured surface and roll out to 1” thick. Cut into squares; then cut in half to make triangles. Place on a cookie sheet lined with parchment paper. Refrigerate overnight.
  5. Preheat oven to 400 degrees. Remove scones from refrigerator; brush with egg wash and sprinkle with sugar. Bake for 20-25 minutes or until outside is crisp.

Cream Cheese Monkey Bread

Ingredients

  • 2/3 cup dark brown sugar
  • 2/3 cup sugar
  • 1 tablespoon cinnamon
  • 2 (12 oz) tubes (10 count) refrigerated flaky biscuits
  • 8 oz package cream cheese, cold, cut into 20 cubes
  • 3/4 cup butter, melted (1 1/2 sticks)

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 350 degrees.
  2. Spray a bundt pan with cooking spray; set aside.
  3. In a medium mixing bowl, combine sugars and cinnamon.
  4. In another small bowl, melt butter in microwave.
  5. Open 1 tube of biscuits and separate biscuits, laying on flat surface.
  6. Place 1 cube of cream cheese in the center of each biscuit.
  7. Wrap edges of dough up and around the cream cheese so that only the top of the cream cheese is visible.
  8. Place dough wrapped cheese cube, cheese side up in bottom of bundt pan.
  9. Sprinkle half of the sugar mixture on top and pour half of the melted butter on top.
  10. Open the 2nd tube of biscuits and separate biscuits again - placing cream cheese cube in center and wrapping edges of dough up and around the cream cheese.
  11. This time place the dough wrapped cream cheese cube, cheese side down, in the bundt pan.
  12. Top with remaining sugar mixture and butter.
  13. Place in preheated oven and bake for 40 minutes.
  14. Remove from oven, invert onto a platter with edges (there is lots of gooeyness)
  15. Serve and enjoy!

"The grass withers, the flowers fade, but the word of our God will stand forever."
Isaiah 40:8 ESV

Crescent Bread (Family Favorite and the easiest!)

Ingredients

  • 1 Stick Butter
  • 3 Cans Crescent Rolls

Optional:

  • 2 cups Cinnamon Sugar

  • Garlic Salt

Instructions

Preheat oven to 350 degrees.

Place a stick of butter in bundt pan and place in oven to melt.

Unwrap the crescent rolls, leaving them rolled up.

Remove bundt pan from oven.

Place crescent rolls in pan, layer the ends if necessary.

Return to oven and bake for 45 minutes.

Remove and enjoy!

To make cinnamon bread:

Unroll crescent rolls and sprinkle with 1/2 cup of sugar on each roll. Roll back up. Sprinkle bundt pan with cinnamon sugar then place the crescent rolls in the pan. Sprinkle the remaining cinnamon sugar on top of the crescent rolls. Bake for 45 minutes.

To make garlic bread:

We like a lot of garlic at my house! :)

Unroll crescent rolls and sprinkle with garlic salt. Roll back up. Sprinkle bundt pan with garlic salt then place the crescent rolls in the pan. Sprinkle a little more garlic salt on top. Bake for 45 minutes.

Crispy Garlic Bread

Ingredients

  • 1 loaf French bread
  • 2 sticks butter, softened to room temperature
  • 2 tablespoons garlic salt

Instructions

Preheat oven to 350 degrees.

In a small mixing bowl, combine softened butter and garlic salt.

Split French bread in half and place on a foil-lined baking sheet.

Generously spread the butter mixture all over the cut sides of the bread.

(The pioneer woman uses all of it - I usually have a little left over)

Place in preheated oven for 15 minutes till melted and crispy.

Breads |

Double Chocolate Muffins

Ingredients

  • 2 oz unsweetened chocolate
  • 1 3/4 cup all-purpose flour
  • 1 tsp baking soda
  • 1/4 tsp salt
  • 1 cup buttermilk
  • 1 tsp vanilla
  • 1 stick butter, softened
  • 1 cup brown sugar
  • 1 egg
  • 1 cup semisweet chocolate chips

Instructions

Melt and let cool the unsweetened chocolate.

In a mixing bowl, whisk together the flour, soda, and salt.

In another mixing bowl, combine buttermilk and vanilla.

Preheat oven to 350 degrees.

Line a 12-cup muffin pan with paper liners or grease and flour the muffin pan.

In a large mixing bowl (preferably a stand mixer bowl), beat butter until creamy.

Gradually add and beat on high speed, brown sugar. It will be light in color and texture - about 4-5 minutes.

Beat in egg.

Beat in chocolate until just blended.

Add the flour mixture, alternating with the buttermilk - start and end with the flour mixture.

Beat on low speed until smooth; scrape sides of the bowl as necessary.

Stir in chocolate chips.

Scoop the batter into the muffin cups and place in the oven.

Bake for 25-30 minutes or until a toothpick inserted in center comes out clean.

Let cool for 2-3 minutes before removing to cool completely on wire rack.

Serve and enjoy!

  • 1/2 cup butter, softened

  3. Spread the butter on the inside of the bread.

  4. Wrap the bread in tin foil.

  7. Sprinkle with cheese.

  10. Remove, sprinkle with parsley, cut into slices and serve.

Harvest Loaf Cake

Ingredients

Double this recipe to make a bundt cake

  • 1 ¾ cup flour
  • 1 tsp baking soda
  • 1 tsp cinnamon
  • ½ tsp salt
  • ½ tsp nutmeg
  • ¼ tsp ginger
  • ¼ tsp ground cloves
  • ½ cup butter, room temperature
  • 1 cup sugar
  • 2 eggs
  • ¾ cup canned pumpkin
  • ¾ cup chocolate chips
  • ¾ cup walnuts, chopped, divided

Glaze:

  • ½ cup powdered sugar
  • 1/8 tsp nutmeg
  • 1/8 tsp cinnamon
  • Blend in 1 tsp at a time of hot tap water till right consistency

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 350 degrees.
  2. Grease and flour ( I use a baking spray) a 9x15 baking dish or 3 mini loaf pans.
  3. Combine dry ingredients in a medium mixing bowl. Set aside.
  4. In a large mixing bowl, cream butter, add sugar.
  5. Blend in eggs, beat well.
  6. At low speed, add dry ingredients alternating with pumpkin.
  7. Stir in chocolate chips and ½ cup walnuts.
  8. Pour into pan. Sprinkle with remaining ¼ cup walnuts.
  9. Bake at 350 degrees for 65–75 minutes. (If baking in mini loaf pans, check after 30 minutes)
  10. Cool.
  11. Drizzle with glaze.
  12. Enjoy!

Homemade Flour Tortillas

Ingredients

  • 3 Cups All-Purpose Flour
  • 2 tsp. Baking Powder
  • 1 tsp. Salt
  • 5 TB Crisco shortening
  • 1 1/4 Cups Hot Water

Instructions

To make the dough, combine the flour, crisco shortening, baking powder, and salt in the bowl of a stand mixer fitted with a dough hook.

Pour the hot water over the top of the ingredients and turn the mixer to medium speed.

Mix until fully combined and the dough begins to pull away from the bowl

Cover the dough with plastic wrap and let sit for 1 to 2 hours at room temperature.

Pull off pieces of dough to form small balls, around the size of golf balls.

Place them on wax paper and let them rest for 30 minutes.

While dough is resting, heat a large skillet or griddle to medium-high heat.(I prefer a cast iron pan)

Dust rolling pin with a little flour and roll balls out into flat circles.(You can also place the dough balls between 2 sheets of wax paper and roll out into flat circles)

Lay tortilla onto the pan. Let cook for about 30 seconds, check to see if browning, then flip them over to cook on the other side.

Serve warm and enjoy!

"But the wisdom from above is first pure, then peaceable, gentle, open to reason, full of mercy and good fruits, impartial and sincere. And a harvest of righteousness is sown in peace by those who make peace." James 3:17-18 ESV

Lemon Blackberry Muffins

Ingredients

  • Sugar Topping:

    • 1/3 cup sugar
    • 1 1/2 tsp zest from 1 lemon

  • Muffins:

    • 2 cups flour
    • 1/2 tsp baking powder
    • 1/2 tsp baking soda
    • 1/2 tsp salt
    • Zest of 2 lemons
    • 1/2 cup butter, softened
    • 1 cup ricotta cheese
    • 1 large egg, room temperature
    • 1 tbsp fresh lemon juice
    • 1 tsp vanilla
    • 1 cup blackberries, fresh or frozen (If using fresh, rinse and dry. If using frozen, keep frozen do not thaw)

Instructions

For the Topping:
In a small bowl, combine 1/3 cup sugar and zest from 1 lemon. Rub together with your fingers, and set aside.

Preheat oven to 350 degrees.

Line 12 muffin cups with paper muffin liners.

In a medium mixing bowl, combine the flour, baking powder, baking soda, and salt. Set aside.

In a large mixing bowl using a stand mixer with the paddle attachment, combine the butter, 1 cup of sugar, and zest from 2 lemons.

Beat together until light and fluffy, about 2 minutes.

Add the ricotta cheese and beat until smooth.

Add the egg, lemon juice, and vanilla, and mix well.

Using a spatula, fold in the flour mixture until just combined. (Careful not to overmix)

Fill each muffin cup halfway.

Add 3 blackberries to each cup.

Top the blackberries with remaining batter, making sure the berries are covered.

Sprinkle the lemon sugar topping evenly over the muffins.

Bake for 20-25 minutes or until tops are slightly golden.

Cool for 5 minutes before serving.

ENJOY!

"And as you wish that others would do to you, do so to them" Luke 6:31 ESV

Breads |

Maple Oatmeal Scones

Ingredients

FOR THE SCONES:

  • 3 1/2 cups all-purpose flour
  • 1 cup whole-wheat flour
  • 1 cup quick-cooking oats, plus additional for sprinkling
  • 2 tablespoons baking powder
  • 4 tablespoons granulated sugar
  • 2 teaspoons salt
  • 1 pound cold unsalted butter, diced
  • 1/2 cup cold buttermilk
  • 1/2 cup pure maple syrup
  • 4 extra-large eggs, lightly beaten

FOR THE EGG WASH:

  • 1 egg beaten with 1 tablespoon milk or water, for egg wash

FOR THE GLAZE:

  • 1 1/4 cups confectioners' sugar
  • 1/2 cup pure maple syrup

FOR THE CREAM CHEESE SPREAD:

  • 8 ounces cream cheese at room temperature
  • 3 tablespoons brown sugar
  • 3 tablespoons maple syrup

Instructions

  1. Preheat the oven to 400 degrees F.

  2. In the bowl of an electric mixer fitted with a paddle attachment, combine the flours, oats, baking powder, sugar, and salt.

  3. Blend the cold butter in at the lowest speed and mix until the butter is in pea-size pieces.

  4. Combine the buttermilk, maple syrup, and eggs and add quickly to the flour-and-butter mixture. Mix until just blended. The dough may be sticky.

  5. Dump the dough out onto a well-floured surface and be sure it is combined. Flour your hands and a rolling pin and roll the dough 3/4 to 1 inch thick. You should see lumps of butter in the dough.

  6. Cut into 3-inch rounds with a plain or fluted cutter and place on a baking sheet lined with parchment paper.

  7. Brush the tops with egg wash. Bake for 20 to 25 minutes, until the tops are crisp and the insides are done.

TO MAKE THE GLAZE:

  • Combine the confectioners' sugar and maple syrup. When the scones are done, cool for 5 minutes and drizzle each scone with 1 tablespoon of the glaze. Sprinkle some uncooked oats on the top for garnish. The warmer the scones are when you glaze them, the thinner the glaze will be.

FOR THE CREAM CHEESE SPREAD:

  • Mix all the ingredients together until well combined. Chill until ready to serve.

Mexican Cornbread

Ingredients

  • 1 cup butter, melted
  • 1 cup sugar
  • 4 eggs
  • 15 oz can cream corn
  • 4 oz can green chilies, chopped
  • 1 cup Pepper Jack cheese, shredded
  • 1 cup Cheddar cheese, shredded
  • 1 cup all-purpose flour
  • 1 cup yellow cornmeal
  • 4 tsp baking powder
  • 1/4 tsp salt

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 300 degrees.
  2. Spray a 9x13 baking sheet with Pam.
  3. In a large bowl using an electric mixer, beat butter and sugar.
  4. Add eggs one at a time, blending well after each egg.
  5. Blend in cream corn, chilies, and cheeses.
  6. In a separate bowl, stir together flour, cornmeal, baking powder, and salt.
  7. Add the flour mixture to the corn mixture and stir with a spoon or spatula until smooth.
  8. Pour batter into prepared pan.
  9. Bake in preheated oven for 1 hour or until a toothpick inserted in the center comes out clean.
  10. Serve with *Mexican Chicken Corn Chowder (recipe on website)
    Enjoy!

Mix-In Muffins

Ingredients

  • 1 1/2 cups flour
  • 1/3 cup sugar
  • 1 1/2 tsp baking powder
  • 1/4 tsp baking soda
  • 1/4 tsp salt
  • 1 stick butter
  • 1 cup sour cream
  • 1 large egg
  • 1 tsp vanilla

Instructions

MIX-IN INGREDIENTS

Preheat oven to 400 degrees and grease muffin cups.

In a bowl, mix flour, sugar, baking powder, baking soda, and salt. In a small bowl, melt butter. With a whisk, add sour cream, egg, and vanilla. Mix well.

Stir in mix-in ingredients:

  • 1 chopped banana and 3/4 cup chocolate chips
  • OR 1 cup almonds, 3/4 cup craisins, and 1/4 tsp almond extract
  • OR 1 cup crushed and drained pineapple and 1 cup coconut
  • OR 1 cup chopped dried apricots and 1 tbsp poppy seeds
  • OR 1 cup fresh or frozen blueberries
  • OR 1 cup brown sugar, 1 cup chopped nuts, and 1 1/2 tsp cinnamon (Mix brown sugar, nuts, and cinnamon in a separate small bowl. Fill muffin tin 1/2 full and sprinkle a layer of brown sugar mixture. Top with batter and another layer of brown sugar mixture.)

Stir butter mixture into flour mixture until just combined. Drop batter into greased muffin tins. Bake until golden and a tester comes out clean, 15-20 minutes.

Makes 12 muffins.

Breads |

Morning Glory Muffins

Ingredients

  • 2 cups all-purpose flour
  • 2 tsp baking soda
  • 2 tsp cinnamon
  • 1/2 tsp ground ginger
  • 1/2 tsp salt
  • 1/2 cup chopped pecans or walnuts (nuts are optional)
  • 3 large eggs, room temp
  • 1/2 cup brown sugar
  • 1/4 cup honey
  • 1/3 cup vegetable oil (you can use melted coconut oil)
  • 1/3 cup applesauce
  • zest of 1 orange
  • 1/4 cup orange juice
  • 1 tsp vanilla extract
  • 1/2 cup raisins, soaked in warm water for 10 minutes
  • 2 cups carrots, grated (about 4 large carrots)
  • 1 apple, peeled and grated

Additional options to add or try:

  1. Add 1/3 cup coconut
  2. Substitute zucchini for carrots
  3. Substitute crushed pineapple (2/3 - 3/4 cup) for apple
  4. Substitute dried cranberries for raisins

Instructions

Preheat oven to 425 degrees.
Spray a 12-count muffin pan with nonstick spray or use greased/sprayed cupcake liners. This recipe should make 16 muffins so you'll need a second muffin pan.
In a large bowl, whisk the flour, baking soda, cinnamon, ginger, salt, and nuts until combined. Set aside.
In a medium bowl, whisk the eggs, brown sugar, honey, oil, applesauce, orange zest, orange juice, and vanilla together until combined.
Pour the wet ingredients into the dry ingredients; stir a few times, then add the raisins, carrots, and apple.
Fold everything together gently just until combined and no flour pockets remain.
Fill liners to the top.
Bake for 5 minutes at 425, then, keeping the muffins in the oven, reduce the oven temperature to 350.
Bake for an additional 18 minutes or until a toothpick inserted in the center comes out clean.
Allow the muffins to cool for 10 minutes in the muffin tins before transferring them to a cooling rack.
Muffins stay fresh for up to 5 days. They may be frozen for up to 2 months, thaw overnight in the refrigerator.

This recipe is adapted from Sally's Baking Addiction.
Here's her tip about the baking temperature:
The hot burst of air will spring up the top of the muffin quickly, then the inside of the muffin can bake for the remainder of the time. This helps the muffins rise nice and tall.

Parmesan Pull-Apart Rolls

Ingredients

  • 1 can Refrigerator Biscuits (10ct)
  • 4 TB Butter
  • 1 tsp Italian Seasoning
  • 1 tsp Garlic Powder
  • ½ tsp Basil
  • 1 tsp Celery Seed or Salt
  • ½ tsp Dill
  • 2 tsp Sesame Seeds
  • 1 cup grated Parmesan Cheese

Instructions

Preheat oven to 400 degrees.
Place butter in round cake pan and place pan in oven to melt.
In a small mixing bowl, combine Italian seasoning, garlic powder, basil, celery seed, dill, and sesame seeds.
Once butter is melted, sprinkle combined seasonings into pan evenly.
Cut biscuits in half and roll into balls
Roll balls in the grated Parmesan cheese.
Place balls in pan, leaving space between.
Bake for 7-10 minutes. Enjoy!

Breads |

Pizza Crust

Ingredients

  • 1 (.25 oz) pkg Active Yeast
  • 1 tsp Sugar
  • 1 cup Warm Water
  • 2 1/2 cups Bread Flour
  • 2 tbsp Olive Oil
  • 1 tsp Salt

Instructions

Preheat oven to 450 degrees. (If you are using a bread stone, place it in the oven first and then turn on the oven.)

In a medium bowl, pour water and then dissolve sugar and yeast. Whisk well, let stand until creamy, about 10 minutes.

Stir in flour, salt, and oil. Beat until smooth. Let rest for 5 minutes.

Turn dough out onto a lightly floured surface and pat or roll into a round.

Transfer crust to a pizza peel lightly dusted with cornmeal (that will make it easier to transfer to a pizza stone) or a lightly greased pizza pan

Spread with desired topping and bake for 15-20 minutes or until golden brown.

Let pizza cool about 5 minutes before serving.

Enjoy!

Pumpkin Bread

Ingredients

  • 1/2 cup butter, softened
  • 1/2 cup shortening
  • 3 cups sugar
  • 3 eggs
  • 29 oz can solid-pack pumpkin
  • 3 cups flour
  • 1 tsp salt
  • 1 tsp baking soda
  • 1 tsp baking powder
  • 1/4 tsp ginger
  • 1/2 tsp nutmeg
  • 1 tsp cinnamon
  • 1 tsp ground cloves

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 350 degrees.
  2. Grease and flour 2 loaf pans or 6 mini loaf pans.
  3. In a large mixing bowl, cream butter, shortening, and sugar.
  4. Beat in eggs, one at a time.
  5. Add pumpkin, flour, salt, baking soda, baking powder, ginger, cinnamon, cloves, and nutmeg.
  6. Pour into pans and bake for 1 hour for loaf pans or 30 minutes for mini-loaf pans.
  7. Let cool for 15 minutes before removing from pans.
  8. Enjoy!

Breads |

Pumpkin Cinnamon Streusel Muffins

Ingredients

For the Muffins:

  • 1 3/4 cups all-purpose flour
  • 1 teaspoon baking soda
  • 1/2 teaspoon salt
  • 1/2 tablespoon cinnamon
  • 1 1/2 teaspoons nutmeg
  • 1/4 teaspoon ground ginger
  • 1/2 teaspoon allspice
  • 1 1/2 cups sugar
  • 1/2 cup vegetable oil
  • 1/3 cup water
  • 1 cup canned pumpkin puree
  • 2 large eggs
  • 1 teaspoon vanilla extract

For the Brown Sugar Cinnamon Filling:

  • 1 cup light brown sugar
  • 1/2 teaspoon cinnamon

For the Streusel Topping:

  • 1 1/4 cup old-fashioned oats
  • 1/2 cup chopped pecans
  • 1 tablespoon all-purpose flour
  • 1/3 cup light brown sugar
  • 1 teaspoon cinnamon
  • Pinch of nutmeg
  • 6 tablespoons cold butter, cut into chunks

Instructions

  1. Preheat the oven to 350 degrees.
  2. Line 24 muffin tins with paper liners or spray with cooking spray; set aside.

To make the brown sugar cinnamon filling:

  1. In a small bowl, combine the brown sugar and cinnamon; set aside.

To make the muffins:

  1. In a medium bowl, whisk together the flour, baking soda, salt, cinnamon, nutmeg, ginger, and allspice; set aside.

  2. In a large bowl, whisk together sugar, oil, water, pumpkin, eggs, and vanilla extract. Whisk until smooth and combined.

  3. Add the flour mixture, stirring gently, until the ingredients are just combined. Don't overmix.

  4. Fill each muffin cup halfway with batter.

  5. Sprinkle the brown sugar cinnamon filling over each muffin cup.

  6. Top the muffins with remaining batter so the filling is covered.

For the Streusel topping:

  1. In a small bowl mix together the oats, pecans, flour, brown sugar, cinnamon, and nutmeg.

  2. Cut in the butter with a pastry cutter (or you can use your hands) until the mixture is crumbly.

  3. Top each muffin with streusel topping.

  4. Bake until muffin tops are just firm and a toothpick inserted into the center comes out clean, about 18 to 22 minutes.

  5. Remove pans from oven and cool on a wire rack.

ADAPTED FROM SWEET PEA'S KITCHEN

"Let us hold unswervingly to the HOPE we profess, for HE who promised is FAITHFUL"
Hebrews 10:23

Cinnamon Sugar Topping:

Breads |

Pumpkin Sugar and Spice Muffins

Ingredients

  • 1 3/4 cups all-purpose flour
  • 2 teaspoons baking powder
  • 1/2 teaspoon salt
  • 1/2 teaspoon cinnamon
  • 1/2 teaspoon nutmeg
  • 1/2 teaspoon allspice
  • 1/8 teaspoon ground cloves
  • 1/3 cup vegetable oil
  • 1/2 cup brown sugar
  • 1 egg
  • 1 teaspoon vanilla extract
  • 3/4 cup canned plain pumpkin (not pumpkin pie filling)
  • 1/2 cup low-fat milk

For Coating:

  • 1 stick of unsalted butter, melted
  • 2/3 cup granulated sugar
  • 2 tablespoons cinnamon

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 350°F. Spray each cup in a 24-cup mini muffin tin with baking spray or butter generously.

  2. In a medium bowl, whisk together flour, baking powder, salt, and spices (through cloves). In a separate, large bowl, whisk together oil, brown sugar, egg, vanilla, pumpkin, and milk until smooth. Add dry ingredients to wet ingredients and mix until just combined – do not over mix!

  3. Divide batter evenly among muffin cups. (Using a 2-tablespoon scoop will ensure uniform size.) Bake at 350°F for 10-12 minutes or until a toothpick comes out clean.

  4. While the muffins bake, melt butter in one bowl and combine granulated sugar and cinnamon in another bowl. Remove muffins from oven and cool for 2 minutes, or until just cool enough to handle. Dip each muffin in melted butter, then roll in cinnamon sugar to coat.

Tip: These are best dipped and coated the same day you are going to eat them, otherwise the coating tends to get soggy.

Pumpkin and Chocolate Chip Cheesecake Muffins

Ingredients

Filling Ingredients:

  • 8 oz pkg. Cream Cheese, softened
  • 1/2 cup Sugar
  • 2 TB all-purpose Flour
  • 1 egg
  • 1/4 cup Sour Cream
  • 1 tsp. Vanilla

Muffin Ingredients:

  • 1 cup Pumpkin
  • 2/3 cup Brown Sugar
  • 1 cup Milk
  • 1/2 cup Vegetable Oil
  • 2 eggs
  • 3 cups all-purpose Flour
  • 1/2 cup Sugar
  • 5 tsp. Baking Powder (yes! five!)
  • 2 tsp. Cinnamon
  • 1 tsp. Salt
  • 1/2 tsp. ground Cloves
  • 1/2 cup chopped Walnuts
  • 1/2 cup Chocolate Chips

Topping:

  • 1/2 cup Brown Sugar
  • 1/2 cup Flour
  • 4 TB Melted Butter

Instructions

  1. In medium mixing bowl, combine all filling ingredients. Place in refrigerator while preparing muffins.

  2. Preheat oven to 400 degrees.

  3. Spray 24 muffin tins with Pam or use liners.

  4. In a large mixing bowl, combine pumpkin, brown sugar, milk, oil, and eggs; mix well.

  5. In medium mixing bowl, combine flour, sugar, baking powder, cinnamon, salt, and cloves. Sift into pumpkin mixture.

  6. Hand stir until flour is moistened.

  7. Fold in walnuts and chocolate chips.

  8. Fill muffin cups 2/3 way full.

  9. Fill a zip-lock baggie with the cream cheese mixture. Cut off tip and push the tip into the muffin batter.

  10. Gently squeeze the cream cheese mixture into each muffin.

  11. Combine topping and crumble over muffins.

  12. Bake for 20 – 22 minutes. Remove; let cool; Enjoy!

Quick Pumpkin Bread Mix - Gift Ja

Ingredients

Ingredients for Jar:

  • 2 cups all-purpose flour
  • 1 cup packed light brown sugar
  • 1/2 cup raisins
  • 1/2 cup chopped pecans or walnuts
  • 1 1/2 tsp pumpkin pie spice
  • 1 tsp baking soda
  • 1 tsp baking powder
  • 1/2 tsp salt

Ingredients for Quick Pumpkin Bread:

  • 1/3 cup butter, softened
  • 2 eggs
  • 1 cup canned pumpkin
  • 1 jar Quick Pumpkin Bread Mix

Instructions

Instructions for Layering Jar:

  1. Layer ingredients attractively in any order in a 1-quart food storage jar with a tight-fitting lid.
  2. Pack ingredients down slightly before adding another layer.
  3. Cover the top of the jar with fabric; attach a gift tag with the recipe instructions.

Instructions for Recipe:

  1. Preheat oven to 350 degrees.
  2. Spray a loaf pan with nonstick cooking spray.
  3. Beat butter in a large bowl with an electric mixer until creamy.
  4. Add eggs, one at a time, beating well after each addition.
  5. Beat in pumpkin until well blended.
  6. Add the contents of the jar; stir until just blended.
  7. Pour into prepared pan.
  8. Bake for 50-55 minutes or until a toothpick inserted into the center comes out clean.
  9. Cool in the pan on a wire rack for 15 minutes.
  10. Remove from pan and cool completely on a wire rack.

Makes 1 loaf.

Raspberry Pecan Bread

Ingredients

  • 2 cups Flour
  • 1/2 cup Sugar
  • 2 tsp Baking Powder
  • 1/2 tsp Salt
  • 1/4 tsp Baking Soda
  • 2 eggs
  • 1/2 cup Vanilla Yogurt
  • 1/3 cup Orange Juice
  • 1/4 cup unsweetened Apple Sauce
  • 1/4 cup Canola Oil
  • 1/2 tsp Orange Extract
  • 1 cup Chopped Pecans
  • 1 cup Fresh or Frozen Raspberries
    (If using frozen raspberries, do not thaw before adding to batter)

Glaze:

  • 1 cup Powdered Sugar
  • 4-5 tsp Orange Juice

Instructions

In a large bowl, combine the flour, sugar, baking powder, salt, and baking soda.

In a small bowl, whisk the eggs, yogurt, orange juice, applesauce, oil, and extract.

Stir into dry ingredients just until moist. Fold in pecans and raspberries.

Pour into 6 small greased and floured loaf pans. Bake at 350 degrees for 25-28 minutes or until a toothpick inserted comes out clean.

Cool for 10 minutes before removing from pans to wire racks.

For the glaze, combine powdered sugar and enough orange juice to achieve drizzling consistency. Drizzle over loaves.

Enjoy!

Breads |

Resurrection Rolls

Ingredients

  • 2 (10 oz) cans crescent dinner rolls
  • 16 large marshmallows
  • 1/2 cup butter, melted
  • 1/2 cup sugar
  • 4 tbsp ground cinnamon

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 375 degrees.

  2. Spray 16 cups in a muffin tin with Pam; coat really well.

  3. Separate crescent rolls into individual triangles.

  4. In a small bowl, combine sugar and cinnamon.

  5. In another small bowl, melt butter.

  6. Dip a marshmallow into the butter and then roll in the cinnamon sugar mixture.

  7. Place the marshmallow at the fat end of the triangle and roll it up.

  8. Carefully seal all edges.

  9. Place in a well-greased muffin tin.

  10. Repeat.

  11. Place muffin tins on a cookie sheet with parchment paper (the marshmallow oozes out while baking and can make a big mess in your oven)

  12. Place cookie sheet in oven and bake about 15 minutes, or until golden brown.

  13. Let cool; then remove from pan.

  14. Very sticky but very good!

These are called resurrection rolls because the marshmallow disappears while baking leaving an empty tomb!
