Sour Cream Coffee Cake

Ingredients

  • 2 sticks softened butter
  • 1 1/2 cups sugar
  • 8 oz sour cream
  • 2 eggs well beaten
  • 1 tsp vanilla
  • 2 cups flour
  • 1 tsp baking powder
  • 1/4 tsp salt
  • 1/2 tsp baking soda

Filling:

  • 1 cup chopped nuts
  • 1 cup brown sugar
  • 2 1/2 tsp cinnamon

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 350 degrees. Grease and flour bundt pan.

  2. In a large mixing bowl, cream butter, sugar, and sour cream. Add eggs, beat well. Add vanilla, beat well.

  3. In a medium mixing bowl, combine flour, baking powder, salt, and baking soda.

  4. Slowly add dry ingredients to creamed mixture. Pour a portion of the batter into greased and floured bundt pan. Alternate layers of filling and batter.

5.Bake 40-45 minutes. When toothpick inserted in center comes out clean, the cake is done.

  1. Let cool for 15 minutes before turning out onto a cake plate.

Enjoy!

'Now this is eternal life: that they may know you, the only true God, and Jesus Christ, whom you have sent.' *John 17:3

Cakes |

Strawberry Cake

Ingredients

  • FROZEN STRAWBERRIES WILL BE USED FOR FROSTING AND CAKE:

  • 1 carton frozen strawberries (thawed)

  • 1 white cake mix

  • 3 tbsp flour

  • 1 small box strawberry jello

  • 3 egg whites

  • 1 tsp almond extract

  • 1/4 cup oil

  • 1 1/4 cup water

  • 1/4 cup juice from frozen strawberries

  • FROSTING:

  • 1 lb box powdered sugar

  • 1/3 cup Crisco shortening

  • pinch of salt

  • 1 tsp vanilla

  • strawberries, no juice

Instructions

  1. Thaw frozen carton of strawberries. Separate the strawberries from the juice and set aside.

  2. Grease and flour (I use Bakers Joy) a bundt pan. *you can use 2-9" cake pans, cupcake pans, or 9x13 baking pan.

  3. In a large mixing bowl, mix together cake mix, flour, and jello.

  4. Add egg whites, almond extract, oil, water, and strawberry juice. Blend until moist, about 30 seconds.

  5. Beat on medium speed for 2 minutes.

  6. Pour batter into pan and bake immediately.

  7. Follow baking directions on cake mix.

  8. Cool completely before frosting.

TO MAKE FROSTING:
In a large mixing bowl, beat Crisco until smooth. (about 3-5 minutes) Add vanilla and salt. Mix well. Slowly add powdered sugar. Add drained strawberries a few tablespoon at a time. Beat together until creamy (about 5-10 minutes). Be careful not to add too many strawberries or the frosting will be too runny.

"This is real love - not that we loved God, but that he loved us and sent his Son as a sacrifice to take away our sins." 1 John 4:10 NLT

Chocolate Chow Mein Clusters

Ingredients

  • 1 cup semi-sweet chocolate chips
  • 1 cup butterscotch chips
  • 1 cup chow mein noodles
  • 1 cup salted peanuts

Instructions

  1. In a heavy saucepan or microwave, melt the chocolate and butterscotch chips; stir until smooth.

  2. Stir in the chow mein noodles and peanuts until well coated.

  3. Drop by teaspoonfuls onto a waxed paper-lined baking sheet.

  4. Let cool until set, then store in a covered container.

Candy |

Cookie Dough Truffles

Ingredients

  • ½ cup butter, softened
  • ¾ cup brown sugar
  • 1 tsp vanilla
  • 2 - 3 cups flour
  • ½ tsp salt
  • 14 oz can sweetened condensed milk
  • ½ cup semisweet mini chocolate chips
  • 1 cup finely chopped pecans
  • 2 lbs chocolate almond bark, melted

Instructions

  1. In a large bowl, cream butter and brown sugar with an electric mixer.
  2. Add vanilla.
  3. Gradually add 2 cups of flour and salt.
  4. Add sweetened condensed milk.
  5. Add chocolate chips and pecans; mix well.
  6. If dough is too sticky, add more flour a tablespoon at a time until dough is firm enough to shape into balls. Using a small cookie scoop, shape into bite-size balls. (If dough is sticky, coat hands with cooking spray)
  7. Place on wax paper; chill for 1-2 hours.
  8. Melt chocolate bark according to package directions.
  9. Dip cookie balls into chocolate to cover.
  10. Sprinkle with sprinkles if desired.
  11. Place on wax paper and let chocolate harden before storing.
  12. Enjoy!

"I am the Lord, and there is no other, besides me there is no God; I equip you, though you do not know me, that people may know, from the rising of the sun and from the west, that there is none besides me; I am the LORD, and there is no other." Isaiah 45:5-6 ESV

Cream Cheese Mints

Ingredients

  • 4oz cream cheese, softened
  • 1/8 to 1/4 tsp peppermint oil (start with 1/8, taste, then add more flavor is desired)
  • 4 cups powdered sugar
  • 1 drop gel paste food coloring (if your want to color your mints)

to make chocolate cream cheese mints:
**add 1/4 cup coco powder, 1/2 tsp vanilla, pinch of salt.

Instructions

Line a baking sheet with parchment paper.

In a medium mixing bowl, beat cream cheese and peppermint oil until smooth.

Gradually add powdered sugar, beating until well combined. (Dough will be soft and elastic.)

Roll dough into small balls and place on prepared baking sheet.

Press fork into each ball to flatten.

Allow mints to set out for 2 hours, flipping over after 1 hour.

Store covered in the refrigerator - check date on cream cheese to see how long they will last

Glazed Crackers

Ingredients

  • 1 cup butter
  • 1 cup brown sugar
  • 1 tsp vanilla
  • 1/4 tsp Salt
  • 1 cup chopped pecans
  • 2 sleeves Ritz crackers, about 45 crackers
  • 11oz chocolate chips

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 350 degrees.

  2. Prepare a large cookie sheet with foil or parchment paper.

  3. Lightly spray with cooking spray

  4. Place cracker in a single layer cookie sheet

5.In a medium saucepan over medium heat, combine butter, sugar and salt.

  1. Bring to a boil,stirring occasionally.

  2. Once boiling, let boil for 5-7 minutes. Dont stir. Swirl pan to even out the color. You're wanting golden amber color.

  3. Remove from heat and stir in vanilla extract.

  4. Pour mixture over crackers. Use a spatula to spread over and cover the crackers evenly.

  5. Bake for 12-15 minutes or until bubbly, thickened and darker in color.

  6. Remove from oven and immediately sprinkle chocolate chips over crackers.

  7. Let sit for 5 minutes, then spread the chocolate chips over the crackers.

11.You can sprinkle chopped nuts or sprinkles on top if you wish.

  1. Let cool 2 hours so the chocolate will harden.

ENJOY!
*Store crackers covered at room temperature or in the refrigerator.

Hershey Kiss Pretzels

Ingredients

  • 1 bag pretzels, square or ring shapes
  • 1 pkg. Hershey kisses (you choose the flavor) or Rolo's
  • Optional: chocolate sprinkles

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 275 degrees.

  2. Place pretzels on a baking sheet.

  3. Place a Hershey kiss or Rolo in the middle of each pretzel.

  4. Bake for 2-3 minutes or until chocolate is slightly softened.

  5. Remove from oven and place another pretzel on top. Press pretzel firmly into chocolate letting the chocolate squish out.

  6. Sprinkle with chocolate sprinkles immediately if desired.

  7. Let chocolate harden before storing.

Enjoy!

Candy |

Microwave Peanut Brittle

Ingredients

  • 1 cup raw peanuts
  • 1 cup sugar
  • 1/8 tsp salt
  • 1/2 cup light corn syrup
  • 1 TB butter
  • 1 tsp vanilla
  • 1 tsp baking soda

Instructions

DO NOT DOUBLE THIS RECIPE!

  1. Place a piece of foil onto a cookie sheet and spray with Pam.

  2. In a glass bowl, mix sugar, salt, corn syrup, and raw peanuts. Microwave on high for 3 to 3 3/4 minutes (depending on microwave). It should be boiling.

  3. Remove from microwave, stir, and microwave for 3 3/4 minutes.

  4. Remove, stir again, and add butter and vanilla. Stir.

  5. Microwave for 1 1/2 minutes. Remove and add baking soda. Stir and pour onto greased foil. Let cool and break apart.

Peanut Butter Bonbons

Ingredients

  • 1 stick butter, softened
  • 8 oz jar peanut butter, creamy or crunchy
  • 3 cups powdered sugar
  • 1/4 cup light corn syrup
  • 1 lb chocolate almond bark

Optional:

  • Chopped peanuts

Instructions

  1. In a large bowl, combine butter, peanut butter, powdered sugar, and corn syrup. Mix well.
    (I use my hands but you can use an electric mixer with a dough hook)

  2. Roll into balls and place onto a cookie sheet lined with wax paper. Chill until hard.

  3. Melt almond bark according to package instructions.

  4. Dip balls into chocolate and place back onto wax paper.

Optional:
Top with chopped peanuts while chocolate is still wet.

You can make this with creamy or crunchy peanut butter. Both ways are great! Enjoy.

Peanut Butter Fudge

Ingredients

  • 2 cups sugar
  • 1 cup milk
  • 7 oz jar marshmallow cream
  • 18 oz jar creamy or crunchy peanut butter
  • 1 tsp vanilla

Instructions

In medium saucepan on medium heat, cook milk and sugar until rolling boil. Continue cooking for 7-10 minutes
( 235°degrees on a candy thermometer - soft ball stage); stirring constantly.

Remove from heat; add peanut butter, marshmallow cream, and vanilla. Beat until creamy.

Pour into greased 9x13 pan. Let cool. Cut and enjoy!

To check soft ball stage without a candy thermometer, from spoon drop a drip of mixture into a glass of water. When it goes into the water, you should be able to form it into a soft ball.

Ingredients

Triple Nut Candy

Ingredients

  • 1 1/2 cups Sugar
  • 1 cups heavy Whipping Cream
  • 1/2 cup Light Corn Syrup
  • 3 cups of Nuts (1 cup of 3 different types of nuts)
  • (Pecans,Walnuts, Almonds, Cashews, Peanuts, Macadamia nuts - your choice)

*Butter for greasing pan

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 350°
  2. Place nuts in a single layer on a baking sheet.
  3. Bake for 4- 8 minutes until toasted, stirring once.
  4. Line a 8" square baking pan with foil
  5. Grease the foil with butter and set aside
  6. In a large saucepan, combine sugar, cream, and corn syrup.
  7. Bring to a boil over medium heat, stirring constantly.
  8. Once a boil begins, add toasted nuts.
  9. Continue cooking with out stirring until candy thermometer reads 238° soft ball stage.
  10. Remove from heat and stir with wooden spoon until creamy.
  11. Quickly spread into into the prepared pan.
  12. Let set for 8 hours or overnight.
  13. Using the foil, lift candy out of the pan.
  14. Cut into squares and store in an airtight container.
    Enjoy!

“But the man who loves God is known by God.”
I Corinthians 8:3

White Coconut Fudge

Ingredients

  • 3 cups White Chocolate Chips
  • 1 14oz can Eagle Brand Milk
  • 1/8 tsp salt
  • 1 cup chopped macadamia nuts
  • 1/4 cup chopped macadamia nuts
  • 1/4 cup coconut
  • 1 1/2 tsp coconut extract

Instructions

  1. Line an 8x8 baking pan with foil, butter foil; set pan aside.

  2. In a medium saucepan, combine chips, Eagle Brand, and salt.

  3. Cook and stir over low heat until baking pieces are melted and smooth.

  4. Remove pan from heat; stir in 1 cup macadamia nuts, coconut, and coconut extract.

  5. Spread fudge in pan.

  6. Sprinkle 1/4 cup nuts on top.

  7. Cover, let set until firm.

When fudge is firm, use foil to lift it from pan.
Peel off foil and cut fudge into squares.
Store in a tightly covered container for up to 2 weeks.

Almond Butter Sugar Cookies

Ingredients

  • 1 cup butter, softened
  • 1 1/2 cups sugar
  • 2 eggs
  • 1 teaspoon vanilla
  • 1 teaspoon almond extract
  • 1 teaspoon butter flavoring
  • 3 cups flour
  • 2 teaspoons baking powder
  • 1 teaspoon baking soda
  • 1/2 teaspoon salt

Cream Cheese Frosting:

  • 1 (8 oz) package cream cheese, softened
  • 1/4 cup butter, softened
  • 1 teaspoon vanilla
  • dash salt
  • 3-4 cups powdered sugar
  • 2-3 tablespoons milk

Instructions

COOKIES

  1. Cream your butter and sugar together for at least 2 minutes.
  2. Add your eggs, vanilla, almond extract, and butter flavoring. Beat the mixture for another 2 minutes or until everything is nice and fluffy.
  3. In a separate bowl use a whisk to combine the flour, baking powder, baking soda, and salt. .
  4. Add the dry ingredients to the creamed mixture and mix until combined.
  5. Roll into 1 1/2 inch balls and place on a cookie sheet that has been sprayed with cooking spray or lined with parchment paper.
  6. Bake at 350°F for about 7 minutes or until the tops of the cookies start to crack.
  7. Take them out of the oven and let them continue to cook on the hot sheet for several minutes.
  8. Remove the cookies and place them on a wire rack to cool completely.

CREAM CHEESE FROSTING

  1. Place the cream cheese and butter in your stand mixer, or large mixing bowl.
  2. Beat it until the two are well combined and nice and smooth.
  3. Add the powdered sugar, milk, vanilla, and dash of salt. Beat well. Add a bit more powdered sugar if it's too thin, and more milk if it's too thick.
  4. Add a few drops of food coloring and mix it in.
  5. Spread or pipe the frosting onto your cooled cookies and top with sprinkles.
    Enjoy!

"But now, O Lord, you are our Father; we are the clay, and you are the potter; we are all the work of your hand" Isaiah 64:8 ESV

Basic Cookie Dough

Ingredients

  • 1 cup melted butter (melt butter in microwave)
  • 1 cup sugar
  • 1 cup brown sugar
  • 1 tsp vanilla
  • 2 eggs
  • 1 tsp salt
  • 1 tsp baking soda
  • 3 cups flour (flour measurement will change if adding oatmeal)

Additional ingredients depending on what cookie you are making:

  • 1 cup chopped Pecans or Macadamia Nuts
  • 6 oz pkg sliced Almonds (2 cups)
  • 6 oz pkg dried Craisins (2 cups)
  • 3 cups old-fashioned Oats
  • 1/2 cup cocoa powder

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 350 degrees.

  2. In a large mixing bowl, mix together melted butter, sugar, brown sugar, vanilla, and eggs. Add salt and baking soda.

  3. Add 3 cups of flour. Mix well.

  4. Add chocolate chips and nuts if desired.

  5. Using a cookie scoop, scoop dough onto cookie sheet covered with parchment paper.

  6. Bake at 350 for 11 minutes.*All ovens bake differently so you may need to adjust baking time according to your oven.

  7. Remove from oven and let cool on cookie sheet for at least 2 minutes.

  8. Place cooled cookies on a cooling rack.
    Enjoy!

Directions for making other types of cookies with this dough.

*For white chocolate/macadamia nut cookies, add 3 cups flour. Mix well. Add 12 oz pkg white chocolate chips and 1 cup chopped macadamia nuts.

*For oatmeal/almond/cranberry cookies, add 1 1/2 cups flour and 3 cups old-fashioned oats. Mix well. Add 6 oz pkg of sliced almonds and 6 oz pkg of craisins (dried cranberries).

*For chocolate chocolate chip cookies, add 2 1/2 cups flour and 1/2 cup cocoa powder. Mix well. Add 12 oz pkg chocolate chips and 1 cup chopped pecans (optional).

Follow the same baking instructions for all cookies.

*** My Family loves to make pizookies with this cookie dough. The recipe will make 9 - 6 1/2" iron skillets. (I bought mine on amazon)
I put 2 large scoops of cookie dough in each skillet and then press it down to fill the skillet. You do not need to preheat or grease the iron skillet. Bake at 350 degrees for 15 minutes. Remove from oven. Serve with ice cream in the center. Enjoy! The skillets are very hot.

'I praise you because I am fearfully and wonderfully made.'
Psalm 139:14

Butter Cookie

Ingredients

  • 1 cup butter, softened
  • 1 1/4 cup powdered sugar
  • 1 egg
  • 1 1/2 tsp vanilla extract
  • 1/2 tsp almond extract
  • 2 1/2 cups flour
  • 1/2 tsp salt
  • Food coloring

Instructions

  1. In a large mixing bowl, cream butter; gradually add powdered sugar, beating at a medium speed until light and fluffy.
  2. Add egg, vanilla, almond extract, and food coloring; beat until blended.
  3. In a small mixing bowl, combine flour and salt; add to creamed mixture, mixing well.
  4. Spoon dough into a pastry bag and pipe mixture into shapes on an ungreased cookie sheet.
  5. Bake at 400 degrees for 6-8 minutes. Remove to a wire rack to cool.

I make hearts for Valentine's, Easter eggs for Easter, stars for the 4th of July or Christmas, pumpkins for Halloween, and Christmas trees for Christmas. Just use your imagination!

"Love is patient, and kind: love does not envy or boast; it is not arrogant" 1 Corinthians 13:4 ESV

Choc Chip Oreo Fudge Brownie

Ingredients

  • 1 cup (2 sticks) butter, softened
  • 1 cup granulated sugar
  • 3/4 cup light brown sugar
  • 2 large eggs
  • 1 Tablespoon pure vanilla extract
  • 2 1/2 cups all-purpose flour
  • 1 teaspoon baking soda
  • 1 teaspoon salt
  • 2 cups (12 ounces) milk chocolate chips
  • 1 pkg Double Stuffed Oreos
  • 1 Family Size (9×13) Brownie mix
  • 1/4 cup hot fudge topping

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 350 degrees F.
  2. Cream the butter and both sugars in a large bowl with an electric mixer on medium speed for 3-5 minutes.
  3. Add the eggs and vanilla and mix well to thoroughly combine.
  4. In a separate bowl, whisk together the flour, baking soda, and salt, then slowly incorporate into the mixer until the flour is just combined.
  5. Stir in chocolate chips.
  6. Spread the cookie dough in the bottom of a 9×13 baking dish that’s been sprayed with cooking spray then lined with parchment paper
  7. Top with a layer of Oreos on top of the cookie dough
  8. Mix together brownie mix, adding an optional 1/4 cup of hot fudge topping to the mix.
  9. Pour the brownie batter over the cookie dough and Oreos.
  10. Bake at 350 degrees F for 45-55 minutes.

To half this recipe for an 8×8 brownie mix, just half the chocolate chip cookie dough ingredients. Enjoy!

"And he is before all things, and in him all things hold together" Colossians 1:17 ESV

Chocolate Fudge Cookies

Ingredients

  • 6 Tb butter
  • 1 can Eagle Brand Condensed Milk
  • 12 oz pkg chocolate chips
  • 1 cup flour
  • 2 cups chopped nuts

Instructions

  1. Combine butter, Eagle Brand milk, and chocolate chips in a large saucepan. Cook over low heat, stirring frequently until melted and smooth. Stir in flour and nuts.

  2. Drop by teaspoon onto lightly greased cookie sheet.

  3. Bake at 300 degrees for 10–12 minutes or until a light glaze forms on top of cookies. Do not overcook.

** Enjoy! My mom's best friend, Phyllis, shared this recipe with me when I got married.

Cookies |

Chocolate Kiss Thumbprint Cookie

Ingredients

  • 2/3 cup unsalted butter, softened
  • 1/2 cup sugar
  • 1/4 cup unsweetened dark cocoa powder
  • 1/4 teaspoon baking soda
  • 1/8 teaspoon salt
  • 1 egg
  • 1 teaspoon vanilla
  • 1 1/4 cups all-purpose flour
  • 3/4 cup chocolate or multicolor sprinkles
  • 20 milk chocolate kisses, unwrapped

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 375 degrees. Line two cookie sheets with parchment paper.

  2. In a large mixing bowl, beat butter on medium-high speed for 30 seconds.

  3. Add sugar, cocoa powder, baking soda, and salt. Beat until combined, scraping the sides of the bowl occasionally.

  4. Beat in egg and vanilla until combined.

  5. Beat in the flour.

  6. Your dough should be at ball-rolling consistency at this point. If it's not, let it chill in the refrigerator for a while to firm up a little.

  7. Once easily handled, roll dough into 1-inch balls.

  8. If you want, roll the balls in sprinkles to coat.

  9. Place balls 2 inches apart on your lined cookie sheets, and, using your thumb, make an indentation in the center of each cookie.

  10. Bake for 7 to 8 minutes or until edges are firm. Immediately press a chocolate kiss into each cookie's center. Transfer to a wire rack and let cool.

"You keep track of all my sorrows. You have collected all my tears in your bottle. You have recorded each one in your book" Psalm 56:8 NLT

Cookies |

Cream Cheese Cookies

Ingredients

  • ½ cup Cream Cheese, softened
  • 1 cup Powdered Sugar
  • ½ cup Sugar
  • ½ cup chopped Walnuts
  • ½ tsp. Vanilla
  • ½ cup Shortening
  • ½ tsp. Almond extract
  • ½ cup Butter, softened
  • 1 ½ cups all-purpose flour

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 350 degrees.

  2. Sift powdered sugar into a shallow bowl, stir in the walnuts and set aside.

  3. In a large mixing bowl using an electric mixer, combine shortening, butter, cream cheese and sugar together until creamy and thoroughly blended (about 2-5 minutes).

  4. Mix in the almond extract, vanilla, and flour. Stir to combine.

  5. Scoop dough by rounded tablespoons and roll into balls about 1 inch in diameter.

  6. Place the balls on ungreased cookie sheets.

  7. Bake in preheated oven until the cookies turn slightly golden at the edges, about 8-15 minutes.

  8. Let the cookies cool on the baking sheets for 1 minute, then roll in the powdered sugar-walnut mixture while still a little warm.

Two are better than one, because they have a good reward for their labor. For if they fall, one will lift up his companion. But woe to him who is alone when he falls, for he has no one to help him up. Ecclesiastes 4:9-10

Gooey Butter Cookies

Ingredients

  • 8 oz pkg cream cheese, softened
  • 1/2 cup butter, softened
  • 1 egg
  • 1/4 tsp vanilla
  • 1 butter cake mix
  • Powdered sugar

Instructions

Preheat oven to 350 degrees.

In a large mixing bowl, cream together cream cheese and butter.

Stir in egg and vanilla.

Add cake mix and stir until well blended.

Batter will be sticky!

Using a cookie scoop, scoop balls of dough and roll in powdered sugar.

Place on ungreased cookie sheet about 1" apart.

Bake 11-13 minutes.

Let cool on pan 1 minute then remove from pan and let finish cooling on wire racks.

Enjoy!

Homemade Oreo Cookies

Ingredients

  • 2 packages devil's food cake mix
    • Experiment with different cake mixes!
  • 5 eggs
  • 2 sticks of butter

Icing:

  • 1 (8 oz) package of cream cheese
  • 1 stick of butter
  • 3 2/3 cups of powdered sugar

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 350 degrees.
  2. Mix cookie ingredients together and spoon small amounts onto greased cookie sheets. (A cookie scoop works great!)
  3. Bake 8-10 minutes. For cake-like cookies, pull out as soon as a clean toothpick can be removed from the cookie center.
  4. Let cool completely.
  5. Mix together icing ingredients and frost between two cookies.
  6. Can be refrigerated briefly to help icing harden.
  7. Enjoy!
Cookies |

Iced Pumpkin Cookies

Ingredients

  • 2 ½ cups Flour
  • 1 tsp Baking Powder
  • 1 tsp Baking Soda
  • 2 tsp Cinnamon
  • ½ tsp Nutmeg
  • ½ tsp Cloves
  • ½ tsp Salt
  • ½ cup Butter, softened
  • 1 cup Sugar
  • ½ cup Brown Sugar
  • 1 cup canned Pumpkin
  • 1 egg
  • 1 tsp Vanilla

Icing:

  • 2 cups Powdered Sugar
  • 3 TB Milk (may need more if icing is too thick)
  • 1 TB melted Butter
  • 1 tsp Vanilla

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 350 degrees.

  2. In a medium mixing bowl, whisk together flour, baking powder, baking soda, cinnamon, nutmeg, cloves, and salt. Set aside.

  3. In a large bowl, beat on medium-high speed butter and sugars until light and fluffy.

  4. Add pumpkin, egg, and vanilla; beat until creamy.

  5. Mix in dry ingredients.

  6. Using a cookie scoop, drop onto an ungreased cookie sheet.

  7. Flatten slightly with the bottom of a cup dipped in sugar.

  8. Bake for 15-20 minutes.

  9. Allow to cool for 5 minutes before removing to a wire rack.

  10. Once cool, spread icing on top of the cookie. Enjoy!

Icing Directions:

While the cookie is baking, combine powdered sugar, milk, butter, and vanilla in a medium mixing bowl. Add more milk to get the desired consistency. Ice cookies once cool. Enjoy!

"He must become greater; I must become less" John 3:30 NIV

Cookies |

Lemon Sugar Cookies

Ingredients

  • 1 1/2 cups cake flour
  • 1 1/2 tsp baking powder
  • 1/4 tsp salt
  • 1/2 cup (1 stick) butter, room temperature*
  • 1/2 cup sugar
  • 1 large egg, room temperature
  • Zest and 1 tbsp Lemon juice from 1 Lemon
  • 1/4 tsp vanilla

Frosting:

  • 1/4 cup (1/2 stick) butter, room temperature*
  • 1 1/2 cups powdered sugar
  • Zest and 1 tbsp Lemon juice from 1 small Lemon
  • 1/4 tsp vanilla

Instructions

To make cookies:

  1. In a small bowl, combine flour, baking powder, and salt.
  2. In a medium mixing bowl, using a hand mixer or stand mixer, cream the butter and sugar until light and fluffy.
  3. Add the egg and beat until combined.
  4. Zest the lemon and then squeeze the juice, adding them both to the creamed mixture.
  5. Add the vanilla and stir to combine.
  6. Gradually stir in the dry ingredients into the wet and stir until combined.
  7. Scoop out the dough onto plastic wrap and place in fridge for 1 hour.
  8. Preheat oven to 350 degrees.
  9. Line a baking sheet with parchment paper.
  10. Sprinkle some flour onto the counter and coat your rolling pin.
  11. Roll out the dough a little thicker than 1/4 inch.
  12. Cut with a round cookie cutter and place them on the baking sheet.
  13. Bake for 10 minutes, until soft and golden.
  14. Transfer to wire rack and let cool before frosting.

To make the frosting:

  1. Beat the butter, powdered sugar, lemon juice, zest, and vanilla until light and fluffy.

Once the cookies are cooled, cover with frosting.
The frosting will harden in about an hour, then place them in an airtight container to store in the fridge for a few weeks. Enjoy!

"But according to His promise we are waiting for new heavens and a new earth in which righteousness dwells" 2 Peter 3:13 ESV

No Bake Cookies

Ingredients

  • 2 cups sugar
  • 1/4 cup cocoa
  • 1/2 cup milk
  • 1 stick margarine
  • 1 tsp vanilla
  • pinch of salt
  • 1/2 cup chunky peanut butter
  • 3 cups quick cooking oatmeal

Instructions

In a medium saucepan, mix margarine, milk, cocoa, and sugar. Cook on medium heat until it starts to boil. Cool for one minute. Then add vanilla, salt, peanut butter, and oatmeal.

Stir well.

Drop by teaspoonfuls on wax paper. Let cool.

ENJOY!

Peanut Butter Cup Peanut Butter Cookies

Ingredients

  • 1 ¾ cup flour
  • ½ tsp salt
  • 1 tsp baking soda
  • ½ cup butter, softened
  • ½ cup sugar
  • ½ cup brown sugar
  • ½ cup peanut butter
  • 2 tbsp milk
  • 1 egg, beaten
  • 1 tsp vanilla
  • 40 miniature Reese’s Peanut Butter cups, unwrapped

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 375 degrees.

  2. In a mixing bowl, combine the flour, salt, and baking soda; set aside.

  3. In another large mixing bowl, cream the butter, sugar, peanut butter, and brown sugar until fluffy.

  4. Beat in the egg, vanilla, and milk.

  5. Add the flour mixture and mix well.

  6. Shape into 40 balls and place each into an ungreased mini muffin pan.

  7. Bake at 375 degrees for 8 minutes. Remove from oven and immediately press a mini Reese’s cup into each ball.

  8. Cool and carefully remove from pan.

  9. Enjoy!

"The thief comes only to steal and kill and destroy. I came that they may have life and have it abundantly" John 10:10 ESV

Cookies |

Pumpkin Brownies

Ingredients

  • 3/4 cup Flour
  • 1/2 tsp Baking Powder
  • 1/2 tsp Salt
  • 3/4 cup Butter, melted
  • 1 1/2 cup Sugar
  • 2 tsp Vanilla
  • 3 eggs
  • 1/4 cup Cocoa Powder
  • 1/2 cup Chocolate Chips
  • 1/2 cup Pumpkin Puree
  • 1/2 cup Chopped Walnuts
  • 3/4 tsp Cinnamon
  • 1/2 tsp ground Cloves
  • 1/2 tsp ground Nutmeg

Instructions

Preheat oven to 350 degrees.

Grease an 8x8 baking dish.

In a mixing bowl, combine the flour, baking powder, and salt. Set aside.

In another mixing bowl, combine butter, sugar, and vanilla.

Beat the eggs in one at a time.

Gradually add the flour mixture and stir the batter until evenly moist.

Divide the batter in half - into 2 separate bowls.

Into one bowl of batter, stir in the cocoa powder and chocolate chips.

Into the other bowl of batter, stir in the pumpkin, walnuts, cinnamon, cloves, and nutmeg.

Spread 1/2 the chocolate batter into the bottom of the prepared baking dish.

Spread 1/2 the pumpkin batter on top of the chocolate batter.

Repeat the layers, ending with a pumpkin layer.

Drag a kitchen knife through the layers in a swirling motion to create a marble appearance.

Bake in preheated oven until the brownies begin to pull away from the sides of the pan and a toothpick inserted into the center comes out clean, 40-45 minutes.

Cool in the pan, cut into squares, and enjoy!

"O Lord, you are my God; I will exalt you; I will praise your name, for you have done wonderful things, plans formed of old, faithful and sure" Isaiah 25:1 ESV

Cookies |

Pumpkin Whoopie Pies

Ingredients

  • 3 cups all-purpose flour

  • 2 tablespoons cinnamon

  • 1 teaspoon baking powder

  • 1 teaspoon baking soda

  • 1 teaspoon salt

  • 1 teaspoon ground ginger

  • ½ teaspoon ground nutmeg

  • 2 cups brown sugar

  • 1 cup vegetable oil

  • 3 cups chilled pumpkin puree (canned pumpkin) about 1 1/2 (15oz) cans

  • 2 eggs

  • 1 teaspoon vanilla extract

For the Maple-Cream Cheese Filling:

  • 1 1/2 cups powdered sugar, sifted
  • 4 ounces cream cheese, at room temperature
  • 1/4 cup butter, at room temperature
  • 2-3 tablespoons maple syrup
  • 1/2 teaspoon vanilla extract

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 350 degrees F. Line baking sheets with parchment paper.

  2. In a medium mixing bowl, whisk together the flour, cinnamon, baking powder, baking soda, salt, ginger, and nutmeg. Set aside.

  3. In a separate large mixing bowl, mix the brown sugar and the oil together.

  4. Add the pumpkin puree and whisk to combine thoroughly.

  5. Add the eggs and vanilla and whisk until combined.

  6. Gradually add the flour mixture to the pumpkin mixture and stir until completely combined.

  7. Use a cookie scoop to drop a rounded, heaping tablespoon of the dough onto the prepared baking sheets, about 1 inch apart.

  8. Bake for 10 to 12 minutes, making sure that the cookies are just starting to crack on top. The cookies should be firm when touched.

  9. Remove from the oven and let the cookies cool completely before filling with icing.

  10. To make the filling, beat the butter and the cream cheese until smooth and combined.

  11. Add the powdered sugar a little at a time.

  12. Add the maple syrup and vanilla and beat until smooth.

  13. To assemble the whoopie pies: Turn half of the cooled cookies upside down. Pipe or spread with a knife the filling (about a tablespoon) onto that half.

  14. Place another cookie, flat side down, on top of the filling. Press down slightly so that the filling spreads to the edges of the cookie. Repeat until all the cookies are used.

  15. Put the whoopie pies in the refrigerator for about 30 minutes to firm before serving. You can dust with powdered sugar before serving.

Enjoy!

*Store in refrigerator.

"The Lord is not slow to fulfill his promise as some count slowness, but is patient toward you, not wishing that any should perish, but that all should reach repentance." 2 Peter 3:9 ESV

Red Velvet Whoopie Pies

Ingredients

Cookie ingredients:

  • 1 Box Red Velvet Cake Mix
  • 1/2 cup Butter, softened to room temperature
  • 2 eggs

Filling ingredients:

  • 2 sticks (1 lb) Butter, softened
  • 8 oz Cream Cheese, softened
  • 4 cups Powdered Sugar
  • 16 oz Marshmallow Creme, such as Marshmallow Fluff
  • pinch of salt
  • 1 tsp Vanilla

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 350 degrees.
  2. In a large mixing bowl, combine cake mix, butter, and eggs.
  3. Mix well with an electric mixer until combined.
  4. Line a cookie sheet with parchment paper.
  5. Using a cookie scoop, drop batter onto the baking sheet about 2 inches apart.
  6. Bake for 10-12 minutes.
  7. Let cool before adding filling.

To make filling:

  1. In a large mixing bowl, with an electric mixer, beat together butter and powdered sugar until smooth.
  2. Beat in marshmallow creme and vanilla.
  3. Mix until smooth and combined.
    *Extra filling may be stored in refrigerator for 5-6 days.
    *This makes a great frosting for cupcakes

To assemble:
To assemble the whoopie pies: Turn half of the cooled cookies upside down. Pipe or spread with a knife the filling (about a tablespoon) onto that half.

Place another cookie, flat side down, on top of the filling. Press down slightly so that the filling spreads to the edges of the cookie. Repeat until all the cookies are used.

Put the whoopie pies in the refrigerator for about 30 minutes to firm before serving. You can dust with powdered sugar before serving.

Enjoy!

*Store in refrigerator.

Snickerdoodles

Ingredients

  • 1 cup softened butter
  • 1 1/2 cups sugar
  • 2 eggs
  • 2 3/4 cups flour
  • 2 tsp cream of tartar
  • 1 tsp baking soda
  • 1/4 tsp salt

Topping:

  • 2 1/2 tbsp sugar
  • 2 tsp cinnamon

Instructions

Preheat oven to 400 degrees.

In a large mixing bowl, cream butter. Add sugar and beat until fluffy. Add eggs, mix thoroughly. Mix flour, cream of tartar, baking soda, and salt in a small mixing bowl. Add to the butter mixture a little at a time.

Mix cinnamon and sugar together in a small bowl. Spoon out a tsp of the dough and roll into a ball. Roll in the sugar-cinnamon mixture.

Place on an ungreased cookie sheet 2'' apart. (These cookies will spread) Bake 8 – 10 minutes. Let cool 1 minute, then move to a cooling rack. Enjoy!

Cookies |

Toffee Bars

Ingredients

  • 2 cups flour
  • 1 3/4 cups packed light brown sugar
  • 2 sticks butter
  • 1 stick butter, softened
  • 1 cup pecans, chopped
  • 1 (6 oz) pkg chocolate chips

Instructions

Preheat oven to 350 degrees.

  1. Mix the flour and 1 cup of the brown sugar, cut in 1 stick of softened butter until the mixture resembles fine crumbs.
  2. Press into the bottom of a 9x13 lightly greased baking dish. Sprinkle with pecans.
  3. In a medium saucepan, bring to a boil the 2 sticks of butter and 3/4 cup brown sugar. Boil exactly 1 minute, stirring constantly.
  4. Pour over the pressed mixture and bake exactly 20 minutes. Let sit 1 minute, then sprinkle with chocolate chips.
  5. When chips begin to melt, spread with a spatula. Chill thoroughly; return to room temperature before cutting.

White Chocolate Cranberry Oatmeal cookie - Gift Jar idea

Ingredients

  • 5/8 cup Flour
  • 1/2 cup Old Fashioned Oats
  • 1/2 cup Flour
  • 1/2 tsp Baking Soda
  • 1/2 tsp Salt
  • 1/3 cup Brown Sugar
  • 1/3 cup Sugar
  • 1/2 cup Dried Cranberries
  • 1/2 cup White Chocolate Chips
  • 1/2 cup Pecans, chopped

Wet ingredients:

  • 1/2 cup Butter, softened
  • 1 egg
  • 1 tsp Vanilla

Instructions

Preheat oven to 350 degrees.

Grease cookie sheet or line with parchment paper.

In a large mixing bowl, beat together 1/2 cup butter, egg, and vanilla until fluffy.

Add the entire jar of ingredients and mix together by hand until well blended.

Drop by teaspoonfuls onto prepared cookie sheets.

Bake for 8-10 minutes or until edges start to brown.

Cool 1 minute then remove to wire racks to finish cooling.

Enjoy!

To make gift jar:
Layer dry ingredients into a 1 quart jar, in the order listed above.

Attach a tag with the directions above.

Apple Crisp

Ingredients

  • 5 apples
  • 1/2 cup sugar
  • 2 tsp flour
  • 1/2 tsp cinnamon
  • 1 cup quick cooking oats
  • 1 cup flour
  • 1 cup brown sugar
  • 1/4 tsp baking powder
  • 1/4 tsp baking soda
  • 1/2 cup butter, melted

Instructions

  1. Peel, core and slice apples. In a large mixing bowl, mix sugar, 2 tsp flour and cinnamon together. Add apple slices and mix together. Place in greased 8x8 baking pan.

  2. In the same large mixing bowl, combine oats, 1 cup flour, brown sugar, baking powder, baking soda and melted butter. Crumble evenly over apple mixture.

  3. Bake at 350 degrees for about 45 minutes.

  4. Serve with vanilla ice cream and enjoy!

Apple Dumplings

Ingredients

  • 2 cans crescent rolls
  • 2 Granny Smith apples
  • 2 sticks butter
  • 1 ¼ cups sugar
  • 1 tsp vanilla
  • 12 oz Mountain Dew

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 350 degrees.

  2. Peel and slice each apple into 8 pieces.

Apricot Bars/Cranberry Bars

Ingredients

  • 3/4 cup butter (softened)

  • 1 cup brown sugar

  • 1 3/4 cup flour

  • 1/2 tsp baking soda

  • 1/2 tsp salt

  • 1 1/2 cup old-fashioned oats

  • For Apricot filling:

    • 1 package dried apricots
    • 1/4 cup sugar

  • If making cranberry bars, substitute 1 package of craisins for apricots

Instructions

  1. Cut apricots up and place in a small saucepan. Cover with water and cook covered until tender. Add sugar and set aside to cool. (This should take about 10 minutes)

  2. Heat oven to 400 degrees. Mix butter and brown sugar. Add dry ingredients, adding the oats last. Press half of the mixture into a greased 9x13 pan. Spread the filling on top. Crumble the rest of the mixture on top and pat lightly.

  3. Bake until golden brown, about 25-30 minutes. Check after 25 minutes. Cut while warm and remove from the pan.

**If making cranberry bars, cook the craisins the same as the apricots. There is no need to cut up the craisins.

  • Prep Time: 15 minutes

  • Cooking Time: 25-30 minutes

  • Makes about 20 bars.

Blessed be the Lord God, the God of Israel, Who alone works wonders. Psalm 72:18

Brownie in a Jar Mix

Ingredients

  • 2 cups sugar
  • 1 cup cocoa
  • 1 cup all-purpose flour
  • 1 cup chocolate chips
  • 1 cup butter, softened
  • 4 eggs

Instructions

In a wide-mouth quart jar, layer the following ingredients:

(Press each ingredient down into the jar)

  1. 1 cup sugar
  2. 1/2 cup cocoa
  3. 1 cup flour
  4. 1/2 cup cocoa
  5. 1 cup sugar
  6. 1 cup chocolate chips

Place the lid on the jar and attach a recipe card. Ingredients can be stored in the jar for up to 3 months.

To make mix:

  1. Preheat oven to 325°F. Grease 13x9 pan, bottom only.

  2. Cream butter, add eggs one at a time; mixing well after each.

  3. Add brownie mix. Continue to beat until smooth. Dough will be thick.

  4. Bake for 40–50 minutes. Let cool, then cut and enjoy.

Desserts |

Bryan's Puff Bars

Ingredients

  • 4 cups popped popcorn
  • 3 cups Rice Krispies
  • 2 cups plain M&M's (holiday colors)
  • 1 cup sugar
  • 1 cup light corn syrup
  • 3/4 cup creamy peanut butter
  • 1/4 cup margarine

Instructions

Combine popcorn, cereal, and M&M's in a large greased mixing bowl; set aside.
Grease a jelly roll pan.
In a large saucepan over medium heat, combine sugar, syrup, peanut butter, and margarine. Bring to a boil and boil 2 minutes, stirring constantly.
Immediately remove from heat and pour over popcorn mixture, mixing and tossing until thoroughly coated.
Quickly spoon and lightly press mixture into pan. Cool about 1 hour. Cut into bars.

Desserts |

Caramel Apple Cheesecake Bar

Ingredients

  • Crust:

    • 2 cups all-purpose flour
    • 1/2 cup firmly packed brown sugar
    • 1 cup (2 sticks) butter, softened

  • Cheesecake Filling:

    • 3 (8-ounce) packages cream cheese, softened
    • 3/4 cup sugar, plus 2 tablespoons, divided
    • 3 large eggs
    • 1 1/2 teaspoons vanilla extract

  • Apples:

    • 3 Granny Smith apples, peeled, cored and finely chopped
    • 1/2 teaspoon ground cinnamon
    • 1/4 teaspoon ground nutmeg

  • Streusel Topping:

    • 1 cup firmly packed brown sugar
    • 1 cup all-purpose flour
    • 1/2 cup quick cooking oats
    • 1/2 cup (1 stick) butter, softened

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 350 degrees.
  2. In a medium bowl, combine flour and brown sugar.
  3. Cut in butter with a pastry blender (or 2 forks) until mixture is crumbly.
  4. Press evenly into a 9x13 baking pan greased with PAM.
  5. Bake 15 minutes or until lightly browned.
  6. In a large bowl, beat cream cheese with 3/4 cup sugar in an electric mixer at medium speed until smooth.
  7. Then add eggs, 1 at a time, and vanilla. Stir to combine.
  8. Pour over warm crust.
  9. In a small bowl, stir together chopped apples, remaining 2 tablespoons sugar, cinnamon, and nutmeg.
  10. Spoon evenly over cream cheese mixture.
  11. In a small bowl, combine all streusel ingredients. Use your hands or pastry cutter to thoroughly combine the butter into the mixture.
  12. Sprinkle evenly with Streusel topping.
  13. Bake 40-45 minutes, or until filling is set.
  14. Drizzle with caramel topping and let cool. Serve cold and enjoy!

For whoever would save his life will lose it, but whoever loses his life for my sake will find it. For what will it profit a man if he gains the whole world and forfeits his soul?" Matthew 16:24

Caramel Apples

Ingredients

  • ½ cup Butter
  • 2 cups Brown Sugar
  • 1 cup Light Corn Syrup
  • Pinch of Salt
  • 1 can Eagle Brand Milk
  • 1 tsp Vanilla
  • 10 apples, washed and dried
  • 1 cup finely chopped peanuts

Instructions

Melt butter in medium saucepan; add brown sugar, corn syrup, and salt. Cook over medium heat, stirring occasionally, until mixture comes to a full boil. (about 10-12 minutes) Stir in Eagle Brand Milk. Continue cooking, stirring occasionally, until candy thermometer reaches 245 degrees. Remove from heat; stir in vanilla. Dip apples into caramel mixture. Dip into peanuts and place onto buttered wax paper. Enjoy!

Desserts |

Caramel Popcorn

Ingredients

  • 1 cup butter
  • 2 cups brown sugar
  • 1/2 cup corn syrup
  • 1 tsp salt
  • 1/2 tsp baking soda
  • 1 tsp vanilla
  • 3 bags popped microwave popcorn

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 250 degrees.

  2. Place popcorn in a very large bowl.

  3. In a medium saucepan over medium heat, melt butter. Stir in brown sugar, corn syrup, and salt. Bring to a boil, stirring constantly. Boil without stirring for 4 minutes.

  4. Remove from heat and stir in soda and vanilla. Slowly pour over popcorn, stirring to coat.

  5. Place in a large baking dish and bake in preheated oven for 1 hour. Stir every 15 minutes.

  6. Remove from oven and let cool completely before breaking into pieces. Store in an airtight container.

Desserts |

Cheesecake in a Jar

Ingredients

  • 1 cup graham cracker crumbs (about 1/2 a sleeve worth)
  • 1 tablespoon sugar
  • 2 tablespoons melted butter
  • 1 (8 oz) package cream cheese, room temperature
  • 1 (14 oz) can sweetened condensed milk
  • 1/2 teaspoon lemon juice (bottled is fine)
  • 1 (21 oz) can cherry pie filling
  • Redi-whip
  • 7 half-pint jars
  • 7 lids and rings

Instructions

  1. Make sure your jars are clean and dry before starting.

  2. For the graham crackers, either crush 1 package in a food processor or buy the crumbs already crushed (that's the easy way!).

  3. After you have crumbs, melt 2 tablespoons of butter in a medium mixing bowl.

  4. Add the graham cracker crumbs and 1 tablespoon sugar. Mix together and set aside.

  5. In another medium mixing bowl, beat cream cheese until smooth.

  6. Add sweetened condensed milk and beat for 3 minutes, scraping bottom as needed to make sure all the cream cheese is incorporated.

  7. Add 1/2 teaspoon lemon juice and beat for another minute.

  8. Time to fill your jars :)

    • Spoon 2 heaping teaspoons of graham cracker mixture into the bottom of a clean glass jar.

    • Next, layer 2 tablespoons of cream cheese mixture on top of the graham cracker crust.

    • Next, layer 2 tablespoons of cherry pie filling on top. Spread around to coat the top.

    • Spray a topping of Redi-whip on top of pie filling.

    • Smooth it with a spoon and repeat all layers, ending with the Redi-whip.

  9. Place lid on jar and refrigerate at least 1 hour before serving.

  10. These will keep for a couple of days if you want to make them ahead of time.

  • 1 cup sugar

Cream Puffs

Ingredients

Filling Ingredients:

  • 1 (3.5 oz) pkg instant Vanilla Pudding
  • 1 cup whipping cream
  • ½ cup Milk

Dough Ingredients:

  • ½ cup Butter
  • 1 cup Water
  • ¼ tsp Salt
  • 1 tsp Vanilla
  • 1 cup Flour
  • 1 tsp Sugar
  • 4 eggs

Topping Ideas:

  • Whipped Cream or Cool Whip
  • Can of Chocolate frosting
  • Strawberries

Instructions

In medium mixing bowl, mix together vanilla pudding mix, whipping cream, and milk. Cover and refrigerate to set.

Preheat oven to 425 degrees.

In a saucepan, bring water and butter to a rolling boil. Stir in vanilla, flour, salt, and sugar until mixture forms a ball. Transfer dough to a mixing bowl and add eggs one at a time. Mix quickly and well after each egg. Drop by tablespoonfuls onto greased baking sheet.

Bake for 20-25 minutes until golden brown. When golden brown, immediately poke hole in center with a toothpick. (This allows the center to cool without collapsing.)

When the shells are cool, either split them or fill them with the pudding mixture. Heat can of chocolate frosting in microwave for 15 seconds – stir till creamy. Add sliced strawberries and drizzle with chocolate. Top with whipped cream and enjoy!

Desserts |

Key Lime Cheesecake

Ingredients

  • 2 cups graham cracker crumbs, crushed (about 2 sleeves)

  • 1/2 cup butter, melted

  • 1/4 cup sugar

  • 3 (8 oz) pkgs cream cheese, softened

  • 1 cup sugar

  • 1 cup sour cream

  • 2 tsp vanilla

  • 4 eggs, room temperature

  • 3/4 cup fresh lime juice

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 375 degrees.

  2. In a medium mixing bowl, combine graham cracker crumbs, sugar, and melted butter.

  3. Pour crumbs into a 9" or 10" springform pan coated lightly with non-stick cooking spray.

  4. Using the bottom of the measuring cup, press the crumbs down into the base of the pan and up the sides of the pan.

  5. Place on cookie sheet and bake in oven for 7-8 minutes; no longer.

  6. Remove and set aside.

  7. In a large mixing bowl, beat cream cheese until smooth and creamy, no lumps.

  8. Add sugar and beat until smooth, scraping down the sides and bottom of the bowl frequently to remove all lumps.

  9. Add sour cream, eggs, and vanilla.

  10. When smooth, stir in lime juice.

  11. Pour over prepared crust and place in oven.

  12. Bake at 375 degrees for 15 minutes then lower temperature to 250 degrees and continue baking for 55 minutes.

  13. Turn oven off, open oven door, and leave in oven for 30 more minutes.

  14. Remove from oven and let cool on wire rack for 30 minutes.

  15. Cover and refrigerate for at least 6 hours before serving.

  16. Top with whipped cream and enjoy!

Lemon Blueberry Bars

Ingredients

CRUST:

  • 2 cups Flour
  • 1/2 cup Powdered Sugar
  • 1 cup Butter, softened

CREAM CHEESE FILLING:

  • 8 oz Cream Cheese, softened
  • 6 TB Sugar
  • 1 Egg
  • 1/2 tsp Vanilla

LEMON FILLING:

  • 4 Eggs, slightly beaten

  • 2 cups Sugar

  • 1/4 cup Flour

  • 1/2 tsp Baking Powder

  • 1/3 cup Lemon Juice

  • 1 pint Blueberries

Instructions

Preheat oven to 350 degrees. In a large bowl, combine 2 cups flour, 1/2 cup powdered sugar and 1 cup butter at low speed until crumbly. Press mixture evenly in bottom of ungreased 13x9 pan. Bake for 20-30 minutes or until light golden brown.

In a large bowl, mix 8 oz cream cheese, 6 TB sugar until creamy. Add 1 egg and 1/2 tsp vanilla and beat well. Set aside.

In another large bowl, combine 4 eggs, 2 cups sugar, 1/4 cup flour, and 1/2 tsp baking powder; blend well. Stir in lemon juice. Set aside.

Spread a layer of cream cheese filling over baked and cooled crust. (Crust does not have to be totally cool) Sprinkle blueberries over the cream cheese layer. Pour lemon filling over blueberries.

Bake for 25 minutes. Let cool about 10 minutes or longer. Cut into squares and sprinkle with powdered sugar just prior to serving.

Jesus said, 'Surely I am with you always, to the very end of the age.' Matthew 28:20

Desserts |

Lemon Brownies

Ingredients

For the brownies:

  • 3/4 cup flour
  • 1/4 tsp salt
  • 8 tbsp butter, room temperature
  • 3/4 cup sugar
  • 1 tsp lemon zest
  • 2 large eggs
  • 1 tbsp fresh lemon juice

Lemon Glaze:

  • 1/2 cup powdered sugar
  • 1 tbsp fresh lemon juice
  • 1 tsp lemon zest

Instructions

Preheat oven to 350 degrees.

Spray an 8x8 pan with nonstick cooking spray.

Whisk flour and salt together in a medium bowl; set aside.

In a mixer with paddle attachment, beat butter, sugar, and lemon zest at medium speed until light and fluffy, about 3 minutes, scraping down sides and bottom of bowl.

Add eggs one at a time, beating well after each addition.

Add lemon juice and mix to combine.

Reduce speed to low and add flour mixture, mixing until incorporated.

Spoon batter into prepared pan.

Bake until a toothpick comes out clean, 22-25 minutes.

Cool in pan on wire rack to room temperature.

To make lemon glaze:
Whisk together the powdered sugar, lemon juice, and zest. Spread over brownies and let harden, about 10 minutes.

Serve and enjoy!

Peach Crescent

Ingredients

  • 2 (8oz) pkgs Crescent Roll Dough
  • 2 pkgs frozen Peaches (OR 8 fresh Peaches, peeled, pitted and halved)
  • 1 1/2 cups Sugar
  • 1 cup Butter, melted
  • 1 tsp Ground Cinnamon
  • 1 cup Mountain Dew or Sprite

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 350 degrees.

  2. Coat a 13x9 baking dish with Pam; set aside.

  3. Unroll crescent roll dough and separate into triangles.

  4. Place 3-4 frozen peach slices (or 1 fresh peach halve) onto the wide end of the crescent and roll up.

  5. Place in prepared pan. Repeat with remaining crescent roll dough and peaches.

  6. In a small mixing bowl, combine sugar, melted butter, and cinnamon.

  7. Pour mixture over rolls.

  8. Pour Mountain Dew on top.

  9. Bake for 45 minutes or until golden brown and crisp.

  10. Serve with vanilla ice cream and enjoy!

Peanut Butter Brownies

Ingredients

  • 2 sticks Butter, softened

  • 1 3/4 cups Sugar

  • 1 cup Creamy Peanut Butter*

  • 2 large Eggs plus 1 Yolk

  • 2 tsp Vanilla

  • 2 cups All-purpose Flour

  • 1 1/2 cups Chocolate Chips

  • 1/2 tsp Salt

  • 1 tsp Butter to grease pan

*If you like nuts, you can use crunchy peanut butter

Instructions

Preheat oven to 350 degrees.

Line a 9x13 baking pan with foil. Grease with 1 tsp Butter.

In a large mixing bowl, beat together butter and sugar at medium high speed until mixture is light and fluffy, then add peanut butter and beat until blended well.

Beat in eggs, egg yolk, and vanilla.

At low speed, add flour and salt, mixing until just combined.

Stir in chocolate chips.

Spread batter into prepared baking pan (this should be a thick batter). Smooth the top until all batter is level in the pan.

Bake until brownies are golden, about 40 - 45 minutes.

Let cool completely before cutting.

Enjoy!

Pecan Pie Cheesecake Bars

Ingredients

  • 1 1/2 cups all-purpose flour
  • 1 1/2 cups brown sugar, divided
  • 1/2 cup butter, softened
  • 2 cups chopped pecans, divided
  • 2 (8 oz) packages cream cheese, softened
  • 1/2 cup sugar
  • 1/2 cup milk
  • 2 tsp vanilla
  • 1/2 cup light corn syrup
  • 1/3 cup butter, melted
  • 3 eggs, lightly beaten

Instructions

Preheat oven to 350 degrees.

In a medium mixing bowl, combine flour and 3/4 cup brown sugar. Using a pastry blender, cut in butter until mixture resembles coarse crumbs.

Stir in 1/2 cup pecans.

Press into bottom of a 13x9 baking pan.

Bake for 10 minutes. Let cool.

In a medium mixing bowl, combine cream cheese and sugar; beat at medium speed until smooth.

Add milk and vanilla, beating until well blended; pour over cooled crust.

Bake for 15 minutes. Cool for 10 minutes.

In a medium mixing bowl, combine 3/4 cup brown sugar, corn syrup, melted butter, and eggs.

Stir in 1 1/2 cups pecans.

Pour mixture over cream cheese mixture.

Bake for 40-45 minutes or until center is set.

Enjoy!

Pioneer Woman's Cobbler

Ingredients

  • 1 stick of butter plus a tablespoon to butter dish
  • 1 1/4 cup sugar
  • 1 cup self-rising flour
  • 1 cup milk
  • 2 cups blackberries (or any other kind of fruit) fresh or frozen

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 350 degrees.

  2. Melt stick of butter in a small bowl.

  3. In a large mixing bowl, combine 1 cup sugar and 1 cup self-rising flour.

  4. Whisk in milk.

  5. Mix well.

  6. Add melted butter and whisk it all together.

  7. Butter a baking dish - 9" square or round.

  8. Pour batter into the buttered baking dish.

  9. Rinse and dry the blackberries, if they are fresh. If using frozen fruit, keep frozen - do not thaw.

  10. Sprinkle blackberries over the top of the batter.

  11. Sprinkle the remaining 1/4 cup sugar on top of the berries.

  12. Place in oven and bake for 1 hour or until golden and bubbly, edges will look crispy.

  13. If desired, sprinkle an additional teaspoon of sugar over the cobbler 10 minutes before it's done.

  14. Serve with whipped cream, ice cream or all by itself and enjoy!

Pumpkin & Pecan Squares

Ingredients

  • 1 box yellow cake mix
  • 1/2 cup butter, melted
  • 1/2 cup chopped pecans
  • 4 large eggs
  • 8 oz pkg cream cheese, softened
  • 1 cup canned pumpkin
  • 1/2 tsp pumpkin pie spice

Top with whipped cream if desired

Pumpkin Funnel Cakes

Ingredients

  • 1 Quart Peanut Oil

  • 1 1/2 cup all-purpose Flour

  • 1/4 tsp Baking Powder

  • 1 tsp Baking Soda

  • 1 tsp Cinnamon

  • 1/4 tsp Salt

  • 1 tsp Pumpkin Pie Spice

  • 1 egg

  • 1/4 cup Brown Sugar

  • 1 cup Canned Pumpkin Puree

  • 1 cup Milk

  • Powdered Sugar and/or Cinnamon Sugar to sprinkle on top

Instructions

  1. Pour the oil into a deep skillet and heat to 375 degrees.

  2. In a medium mixing bowl, sift together the flour, baking powder, baking soda, salt, cinnamon, and pumpkin pie spice.

  3. In a large mixing bowl, whisk together the egg, brown sugar, pumpkin puree, and milk. Combine until well blended.

  4. Gradually add the flour mixture, using a whisk, stir till smooth.

  5. Pour 1/2 cup of batter into a funnel or zip lock bag with the corner cut out.

  6. Drizzle the batter into the hot oil using a circular motion, cross over the circle to connect the batter.

  7. Cook until golden, about 2-3 minutes.

  8. Turn the cake over to cook on the bottom side, about 2 minutes.

  9. Use a slotted spoon or tongs to remove from the oil and drain on paper towels.

  10. Sprinkle with cinnamon sugar and/or powdered sugar.

  11. Enjoy!

Ingredients

  • 4 cups fresh or frozen raspberries or blackberries

  • 2 tsp cornstarch
  • 3 tsp water
  • 1 tsp lemon juice
  • 1 cup quick-cooking oats
  • 1/2 cup flour

Sugar Lips Brownies (thank you Abbey)

Ingredients

  • 1 1/2 sticks salted butter
  • 1 1/2 cups sugar
  • 3/4 cup cocoa powder
  • 1/2 tsp baking powder
  • 1/2 tsp dry instant coffee, or a splash of your morning brew ;)
  • 2 tsp vanilla
  • 2 large eggs
  • 3/4 cup flour
  • 3 oz semi-sweet chocolate, or dark chocolate cut up (or chocolate chips in a pinch)

Instructions

  1. Spray an 8×8 pan with baking spray and set aside. Preheat the oven to 325 degrees. Place butter in a saucepan and slowly brown. After a few minutes, it browns quickly so watch carefully. Meanwhile, measure out sugar, cocoa, baking powder, coffee, and vanilla.

  2. On low heat, add sugar, cocoa, baking powder, coffee, and vanilla to the pan with the browned butter. Stir well to incorporate. Remove from heat and let cool for about 5-10 minutes. Chop your chocolate if you haven’t already while this cools.

  3. Add eggs to the chocolate mixture one at a time (making sure it is not too hot, or your eggs will cook). Add the flour and stir with a wooden spoon until incorporated. Add the chopped chocolate, give a quick couple of stirs, and quickly scrape the mixture into your sprayed baking dish (8×8). Give the batter a big swirl to create some nice texture on top of the baked brownies. Cook for about 25 minutes give or take…usually give. Until the pan is not jiggly when lightly moved.

  4. Cool brownies completely. Cut into squares with a plastic knife (this will help with sticking. Just use a sharp knife if you do not have any plastic around.) In a bowl, pour 1/2 cup of sugar. Dip the fresh cut edges of the brownies into the sugar to coat the edges. Enjoy with a glass of milk!

Turtle Shortbread Bars

Ingredients

Ingredients for crust:

  • 2 cups all-purpose flour
  • 1 cup brown sugar
  • ½ cup butter, softened
  • 1 ½ cups pecans, chopped

Ingredients for filling:

  • ¾ cup brown sugar
  • 1 cup butter, softened

Ingredients for topping:

  • 1 cup chocolate chips

Instructions

Preheat oven to 350 degrees.

In a mixing bowl, combine flour, 1 cup brown sugar, and ½ cup butter. Beat on low speed with electric mixer until mixture resembles coarse meal. Press mixture into ungreased 13x9 baking dish. Sprinkle with chopped pecans.

In a medium saucepan on medium heat, melt 1 cup butter; add ¾ cup brown sugar. Cook 3 minutes or until bubbles form around edges. Cook stirring constantly, 4 more minutes. Pour over pecans.

Bake for 22-25 minutes or until bubbly. Remove from oven and sprinkle chocolate chips evenly over top. Let stand until chips are softened, about 3 minutes. Swirl chips over surface to cover.

Cool completely. Chill 1 hour or until chocolate is set. Cut into bars. Enjoy!

Desserts |

Vanilla Glaze

Ingredients

  • 1 lb powdered sugar
  • 5 TB milk
  • 2 tsp vanilla

Instructions

In a large mixing bowl, stir together milk and vanilla. Sift powdered sugar and whisk into milk mixture. Add an additional TB of milk, if needed. Use immediately.

White Chocolate Popcorn

Ingredients

  • 2 bags microwave popcorn
  • 12 oz pkg white chocolate chips
  • 1 TB shortening

Instructions

  1. Pop popcorn.

  2. Melt chocolate chips in microwave - heat for 30 seconds and stir; repeat until all melted.

  3. Add shortening to chocolate and stir until melted.

  4. Pour over popcorn. Stir till all covered.

  5. Spread onto wax paper to cool and dry.

  6. Enjoy!

Sesame Dressing

Ingredients

  • 1/2 cup (2 oz) sesame seeds(toasted)
  • 1/4 cup olive oil
  • 1/3 cup soy sauce
  • 1/3 cup rice vinegar
  • 3 TB white sugar
  • 1/2 tsp ground ginger
  • 1/4 tsp black pepper

Instructions

  1. Heat a skillet over medium-high heat. Add sesame seeds, cook stirring frequently until lightly toasted. (watch because they burn quickly!) Remove from heat, and set aside.

  2. In a jar with a tight-fitting lid, combine olive oil, soy sauce, vinegar, sugar, sesame seeds, ginger, and pepper. Shake well.

Ready to serve and enjoy!

Sweet & Sour Salad Dressing

Ingredients

  • 1 cup sugar
  • 1 1/2 cups apple cider vinegar
  • 2 pkts Good Seasons Italian Dressing Mix
  • 2 ice cubes
  • 1/4 cup vegetable oil

Instructions

  1. Bring vinegar to a boil.
  2. Remove from heat.
  3. Add sugar and stir until dissolved.
  4. Add dressing mix, then ice.
  5. Stir until the ice melts, then add oil.
  6. Store in a covered container in the refrigerator.
  7. Shake well before serving.
  • This will last several weeks in the refrigerator.

Jellies, Sauces and Dips |

7-Layer Dip

Ingredients

  • 16 oz can refried beans
  • 1 tablespoon taco seasoning
  • 10 oz sour cream
  • 8 oz package cream cheese, softened
  • 1 bunch green onions, thinly sliced
  • 16 oz jar salsa
  • 2 medium tomatoes, chopped
  • 1/2 of a head iceberg lettuce, coarsely chopped
  • 2 cups cheddar cheese, shredded

Instructions

  1. In a medium bowl, combine the refried beans and taco seasoning. Mix well. Spread mixture evenly onto the bottom of a 9x13 pan.

  2. In another medium bowl, combine sour cream and cream cheese. Mix well. Spread mixture over beans in the pan.

  3. Top with green onions, then salsa, followed by tomatoes, lettuce, and cheese. Refrigerate until ready to serve. Serve with chips.

Black Bean Queso

Ingredients

  • 2 TB Butter
  • 1/2 Yellow Onion, chopped
  • 4 oz can Green Chilies
  • 1 can Rotel diced chilies and tomatoes
  • 1/2 tsp Salt
  • 1/2 tsp Garlic Powder
  • 1/2 tsp Black Pepper
  • 1/4 jalapeno, finely chopped
  • 1 can Black Beans, drained
  • 8 oz Shredded Cheddar Cheese
  • 4 oz Velveeta, cut into small cubes
  • 3 oz Cream Cheese, softened

Instructions

  2. Add onions, sauté until tender and clear.

  3. Add green chilies, Rotel, salt, pepper, and garlic powder.

  6. Serve with tortilla chips. Enjoy!

Bread Bowl Party Dip

Ingredients

  • 1 large round bread
  • 1 1/2 cup mayonnaise

  • 2 tsp dill weed
  • 2 Tbsp chopped parsley

Jellies, Sauces and Dips |

Chipotle Pimento Cheese

Ingredients

Chipotle Mayo:

  • 30 oz Mayo
  • 7 oz can chipotle peppers in adobo
  • 1/2 tsp cumin
  • 1 tsp lime zest
  • 2 tbsp lime juice

Chipotle Pimento Cheese:

  • 1 lb pepper jack cheese
  • 1 lb yellow cheddar cheese
  • 1 small red onion
  • 1 heaping tbsp diced pimento peppers
  • 1 tbsp dill
  • 1 tbsp parsley
  • 2 tsp garlic powder, or 2 cloves minced
  • 1 tsp salt, to taste
  • 6-8 oz chipotle mayo, about 1 cup

Instructions

Directions for Chipotle Mayo

  1. Zest your lime, and add it to a food processor with your chipotles. Add cumin, and lime juice. Blend until smooth.

  2. Add your mayo to the food processor and pulse until fully combined.

  3. Store in airtight jars for up to two weeks.

Directions for Chipotle Pimento Cheese

  1. Dice up your onion and throw it in a food processor. Add your garlic at this point, too if using cloves. Pulse until minced and uniform in size.

  2. Remove onions and put in a bowl and set aside.

  3. Run your cheese through the grating blade of your food processor or grate your cheese by hand (don't use the packaged grated cheese - it won't taste the same).

  4. Remove cheese and put in the same bowl with your onions.

  5. Add your pimentos, dill, and parsley at this time. Mix well with a large spoon.

  6. Start out adding 6 oz of chipotle mayo, adding more if needed. You want just enough to hold the cheese mixture together.

  7. Store in an airtight container for up to a week. Serve with pita chips.

"So that Christ may dwell in your hearts through faith—that you, being rooted and grounded in love, may have strength to comprehend with all the saints what is the breadth and length and height and depth, and to know the love of Christ that surpasses knowledge, that you may be filled with all the fullness of God." Ephesians 3:17-19

Jellies, Sauces and Dips |

Cranberry Pineapple Jelly

Ingredients

  • 4 cups fresh cranberries (1 bag has 3 cups)
  • 1 ¼ cup water
  • 9 cups sugar (yes! Nine!)
  • 1 cup walnuts, chopped
  • ½ cup golden raisins
  • 15 oz can crushed pineapple (drained)
  • 1 pkg. Certo

Instructions

  1. Make sure canning jars are clean and ready to use.

  2. In a large pot, add water and cranberries.

  3. Simmer on medium-low for about 8 minutes (until cranberries start to pop!).

  4. Add sugar, walnuts, raisins, and pineapple.

  5. Raise heat to medium-high, bring to a boil, and boil for 2 minutes, stirring occasionally.

  6. Add pkg. of Certo. Lower heat to low and cook and stir for 5 minutes.

  7. Remove from heat. Fill jars (this recipe makes 10 - 8oz jars); clean any spill from rim and sides of jar before placing lid on top.

  8. Once lid is on, turn jar over to seal (lid will pop when it’s sealed).

  9. Let set for 24 hours. After opening, keep refrigerated for up to 3 weeks.

“Rest in the Lord; wait patiently for Him to act. Don’t be envious of evil men who prosper.”
Psalm 37:7 TLB

Jellies, Sauces and Dips |

Elizabeth's Chicken Enchilada Dip (makes a great filling for Chicken Enchiladas)

Ingredients

  • 2 chicken breasts, cooked and shredded (cooking instructions below)
  • 1 (8 ounce) package cream cheese, softened
  • 1 (8 ounce) jar mayonnaise
  • 1 (8 ounce) package shredded Cheddar cheese
  • 1 (4 ounce) can diced green chili peppers
  • 1 jalapeno pepper, finely diced

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 350 degrees F (175 degrees C). Place chicken breast halves on a medium baking sheet.

  2. Bake in the preheated oven 20 minutes, or until no longer pink. Remove from heat, cool, and shred.

  3. Place shredded chicken in a medium bowl, and mix in cream cheese, mayonnaise, Cheddar cheese, green chili peppers, and jalapeno pepper. Transfer the chicken mixture to a medium baking dish.

  4. Bake uncovered in the preheated oven 30 minutes, or until the edges are golden brown.

  5. Serve with chips or flour tortillas. Enjoy!

**This makes a great filling for chicken enchiladas

“My salvation and honor depend on God; He is my mighty rock, my refuge.” Psalm 62:5

Fruit Salsa and Cinnamon Tortilla Chips

Ingredients

  • 2 Kiwi, peeled and diced

  • 2 Golden Delicious Apples, peeled, cored, and diced

  • 8 oz Raspberries, diced

  • 1 lb Strawberries, diced

  • 2 TB Sugar

  • 1 TB Brown Sugar

  • 3 TB Fruit preserves, any flavor

  • 10 Flour Tortillas

  • Butter flavored cooking spray

  • 2 Cups Cinnamon Sugar

Instructions

In a large bowl, thoroughly mix kiwi, apples, raspberries, strawberries, sugars, and fruit preserves. Cover and chill for at least 15 minutes.

Preheat oven to 350 degrees. Cut flour tortillas into wedges and arrange in a single layer on a large baking sheet. Coat with butter flavored cooking spray. Sprinkle with cinnamon sugar. Spray again with cooking spray. Bake for 8-10 minutes. Allow to cool at least 15 minutes. Serve with fruit salsa and ENJOY!

Salsa

Ingredients

  • 1 can Rotel
  • 1 can tomatoes
  • 1 onion
  • 1 jalapeño, cilantro to taste
  • 1-2 TB sugar
  • 1-2 TB chili powder
  • 1 can diced green chilis
  • salt and pepper to taste

Instructions

Chop up onion, jalapeño (leave in seeds if you like it hot!), and cilantro. Add to the tomato and Rotel mixture. Add green chilis. Mix well.

The longer it sets, the hotter it gets. Enjoy!

7-Layer Tortilla Pie

Ingredients

  • 2 (9oz) cans Spicy Bean Dip
  • 15 oz can Black Beans, rinsed and drained
  • 1 Tomato, diced
  • ¼ cup Salsa
  • 7 (8”) flour tortillas
  • 2 ½ cups Shredded Cheddar cheese

Instructions

Preheat oven to 400 degrees.
In a mixing bowl, combine black beans, tomato, and salsa.
Place 1 tortilla in a greased deep dish pie plate.
Spread ½ can of spicy bean dip on top of the tortilla, sprinkle ½ cup cheese on top.
Place 1 tortilla on top of the bean dip & cheese.
Spread 2/3 cup of black bean mixture on top of the tortilla, sprinkle with ½ cup of cheese.
Repeat layers.
Cover last tortilla with remaining bean dip and black bean mixture, cover with cheese.
Cover with foil and bake for 40 minutes.
Cut into wedges and serve with salsa and sour cream.

The Lord will fulfill his purpose for me; your love, O Lord, endures forever – do not abandon the works of your hands. Psalm 138:8

Main Course |

Asian Beef with Snow Peas

Ingredients

  • 6 TB soy sauce
  • 4 TB rice wine
  • 2 TB brown sugar
  • 1 tsp cornstarch
  • 2 TB vegetable oil
  • 1 TB ground ginger
  • 1 TB minced garlic
  • 1 lb round steak, cut into thin strips
  • 8 oz snow peas

Instructions

  1. In a small bowl, combine soy sauce, rice wine, brown sugar, and cornstarch. Set aside.

  2. Heat oil in a large skillet over medium-high heat.

  3. Add garlic and cook for 1 minute. Add steak and ginger. Cook for 3-5 minutes or until meat is cooked.

  4. Add the snow peas and cook for an additional 3 minutes.

  5. Add the soy sauce mixture, bring to a boil, stirring constantly.

  6. Lower heat and simmer until the sauce is thick and smooth.

  7. Serve immediately over rice or noodles – Enjoy!

"Investigate my life, O God, find out everything about me; cross-examine me and test me, get a clear picture of what I'm about; see for yourself whether I've done anything wrong - then guide me on the road to eternal life."

Main Course |

BBQ Ribs

Ingredients

  • 4 lbs Pork Ribs
  • 1 cup Brown Sugar
  • 1 tsp Hickory Smoke Salt (I make mine using a drop of liquid smoke and 1 tsp of Coarse Sea Salt)
  • 1 TB Paprika
  • 1 TB Garlic Powder
  • 1 Bottle BBQ Sauce (we like Sweet Baby Ray's)

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 300 degrees.

  2. Peel off the tough membrane that covers the bony side of the ribs (this is the key to good ribs – it's not hard once you get the hang of it. Google it if you have questions).

  3. In a small mixing bowl, combine the sugar and spices to make the rub.

  4. Apply rub to ribs on all sides.

  5. On a large baking sheet, place 2 sheets of foil shiny side out – make them longer than the ribs – you're going to make a foil tent.

  6. Place ribs onto foil, placing the meaty part down on the foil.

  7. Lay 2 more layers of foil on top of the ribs and crimp edges tightly to make a tent.

  8. Bake for 2 1/2 hours.

  9. Remove from oven.

  10. Have your charcoal grill ready.

  11. Coat the meaty side of ribs with BBQ sauce and grill till you get that nice charred look.

  12. While cooking, coat the top of the ribs. When the bottom is cooked, turn them over and char the other side. This should take about 15 minutes.

  13. Slice them up and enjoy!

Alternative method:

  • If you don't have a grill, the original recipe recommends doing the final step under the broiler in the oven.

  • Remove ribs from foil and place on a broiling pan or baking sheet, bony side up.

  • Brush on sauce.

  • Broil on high for 1-2 minutes until sauce is cooked on and bubbly.

  • Turn ribs over and repeat.

We haven't tried them this way, but I don't know why it wouldn't work. My husband just thinks there has to be a grilling step in making BBQ ribs :-)

Hope you enjoy!

Main Course |

Bacon-Wrapped Pork Medallion

Ingredients

  • 8 slices Bacon
  • 2 lbs Pork Tenderloin
  • Lemon Pepper
  • Garlic Salt
  • 2 TB Butter
  • 2 TB Olive Oil

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 400 degrees.

  2. Slice the tenderloin to create medallions, about 2 inches thick.

  3. Wrap the medallions with the bacon strips securing with toothpicks.

  4. Season both sides of the medallions with lemon pepper and garlic salt.

  5. In a large skillet over medium-high heat, melt butter and olive oil.

  6. Cook each medallion for 4 minutes on each side.

  7. Spray a baking dish with cooking spray.

  8. Place medallions in baking dish and bake 17-20 minutes. The temperature of the pork should be 160 degrees.

  9. Remove from oven and serve. Enjoy!

Main Course |

Baked Linguine

Ingredients

  • 1 lb ground beef
  • 1 tsp minced garlic
  • 28 oz can crushed tomatoes
  • 8 oz can tomato sauce
  • 6 oz can tomato paste
  • 2 tsp sugar
  • 1/2 tsp salt
  • 8 oz linguine
  • 16 oz container sour cream
  • 8 oz package cream cheese
  • 1 bunch green onions, chopped
  • 1 1/2 cups shredded mozzarella cheese
  • Cooking spray

Instructions

  1. Cook beef and garlic in a Dutch oven, stirring until beef crumbles and is no longer pink. Drain beef mixture; pat dry with paper towels, place beef back in pan.

  2. Stir in tomatoes, tomato sauce, tomato paste, sugar, and salt; simmer for 30 minutes. Set meat sauce aside.

  3. Cook pasta according to package directions; drain.

  4. Place pasta in a 13 x 9 baking dish coated with cooking spray.

  5. Stir together sour cream, cream cheese, and green onions. Spread over linguine. Top with meat sauce.

  6. Bake at 350 degrees for 20 – 25 minutes or until thoroughly heated. Sprinkle with mozzarella cheese and bake 5 minutes more or until cheese is melted. Let stand 5 minutes before serving.

To make this low-fat, you can use nonfat sour cream and fat-free cream cheese. To cut sodium, omit salt and use reduced sodium canned tomato products.

… Praise and exalt and glorify the King of heaven, because everything he does is right and all his ways are just... Daniel 4:37

Main Course |

Baked Mexican Pie

Ingredients

  • 1 lb Ground Beef
  • 1 pkg Taco Seasoning
  • 2/3 cup Beef Broth
  • 2 TB Butter
  • 1 Yellow Onion, chopped
  • 2 TB chopped Garlic
  • 1 bunch Green Onions, chopped
  • 4.5 oz can chopped Green Chilies
  • 1 can Cream of Celery Soup
  • 1 can Enchilada Sauce
  • 1 can Refried Beans w/ green chilies
  • 8- 12" Flour Tortillas or 20 - Corn Tortillas
  • 3 cups shredded Pepper Jack Cheese

Garnish: Sour Cream and Salsa

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 350 degrees.

  2. In a large skillet, cook ground beef with taco seasoning and beef broth over medium heat until meat is done.

  3. While beef is cooking, chop your green onions and yellow onion and set aside.

  4. Remove beef onto plate and set aside.

  5. In the same skillet, melt butter. Add yellow onion, green onion and garlic. Cook until tender. Add green chilies, soup, and cooked beef, stir until mixed well.

  6. Remove from heat.

  7. In a medium mixing bowl, combine enchilada sauce and refried beans, whisking well.

  8. Cut flour tortillas in half. If using corn tortillas, no need to cut.

  9. In a greased 9x12 baking dish, place 1 layer of tortillas.

  10. Next layer meat mixture. Sprinkle cheese on top.

  11. Add another layer of tortillas.

  12. Next layer bean mixture. Sprinkle cheese on top.

  13. Repeat steps once more ending with tortillas.

  14. Top with shredded cheese.

  15. Try to cover entire tortillas with mixture so they don't dry out.

  16. Bake for 30 minutes or until cheese is browned.

  17. Garnish with sour cream and salsa. Enjoy!

"He who dwells in the shelter of the Most High will rest in the shadow of the Almighty. I will say unto the Lord, 'He is my refuge and my fortress, my God, in whom I trust.' Psalm 91:1-2

Main Course |

Baked Spaghetti Squash

Ingredients

  • 1 lb ground beef or ground turkey

  • Salt and pepper to season meat with

  • 1 spaghetti squash

  • 1 egg

  • 1 cup marinara sauce (I like spicy tomato and basil)

  • 1 can diced tomatoes with basil, garlic, and oregano (drained)

  • 1 cup ricotta cheese

  • 8 oz mozzarella cheese, shredded

  • 1 tsp pepper

  • 1/2 tsp salt

Instructions

Preheat oven to 350 degrees.

Poke spaghetti squash 10 times with an ice pick and place in microwave for 10-12 minutes.

You'll know it's done when the skin starts to buckle.

While squash is cooking, start browning your meat in a skillet. Season with salt and pepper.

Cook until brown and crumbly. Set aside to cool.

Remove squash from the oven and split open. (This is harder than you think!) Be careful - it's hot.

Let cool. After it's cooled, take a fork and start scraping the insides of the squash. It will start to come off and look like spaghetti (that's why it's called spaghetti squash!)

In a large mixing bowl, combine sauce, diced tomatoes, egg, ricotta cheese, half of the mozzarella cheese (save some to place on top), pepper, salt, and squash.

Spray small ramekins or pie plates with PAM. Place a scoop of the squash mixture in the dish.

Next place a layer of the cooked meat.

Sprinkle mozzarella on top.

Place containers on a cookie sheet and bake for 35-45 minutes or until cheese is melted and bubbly.

Remove from oven and let cool for 5 minutes before serving.

Enjoy!

Main Course |

Baked Tacos

Ingredients

  • 2 lbs ground beef
  • 4 teaspoons dried minced onion
  • 3 teaspoons chili powder
  • 1 teaspoon cornstarch
  • 1 teaspoon garlic powder
  • 1 teaspoon ground cumin
  • ½ teaspoon dried oregano
  • 1/8 teaspoon cayenne pepper
  • 1 (8 oz) can tomato sauce
  • 1 (16 oz) can refried beans or drained black beans
  • 1 (15 oz) can corn, drained
  • 2 cups shredded Colby-jack cheese
  • 18-20 hard taco shells

Optional toppings:

  • Lettuce (shredded)
  • Tomato (diced)
  • Salsa
  • Guacamole

Instructions

  1. Preheat the oven to 400 degrees.

  2. In a small bowl combine dried minced onion, chili powder, cornstarch, garlic powder, cumin, oregano, and cayenne pepper. Mix well and set aside.

  3. In a large skillet, brown ground beef over medium-high heat. Drain off any excess liquid. Add beans, corn, tomato sauce, and prepared taco seasoning. Mix well.

  4. Sprinkle a little cheese into the bottom of taco shells. Spoon the taco meat mixture into the taco shells and place into a 9 x 13 inch baking dish, standing up.

  5. Sprinkle remaining cheese over the top of the taco meat in each shell. Place into the oven and bake at 400 degrees for 10-12 minutes or until the cheese has melted and the tacos are heated through.

  6. Remove from the oven and top with any optional condiments for serving.

Enjoy!

Ephesians 1:11-12 The Message

Baked Ziti

Ingredients

  • 1 lb pkg ziti pasta
  • 1 onion, chopped
  • 1 lb lean ground beef
  • 2 (26 oz) jar spaghetti sauce
  • 6 oz provolone cheese, sliced
  • 8 oz sour cream
  • 6 oz mozzarella cheese, shredded
  • 2 tbsp grated Parmesan cheese

Instructions

  1. Bring a large pot of water to a boil. Add ziti pasta, and cook about 8 minutes. Drain.

  2. In a large skillet, brown onion and ground beef over medium heat. Add spaghetti sauce, and simmer 15 minutes.

  3. Preheat oven to 350 degrees. Grease a 9x13 baking dish.

  4. Layer as follows: 1/2 of the ziti pasta, provolone cheese, sour cream, 1/2 the sauce mixture, remaining ziti pasta, mozzarella cheese, and remaining sauce mixture.

  5. Bake for 30 minutes or until cheeses are melted.

  6. Remove from oven and top with grated Parmesan cheese.

  7. Serve with hot bread and enjoy!

Main Course |

Balsamic Chicken with Pesto Gravy

Ingredients

  • 4-6 Chicken Breasts
  • 5 TB Balsamic Vinegar
  • 3 TB Extra-Virgin Olive Oil
  • Salt and Pepper, to taste
  • 2 TB Butter
  • 1 TB Flour
  • 1 ½ cups Chicken Broth
  • 1/3 cup Whipping Cream
  • ¼ cup Pesto
  • Grated Parmesan Cheese

Instructions

  1. Heat a large skillet over medium-high to high heat. Drizzle pan with olive oil.
  2. Place chicken breasts between 2 pieces of plastic wrap. Using the bottom of a small pan, flatten chicken breasts to make them thinner. Unwrap plastic wrap and drizzle the top side of each chicken breast with balsamic vinegar and season with salt and pepper.
  3. Place chicken in hot pan, seasoned side down, and cook on each side for about 2 minutes.
  4. Remove chicken from pan and set aside on a plate.
  5. Add 2 TB butter to the pan and melt. Add flour and cook for about 1 minute. Whisk in chicken stock and cream, cook on medium heat until it thickens. (If needed, add 1 TB cornstarch to 2 TB chicken broth and add to gravy mixture to thicken.)
  6. Remove from heat, add the pesto and combine well.
  7. Pour pesto gravy over chicken, top with Parmesan cheese, and serve with salad. Enjoy!

I Corinthians 6:12

Beef Stroganoff

Ingredients

  • dash of paprika

  • Seasoned Salt and Pepper to taste

  • 2 TB Worcestershire Sauce

  • 1/2 cup Tomato Juice

  • 1 can Cream of Mushroom Soup

  • 8 oz Sour Cream

  • Rice or Egg Noodles

Instructions

Brown the sirloin strips in the butter, along with the onion and mushrooms. Add the seasonings, Worcestershire Sauce and Tomato juice. Cover and simmer about 20 minutes, stirring occasionally. Stir in the soup and sour cream. Heat gently.

Beefy Baked Tortellini

Ingredients

  • 1 lb Ground Beef
  • 1/2 Yellow Onion, diced
  • 20 oz Pkg Frozen Cheese Tortellini, no need to thaw or cook
  • 14 oz jar Spaghetti Sauce, your favorite kind
  • 14.5 oz can Diced Tomatoes, drained
  • 1 cup shredded Mozzarella Cheese
  • 1 cup shredded Monterey Jack Cheese
  • Freshly Grated Parmesan Cheese

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 450 degrees.
  2. In a large skillet over medium-high heat, brown hamburger meat and diced yellow onion.
  3. Season with salt and pepper while cooking.
  4. Drain grease, then stir in spaghetti sauce and tomatoes.
  5. Using Pam, grease an 11x7 baking dish.
  6. Spread 1/3 of sauce mixture on bottom of pan.
  7. Arrange 1/2 of the tortellini on top of the sauce.
  8. Sprinkle 1/2 of the mozzarella and Monterey jack cheese over ravioli.
  9. Repeat layers, ending with the sauce on top.
  10. Cover with foil.
  11. Bake for 30 minutes.
  12. Sprinkle parmesan cheese on top before serving.
  13. Enjoy!
