Black Bean Corn Quesadillas

Ingredients

  • 2 tsp Olive Oil
  • ½ cup Rotel
  • 3 TB chopped Onion
  • ¼ tsp Red Pepper Flakes
  • 15 oz can Black Beans, drained and rinsed
  • 10 oz can Mexi-corn, drained
  • 1 TB Brown Sugar
  • ¼ tsp Cumin
  • *optional - 1 cooked chicken breast, shredded
  • 8 flour Tortillas
  • 1 ½ cups Monterey Jack Cheese, shredded

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 350 degrees.

  2. Heat oil in a large saucepan over medium heat.

  3. Stir in onion, and cook until softened, about 2 minutes.

  4. Stir in beans and corn.

  5. Add sugar, Rotel, cumin, and red pepper flakes; mix well. (Add chicken at this time if desired)

  6. Cook until heated through; about 3 minutes.

  7. (This can be made ahead and refrigerated until ready to use – will keep for 2 days)

  8. Spray tortilla with cooking spray.

  9. Sprinkle evenly with cheese then top with bean mixture.

  10. Top with another tortilla.

  11. Assemble all quesadillas and place on a cookie sheet.

  12. Bake for 10 minutes in the oven.

  13. Serve with sour cream, salsa or guacamole and enjoy!

Black Bean and Rice Pita

Ingredients

  • 1 cup Brown Minute Rice
  • 2 cups Water
  • 15 oz can Black Beans, rinsed and drained
  • 15 oz can Corn, rinsed and drained
  • 3 Green Onions, chopped
  • 1 tsp Salt
  • 1/4 tsp Pepper
  • 1/2 cup Salsa
  • 3 Pita Bread rounds, halved

Serve with:

  • Romaine Lettuce
  • Yogurt or Sour Cream
  • Fresh Salsa (ingredients below)
    • 15 oz can chopped or crushed tomatoes
    • 1 TB fresh lime juice
    • 1/2 small red onion, chopped finely
    • 2 tsp minced garlic
    • 4 oz can diced green chilis
    • 1/2 tsp salt
    • 1/2 tsp crushed red pepper

Instructions

  1. Bring 2 cups water to a boil in a small saucepan; stir in rice. Cover and let set 10 minutes.

  2. While rice is setting, make salsa. In a small mixing bowl, combine tomatoes, lime juice, onion, garlic, chilis, salt, and red pepper. Cover and chill for at least 1 hour.

  3. After rice is ready, in a large mixing bowl combine rice, black beans, chilis, corn, green onions, salt, pepper, and 1/2 cup salsa. Gently toss. Cover and chill if desired.

  4. Cut pita rounds in half. Line with lettuce. Spoon rice mixture into each half; top with salsa and sour cream or yogurt. Enjoy!

'For God did not give us the spirit of timidity, but a spirit of power, of love and of self-discipline.'
II Timothy 1:7

Black Bean/Chicken Chilaquiles

Ingredients

  • 1 small onion, chopped
  • 4 tsps or 4 cloves of garlic, minced
  • 2 chicken breasts
  • Garlic salt and black pepper to season chicken breasts
  • 15 oz can black beans, rinsed and drained
  • 1 cup chicken broth
  • 8 oz jar Pace Picante Sauce
  • 15 corn tortillas, cut into 1-inch strips
  • 1 cup shredded queso blanco, I use Velveeta brand
  • Pam cooking spray to grease baking dish

Instructions

Preheat oven to 450°.
Flatten and season chicken breasts with garlic salt and black pepper.
Heat a large non-stick skillet over medium-high heat.
Add chicken breasts to pan and cook 5-7 minutes on each side, or until no pink in center.
Remove from heat and shred.

While chicken is cooking, chop onion and garlic.
Drain and rinse black beans.

After removing chicken from pan, add onion and sauté for 5 minutes or until lightly browned.
Add garlic; sauté 1 minute. Be careful not to burn garlic.
Add shredded chicken; cook 30 seconds.
Transfer mixture to a medium bowl;
Stir in black beans.
Add broth and salsa to pan; bring to a boil. Reduce heat, and simmer 5 minutes, stirring occasionally.

While broth and salsa are cooking, shred cheese and set aside.

Place half of tortilla strips in bottom of an 11 x 8-inch baking dish coated with Pam.
Layer half of chicken mixture over tortillas; top with remaining tortillas and chicken mixture.
Pour broth mixture evenly over chicken mixture. Sprinkle with cheese.
Bake at 450° for 10 minutes or until tortillas are lightly browned and cheese is melted.

Store leftovers in individual containers. To reheat, drizzle with 1 tablespoon water and microwave just until thoroughly heated.

Psalm 143:7-9

Bubble Pizza

Ingredients

  • 1 lb ground beef or 1 lb sausage
  • 14 oz can pizza sauce
  • 2 – 12 oz cans buttermilk biscuits
  • 1 ½ cups mozzarella cheese, shredded
  • 1 cup cheddar cheese, shredded

Optional Toppings - Be creative!

  • Pepperoni
  • Onions
  • Black olives
  • Mushrooms
  • Cooked chicken
  • Pineapple
  • Canadian bacon

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 400 degrees.

  2. Grease a 9x13 baking sheet.

  3. In a large skillet, cook ground beef over medium heat until evenly brown. Drain excess fat.

  4. Stir in pizza sauce. Remove from heat and set aside.

  5. Cut biscuits into quarters and place in the bottom of the baking dish.

  6. Spread meat mixture evenly over the biscuits.

  7. Sprinkle top with onions, olives, and mushrooms (or whatever topping you like).

  8. Bake uncovered in preheated oven for 20-25 minutes.

  9. Sprinkle top with mozzarella and cheddar cheese.

  10. Top with pepperoni or whatever other toppings you like.

  11. Bake an additional 5-10 minutes or until cheese is melted.

  12. Let stand 10 minutes before serving. Enjoy!

Cajun Chicken Pasta

Ingredients

  • 8 oz Linguine Pasta
  • 3 Boneless Chicken Breasts, cut into strips
  • 1 TB Cajun Seasoning
  • 2 tsp Garlic Salt
  • 2 tsp Lemon Pepper
  • 2 TB olive oil
  • 1 Red Bell pepper, cut into slices
  • 2 heads Broccoli, cut into bite-size pieces
  • 2 cups Whipping Cream
  • ½ tsp dried Basil
  • ½ tsp Lemon Pepper
  • ½ tsp Salt
  • ½ tsp Garlic Powder
  • ½ tsp Black Pepper
  • Parmesan Cheese

Instructions

  1. Cook pasta according to package directions. Drain and set aside.

  2. In a ziplock baggie, combine 1 TB Cajun seasoning, 2 tsp garlic salt, and 2 tsp lemon pepper. Add chicken strips and shake to coat.

  3. In a large skillet over medium-high heat, heat olive oil. Add coated chicken strips and cook 7-8 minutes or until no longer pink.

  4. Add red bell pepper and broccoli. Saute and stir for 3-4 minutes.

  5. Add cream, basil, lemon pepper, salt, garlic powder, and black pepper. Cook about 5 minutes or until heated through.

  6. Add pasta, toss, and cook an additional 5 minutes.

  7. Grate Parmesan cheese on top of servings and enjoy!

Cheesesteak Sandwiches

Ingredients

  • 1 Tbsp Olive Oil
  • 1 Pound Top Round Steak, Trimmed and thinly sliced (ask your butcher to do this)
  • 2 Tbsp Worcestershire sauce, divided
  • 1 Yellow Onion, thinly sliced
  • 1 Green Bell Pepper, thinly sliced
  • 1 1/2 tsp Salt
  • 1 1/2 tsp Pepper
  • 1 tsp Garlic Powder
  • 6 slices Provolone Cheese, torn into small pieces
  • 4 Hoagies

Instructions

Preheat oven to 225 degrees.
Wrap hoagies in foil and warm while preparing meat.
In a large skillet, heat oil over medium-high heat.
Add steak and 1 Tbsp Worcestershire Sauce, salt, pepper, and garlic powder, cook for 5 minutes or until steak is browned.
Remove steak from pan, set aside in a warm oven.
Add onion, bell pepper, and remaining 1 Tbsp Worcestershire Sauce to skillet.
Cook for 8 minutes or until onion slices are translucent.
Return cooked steak to skillet with onion mixture.
Reduce heat to low, add provolone cheese.
Cook until cheese melts, stirring constantly.
Divide steak mixture among hoagies and serve warm.
Enjoy!

Chicken & Asparagus Roulade

Ingredients

  • 6 Chicken Breasts
  • 6 sandwich slices of Swiss Cheese
  • 1 bunch of Asparagus
  • Salt
  • Pepper
  • Lemon pepper
  • Garlic salt
  • 1 TB Cooking Oil

Instructions

Preheat oven to 350 degrees.

Snap off woody ends of asparagus. In a large skillet, place 1-2 cups of water. Add 1/4 tsp salt and asparagus. Place lid on skillet and simmer until asparagus turns bright green and tender. Remove and rinse with cold water. Set aside for later use.

Place chicken breasts between sheets of plastic wrap.

Pound chicken breast to flatten into equal sizes and thickness.

Uncover and season with salt, pepper, garlic salt, and lemon pepper.

Place one slice of cheese in the center of the chicken breast.

Place 5 slices of steamed asparagus in the center of the chicken breast.

Tightly roll up; secure with a toothpick.

In your large skillet heat 1 TB cooking oil.

Add roulades and brown on all sides. This takes about 5-8 minutes.

Remove from skillet and place on a baking sheet.

Bake for 20-30 minutes until chicken is cooked through and cheese is melted.

Serve with rice or baked potato and enjoy!

These can be reheated for the next day - either place in microwave for 2-3 minutes on high or reheat in a 350-degree oven for about 10 minutes.

Chicken & Broccoli Supreme

Ingredients

  • 1 lb. fresh broccoli, break in pieces and steam for 2 minutes
  • 3 cups cooked chicken breasts, break up in small pieces
    • You can use a rotisserie chicken for a shortcut
  • 3 cups grated cheddar cheese, divided
  • 2 tubes Ritz crackers
  • 1 stick melted butter
  • 1 tablespoon poppy seeds

SAUCE:

  • 1/3 cup butter, melted
  • 1/4 cup cornstarch, dissolved in 1/2 cup cold water
  • 1/3 cup chicken broth
  • 1/4 tsp salt
  • 1/4 tsp pepper
  • 2 cups milk
  • 1 1/2 cups of the above cheddar cheese

Instructions

  1. In a greased 13×9 pan, layer the broccoli and chicken, then set aside.

  2. In a saucepan over medium heat, combine the melted butter, cornstarch dissolved in water, chicken broth, seasonings, and milk. Stir well and continue stirring until the sauce has thickened. Turn heat down to low and add 1 1/2 cups grated cheddar cheese. Stir until melted. Pour over the chicken and broccoli. Top with 1 1/2 cups grated cheddar cheese.

  3. Melt the butter and add the poppy seeds, stirring well. Crush Ritz crackers in a large zip-lock bag with a rolling pin. Don’t crush too small. Add crumbs to the melted butter. Sprinkle crumbs over the top of the grated cheese.

  4. Bake uncovered at 350 degrees for about 30 minutes, or until hot & bubbly.

  5. Eat and enjoy!

Main Course |

Chicken Caesar Salad Wrap

Ingredients

  • Juice of 1 lemon

  • 1/4 cup finely grated Parmesan cheese

  • 3 tablespoons Greek yogurt

  • 3 tablespoons extra virgin light olive oil

  • 1 teaspoon garlic powder

  • 1/2 teaspoon salt

  • 1 teaspoon black pepper

  • 1 large head Romaine lettuce

  • 4 chicken breasts

  • Lemon pepper and garlic salt to season

  • 8 wrap tortillas

Instructions

Prepare Caesar dressing first so the flavors can mix together.

In a small canning jar, combine lemon juice, Parmesan cheese, yogurt, olive oil, garlic powder, salt, and pepper.

Place lid on jar and shake vigorously until blended well.

Refrigerate until ready to serve.

Rinse Romaine lettuce.

Tear or cut into pieces.

Dry with paper towels or place in salad spinner to remove excess water.

Place in ziplock bag until ready to use.

Clean chicken (I like to remove extra skin and rinse in white vinegar).

Place in large ziplock bag and seal.

Using a meat tenderizer or a small fry pan, pound chicken to flatten until even in thickness.

Heat a large griddle (sprayed with cooking spray) or a large nonstick skillet to medium to medium-high heat.

Remove chicken from ziplock and season both sides with garlic salt and lemon pepper.

Place chicken on hot skillet and cook on one side 5-7 minutes, till golden.

Turn over and cook on the other side, about 5 minutes.

If you're unsure if it's done, cut the thickest part of the chicken to make sure there is no pink in the center.

Once done, remove from heat and let rest about 5 minutes.

Place on cutting board and slice into thin slices.

In a large bowl, combine romaine lettuce and Caesar dressing. Toss to coat well.

Place 1 wrap tortilla on the counter, place Caesar salad onto tortilla.

Top with sliced chicken.

Fold up bottom half and then roll.

Wrap in foil and refrigerate until ready to eat or serve immediately and enjoy.

Can be refrigerated for 2 days.

Enjoy!

Chicken Calzones

Ingredients

  • 2 cans pizza dough
  • 8 tsp. pesto
  • 16 oz mozzarella cheese, shredded
  • 2 tbsp butter
  • 2 tbsp olive oil
  • ½ cup chopped onion
  • 3 garlic cloves, minced
  • 1 rotisserie chicken, shredded
  • 1 tbsp garlic salt
  • 1 tbsp pepper
  • 2 cups mushrooms, sliced (optional)
  • 3 cups baby spinach, stems removed
  • ½ cup ricotta cheese
  • 1 cup Parmesan cheese, grated
  • Marinara sauce, optional

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 350 degrees.

  2. Line 2 baking sheets with parchment paper.

  3. On a clean flat surface, unroll pizza dough; cut in half and half again making 4 squares. Spread 1 tsp. of pesto evenly over half of each dough piece, leaving 1-inch border around sides of dough.

  4. Place dough on prepared baking sheet.

  5. Place 1/8 cup shredded mozzarella cheese over pesto on each dough piece.

  6. In a large skillet, heat butter and olive oil over medium heat.

  7. Add onion and garlic and cook for 2 minutes.

  8. Add chicken and season with garlic salt and pepper and cook for 4 minutes.

  9. Add mushrooms and cook for 4 minutes.

  10. Add spinach and cook for 2 minutes.

  11. Remove from heat; stir in ricotta cheese and Parmesan cheese.

  12. Spoon chicken mixture over mozzarella; place another 1/8 cup mozzarella cheese over chicken mixture.

  13. Fold dough over filling, crimping edges to seal.

  14. Bake for 20 – 25 minutes or until lightly browned.

  15. Cool for 5 minutes before serving.

  16. Serve with marinara sauce if desired.

  17. Enjoy!

Chicken Cannelloni

Ingredients

  • 1 pkg Cannelloni Shells
  • 2 (8 oz) containers Chive and Onion Cream Cheese
  • 1 (10 oz) pkg frozen Spinach, thawed and drained
  • 1 cup Shredded Mozzarella Cheese

  • 1 jar Tomato Basil Sauce

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 350 degrees.
  2. Cook Cannelloni Shells according to package directions.
  3. Grease 2 - 9x13 baking dishes.
  4. In a large mixing bowl, shred or cut chicken into bite-size pieces.
  5. Combine chicken, cream cheese, spinach, mozzarella, breadcrumbs, and pepper.
  6. Slice cannelloni shells down the middle and spoon mixture into each shell.
  7. Place cut side down into baking dish.
  8. Pour sauce over shells.
  9. Bake covered for 25-30 minutes.
  10. Serve and Enjoy!

Chicken Cordon Bleu

Ingredients

  • 6 skinless, boneless chicken breast halves
  • 6 slices Swiss cheese
  • 6 slices ham
  • 3 tablespoons all-purpose flour
  • 1 teaspoon paprika
  • 6 tablespoons butter
  • 1/2 cup dry white wine
  • 1 teaspoon chicken bouillon granules
  • 1 tablespoon cornstarch
  • 1 cup heavy whipping cream

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 350 degrees.

  2. Place chicken breasts between 2 pieces of freezer paper and pound till flat and even thickness.

  3. Place a slice of cheese and a slice of ham on each chicken breast.

  4. Fold the edges of the chicken over the filling and secure with toothpicks.

  5. In a small mixing bowl, combine the flour and paprika.

  6. Coat the chicken breasts with the flour mixture.

  7. In a large skillet over medium high heat, melt the butter.

  8. Place the chicken breasts in the melted butter and brown on all sides.

  9. Remove from skillet and place chicken breasts in a greased baking dish.

  10. Cover baking dish with foil and bake for 30 minutes.

  11. While chicken is baking add the wine and bouillon to the skillet.

  12. In a small mixing bowl, combine the cornstarch and the whipping cream.

  13. Whisk the mixture slowly into the skillet.

  14. Cook, stirring until thickened.

  15. Serve over baked chicken with egg noodles.

Enjoy!

Chicken Enchiladas and Sour Cream Sauce

Ingredients

  • 4 Cooked chicken breasts, shredded
  • 2 cups Pepper Jack cheese, shredded
  • 1 can Cream of Chicken Soup
  • 8 oz Sour Cream
  • 1/2 can chopped chilies
  • 1/4 onion, chopped
  • 16 Flour Tortillas

Sour Cream Sauce:

  • 1/4 cup Butter
  • 3 tbsp Flour
  • 1-2 cans Chicken Broth
  • 1/2 can chopped chilies
  • 8 oz Sour Cream
  • 1 cup Colby/Jack cheese, shredded

Instructions

  1. Spray two 9x13 baking dishes with PAM.

  2. In a large mixing bowl, mix together chicken, cheese, soup, sour cream, chilies, and onion.

  3. In a skillet on medium heat, heat tortillas till warm (about 30-45 seconds).

  4. Spoon 3-4 tablespoons of chicken mixture into a warm tortilla. Roll up and place seam side down into baking dish. Finish filling all tortillas.

  5. In a saucepan, melt butter. Add flour and blend well. Add 1 can of chicken broth and cook until thick. Add additional chicken broth if too thick. Stir in sour cream and chilies. Cook until heated. (Do Not Boil)

  6. Pour over tortillas. *Top with colby/jack cheese if desired.

  7. Bake at 350 degrees, uncovered, for 30 minutes or until bubbly. ENJOY!

Main Course |

Chicken Parmesan

Ingredients

  • 4 skinless boneless chicken breasts
  • 3/4 cup Italian-seasoned breadcrumbs
  • 1/2 tsp salt
  • 1/4 tsp pepper
  • 1/2 tsp Italian seasoning
  • 1/2 cup grated parmesan cheese
  • 1/4 cup vegetable oil
  • 1 (14 oz) jar meatless spaghetti sauce
  • 1/2 cup shredded parmesan cheese
  • 1 cup shredded mozzarella cheese
  • 1 (12oz) pkg spaghetti
  • 1/4 cup chopped parsley

Instructions

  1. Clean chicken breasts.

  2. Heat oil over medium heat in skillet.

  3. Combine breadcrumbs, salt, pepper, Italian seasoning, and 1/2 cup grated parmesan cheese.

  4. Coat chicken in mixture and brown in skillet, turning once.

  5. Remove from pan and place in ungreased baking dish.

  6. Spoon spaghetti sauce over chicken and bake at 375 degrees for 15 minutes.

  7. Remove from oven and sprinkle chicken breasts with shredded parmesan cheese and shredded mozzarella cheese.

  8. Bake an additional 5 minutes or until cheese is melted.

  9. While chicken is baking, cook spaghetti as directed on package.

  10. Serve chicken over spaghetti. Add additional sauce, if desired.

  11. Serve with french bread and a salad.

Serves 4 people.

'But the man who loves God is known by God.' I Corinthians 8:3

Main Course |

Chicken Spaghetti

Ingredients

  • 4 skinless boneless chicken breasts, cooked & chopped
  • 1 (8oz) pkg spaghetti
  • 1/4 cup butter
  • 1/2 large yellow onion, chopped
  • 1 (8oz) pkg Velveeta, cubed
  • 1 can Rotel
  • 1 can cream of chicken soup

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 350 degrees. Cook spaghetti according to package directions; drain. Place spaghetti in a 13 x 9 baking dish greased with Pam.

  2. Melt butter in a large skillet over medium heat; add onion, and sauté 3 – 5 minutes or until tender. (Onions will turn clear) Add cheese and Rotel, stirring until cheese melts. Stir in chicken and soup, blending well. Pour mixture over spaghetti in baking dish.

  3. Bake at 350 degrees for 25 minutes or until bubbly.

  4. Serve with French bread and a salad to complete the meal. Enjoy!

'Those who fear the Lord are secure; He will be a place of refuge for their children.'
Proverbs 14:26

Main Course |

Chicken Spaghetti with Mushrooms

Ingredients

  • 6 chicken breasts, cooked and shredded
  • 4 TB butter
  • 1/2 onion, diced
  • 2 garlic cloves, minced
  • 1/2 cup minced celery
  • 6-8 sliced fresh mushrooms
  • 2 cans cream of mushroom soup
  • 1 can cream of chicken soup
  • 1 tsp salt
  • 1/4 tsp white pepper
  • 2 cups whipping cream
  • 1/4 cup cooking sherry
  • 1 inch cube of Velveeta cheese, cut up
  • 16 oz pkg spaghetti noodles, cooked, drained, and rinsed

Instructions

In a large skillet, saute onion, garlic, and celery in butter until tender. Add mushrooms and saute 2-3 minutes. Add soups, whipping cream, Velveeta, chicken, salt, and pepper. Stir until cheese is melted. Add sherry. Mix well. Grease a large casserole pan. Place 1/2 of the spaghetti noodles in the pan. Pour 1/3 of the chicken mixture over. Layer remaining spaghetti and pour remaining mixture over. Bake at 350 degrees for 30-45 minutes or until bubbly. Enjoy!

"The law of the Lord is perfect, reviving the soul." Psalm 19:7

Main Course |

Chicken Ziti

Ingredients

  • 8 oz uncooked penne or bow tie pasta
  • ½ yellow onion, chopped
  • 1 tsp minced garlic
  • 1 TB olive oil
  • 3 boneless chicken breasts, cut into bite-size pieces
  • ¼ cup white wine
  • ¼ cup chopped fresh basil
  • 1 TB dried parsley flakes
  • 10 oz jar Alfredo sauce
  • ¾ cup sliced sun-dried tomatoes in olive oil, drained
  • ½ cup grated Parmesan cheese, divided
  • ¼ tsp pepper

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 350 degrees.

  2. Cook pasta according to package directions; drain.

  3. In a large skillet over medium heat, cook yellow onion and garlic in olive oil for 30 seconds.

  4. Add chicken and cook for 3 minutes or until no longer pink.

  5. Add wine, basil, and parsley; cook for 1 minute.

  6. In a large bowl, combine cooked pasta, chicken mixture, Alfredo sauce, tomatoes, ¼ cup of Parmesan cheese, and pepper.

  7. Spoon into a 9x13 baking dish that has been sprayed with cooking spray.

  8. Sprinkle with remaining ¼ cup Parmesan cheese.

  9. Cover and bake for 15 minutes.

  10. Uncover and bake an additional 10-15 minutes or until heated through.

  11. Serve and enjoy!

Main Course |

Chicken and Spinach Lasagna

Ingredients

  • 9 Lasagna Noodles
  • ½ cup Butter
  • 1 yellow Onion, chopped
  • 1 tsp. Chopped Garlic
  • ½ cup Flour
  • 1 tsp. Salt
  • 1 can Chicken Broth
  • 1 ½ cups Milk
  • 4 cups shredded Mozzarella Cheese
  • 1 cup Grated Parmesan Cheese
  • 1 tsp. Dried Basil
  • 1 tsp. Dried Oregano
  • ½ tsp. Black Pepper
  • 2 cups Ricotta Cheese
  • 2 Chicken Breasts, cooked and chopped
  • 10 oz pkg. Frozen spinach, thawed and drained
  • 1 TB Parsley
  • ¼ cup Parmesan cheese for topping

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 350 degrees.

  2. Melt the butter in a large saucepan over medium heat.

  3. Cook the onion and garlic in the butter until tender, stirring frequently.

  4. Stir in the flour and salt and simmer until bubbly.

  5. Mix in the chicken broth and milk, and boil, stirring constantly for 1 minute.

  6. Add 2 cups mozzarella cheese, ½ cup Parmesan cheese, basil, oregano, and pepper.

  7. Remove from heat and set aside.

  8. Spread 1/3 of the sauce in the bottom of a greased 9x13 baking dish.

  9. Layer with 3 noodles, then ricotta cheese, then chicken.

  10. Layer 3 more noodles on top of chicken and layer another 1/3 of the sauce.

  11. Layer spinach, and 2 cups of mozzarella cheese and ½ cup Parmesan cheese.

  12. Arrange remaining 3 noodles over cheese and spread remaining sauce over noodles.

  13. Sprinkle with parsley and ¼ cup Parmesan cheese.

  14. Bake 35-40 minutes. Let cool 5 minutes before serving. Enjoy!

Chicken with Asparagus

Ingredients

  • 4 skinless boneless chicken breasts
  • 14.5 oz can chicken broth
  • 5 TB olive oil
  • 2 eggs, beaten
  • 1 cup Italian-style dry bread crumbs
  • 4 slices provolone cheese
  • 1 bunch fresh asparagus, divided into 4 groups
  • Garlic salt and lemon pepper to taste

Instructions

  1. Using a pan, flatten chicken breasts between 2 sheets of parchment paper. (This will help them cook more evenly.)

  2. In a large skillet, heat olive oil over medium heat.

  3. Season chicken breasts with garlic salt and lemon pepper.

  4. Dip chicken breasts in egg; then coat with bread crumbs.

  5. Place chicken breasts in skillet and brown on both sides.

  6. Once chicken is browned, add broth to skillet.

  7. Place a group of asparagus on top of each chicken breast.

  8. Lay a slice of cheese on top of asparagus.

  9. Cover skillet and cook over medium heat for 15 minutes or until chicken is no longer pink inside.

  10. Serve and enjoy!

Main Course |

Chili

Ingredients

  • 2 lbs ground beef

  • 1 tsp chili powder

  • 1/2 tsp cumin

  • 1/2 tsp salt

  • 1/2 tsp black pepper

  • 1 large yellow onion

  • 4 cloves garlic, minced

  • 2 tbsp vegetable oil

  • 15 oz can tomato sauce

  • 15 oz can fire roasted diced tomatoes

  • 15 oz can chili beans, undrained

  • 15 oz can kidney beans, drained

  • 6 oz can tomato paste

  • 1 cup water

  • 1/2 cup salsa

  • 4 tbsp chili powder

  • 1 tsp pepper

  • 1 tsp salt

  • 1/2 tsp cumin

Topping ideas:

  • Shredded cheddar cheese
  • Sour cream
  • Tortilla chips or Fritos
  • Salsa

Instructions

  1. In a large skillet, brown ground beef with chili powder, cumin, salt, and pepper. Drain and set aside.
  2. In a large dutch oven, heat vegetable oil over medium heat. Sauté onions and garlic for about 5 minutes or until tender. Add ground beef.
  3. Stir in remaining ingredients: tomato sauce, diced tomatoes, chili beans, kidney beans, tomato paste, water, salsa, chili powder, pepper, salt, and cumin.
  4. Cover and simmer at least 30 minutes or until ready to serve. Enjoy!

Cowboy Casserole

Ingredients

  • 1 lb Ground Beef
  • 15 oz can Corn, drained
  • 1 can Cream of Chicken Soup
  • 8 oz Cheddar Cheese, grated
  • 2/3 cup Milk
  • 1/4 cup Sour Cream
  • 1 1/2 tsp Onion Powder
  • 1/2 tsp Pepper
  • 1 pkg Frozen Tater Tots

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 375 degrees.

  2. In a large skillet, cook ground beef over medium heat until no longer pink.

  3. Stir in the corn, soup, 1/2 of the cheese, milk, sour cream, onion powder and pepper.

  4. Mix well and set aside.

  5. Place 1/2 of the Tater Tots in the bottom of a greased baking dish.

  6. Layer with the meat mixture.

  7. Top with remaining tater tots.

  8. Sprinkle with remaining cheese.

  9. Bake, uncovered for 20-25 minutes or until bubbly.

  10. Enjoy!

Creamy Potato Pork Chop Bake

Ingredients

  • 1 TB Vegetable Oil
  • 6 Pork Chops
  • Garlic Salt and Lemon Pepper to taste
  • 1 Can Cream of Celery Soup
  • 2 Cups Shredded Cheese
  • 16 oz Sour Cream
  • 1/2 cup French-fried Onions
  • 16 oz Frozen Hash Browns, thawed

Topping:

  • 1/2 cup Shredded Cheese
  • 1/2 cup French-fried Onions

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 400 degrees.

  2. In a large skillet, heat oil over medium high heat. Season pork chops and sauté until browned. Remove from skillet and drain on paper towel.

  3. In a large mixing bowl, combine soup, sour cream, potatoes, cheese, and onions. Spread mixture into a greased 9x13 baking dish. Arrange pork chops over mixture. Cover with foil and bake for about 40 minutes (potato mixture should be bubbling).

  4. Remove cover and top with 1/2 cup Shredded Cheese and 1/2 cup French-fried onions. Bake uncovered for 5 more minutes.

  5. Serve and enjoy!

Creamy Tacos

Ingredients

  • 2 lbs Ground Beef
  • Salt and pepper to taste
  • 1 Onion, chopped
  • 1 pkg Williams's Chili Seasoning Mix
  • 12 oz can Evaporated Milk
  • 1 can Rotel, diced
  • 2 lbs Velveeta Cheese
  • 1 pkg Fritos

Instructions

In a dutch oven over medium to medium-high heat, brown beef with onion, salt, and pepper to taste. Drain meat and return to skillet. Add chili seasoning, evaporated milk, and Rotel. Turn down heat to medium-low, add Velveeta cheese, and cook until cheese is melted. Serve over Fritos and enjoy!

Note: Kathy prefers this as a Mexican chili over hot dogs!

Main Course |

Crockpot Chicken Tacos

Ingredients

  • 4-6 Chicken Breasts (they can be frozen)
  • 2 cans Ranch Style Beans
  • 2 cans Corn
  • 1 small can chipotle sauce
  • 2 cans fire-roasted diced tomatoes, drained
  • 2 small cans tomato paste

Instructions

Combine beans, corn, chipotle sauce, tomatoes, and tomato paste in crockpot. Mix well.

Place frozen or uncooked chicken breast on top and then stir to coat chicken.

Cook on high for 4-5 hours.

After 4 hours, remove lid and shred chicken with fork.

Stir ingredients and continue to cook until ready to serve.

Serve with tortillas, lettuce, tomatoes, guacamole, sour cream, and cheese. Top with salsa and enjoy!

This makes a lot so you can use the leftovers to make chicken enchiladas.

Chicken Enchiladas:

Fill flour or corn tortillas with filling. Add shredded cheese and roll placing seam side down in a greased 9x13 baking dish.

Top with enchilada sauce of your choice and shredded cheese.

Bake at 350°F for 30 minutes.

Enjoy!

Crockpot Chicken and Rice

Ingredients

  • 3 uncooked chicken breasts, cut up into bite size pieces
  • 8 oz pkg cream cheese, diced
  • 1 can cream of chicken soup
  • 1 can chicken broth
  • 1 box Uncle Ben's Wild Rice (you won't be using the seasoning packet)
  • 1 pkg Zesty Italian dressing mix

Instructions

  1. Place all ingredients in a crockpot.

  2. Stir together.

  3. Place lid on and set to low temperature.

  4. Cook 3 1/2 - 4 hours.

  5. Serve and enjoy!

Main Course |

Crockpot Roast Sliders

Ingredients

  • 3-4 lb beef roast
  • 1 cup water
  • 1 cup salsa
  • 1 envelope onion soup mix
  • 1 envelope Italian dressing mix
  • 1 envelope Au Jus mix
  • 1 pkg. King's Hawaiian Rolls
  • Sliced Swiss cheese (1 slice per slider)

Creamy Chipotle Sauce ingredients:

  • 1/4 cup sour cream
  • 2 Tbsp chili sauce
  • 2 tsp creamy horseradish sauce

Instructions

  1. Whisk together water, salsa, and seasoning packets. Place roast in slow cooker. Pour seasoning mixture over roast. Cook on low for 8-10 hours, or high for 4-5 hours.

  2. Cut each roll in half and place on a baking sheet. Place a slice of Swiss cheese on the bottom half of each roll. Place baking sheet in oven with broiler on low. Broil for 1-2 minutes or until edges of the rolls are golden and cheese has melted (be sure to watch them closely so they don't burn!). Remove baking sheet from oven.

  3. Place a spoonful of shredded beef on top of each roll with Swiss cheese on it. Spread Creamy Chipotle sauce on the top half of each roll. Assemble each slider by placing the top half of the roll on top of the bottom.

Dijon Chicken

Ingredients

  • 1/2 cup all-purpose flour
  • 4 boneless, skinless chicken breasts
  • Salt, pepper, lemon pepper, and garlic salt to season
  • 2 tablespoons butter
  • 1/2 cup white cooking wine
  • 1 cup chicken broth, divided into 2 - 1/2 cups
  • 1 tablespoon Dijon mustard
  • 1 zucchini, cut into slices
  • 5 sun-dried tomatoes, chopped
  • 1 teaspoon garlic, minced
  • 1 teaspoon dill weed
  • 1/3 cup sour cream

Instructions

In a large skillet, melt butter over medium-high heat.

Place the flour in a pie pan.

Season chicken with salt, pepper, garlic salt, and lemon pepper.

Dredge in flour, shaking off excess.

Brown the chicken breasts on each side, about 3 minutes per side.

In a small bowl, combine the white wine, 1/2 cup of chicken broth, and mustard.

Remove chicken from pan.

Pour in broth mixture and scrape any chicken residue from the bottom of the pan for flavor.

Return chicken to pan, cover, and cook for 4-5 minutes.

In another small bowl, combine zucchini slices, tomatoes, garlic, and dill weed.

Add zucchini mixture to the pan, cover, and cook until zucchini is tender; about 5-10 minutes.

Remove chicken and zucchini; place on plate to serve.

Add 1/2 cup of chicken broth to the pan, scraping the bottom of the pan for residue. Heat for 2 minutes.

Remove from heat and add sour cream. Stir until well blended.

Serve sauce over chicken and enjoy!

I lift up my eyes to the hills – where does my help come from? My help comes from the LORD, the Maker of heaven and earth.
Psalm 121:1-2 NIV

Main Course |

Fettuccine Alfredo

Ingredients

  • 12 oz pkg fettuccine
  • 5 garlic cloves (chopped)
  • 1 stick of butter
  • 1 cup whipping cream
  • 5 oz pkg Parmesan cheese (grated)
  • 1/4 tsp salt
  • 1/4 tsp pepper
  • 2 tbsp fresh parsley (chopped)

Instructions

Cook fettuccine according to package directions, drain.

While fettuccine is cooking, chop 5 garlic cloves. In large skillet over low heat, melt 1 tablespoon from the stick of butter and sauté the chopped garlic until golden in color. Add the rest of the butter and whipping cream. Continue cooking over low heat until butter is melted. Stir in cheese, salt, pepper, and parsley. Continue cooking until mixture is creamy. If too thick, add more whipping cream.

Add fettuccine and toss well. Serve immediately.

Cooking Time: 11 minutes.

Serves: 4

He will cover you with His feathers, and under His wings you will find refuge; His faithfulness will be your shield and rampart.
Psalm 91:4

Main Course |

Fried Chicken

Ingredients

  • 1 1/2 lbs boneless skinless chicken breasts
  • Salt
  • Pepper
  • Garlic Salt
  • Lemon Pepper
  • 2 eggs (lightly beaten)
  • 1/2 cup milk
  • 2 cups all-purpose flour
  • Vegetable Oil

To Prepare the chicken, you will need:

  • White vinegar
  • 1/2 lemon

Instructions

To prepare chicken breasts:

  1. Rinse in cold water and pour 1 cup white vinegar over chicken.
  2. Cut off excess fat and cut breasts in half.
  3. Place chicken in a ziplock bag and squeeze the juice of half of a lemon over chicken. Refrigerate until ready to use. (When chicken is on sale, I buy a lot and prepare it this way, then freeze until ready to use. The lemon juice makes the meat tender.)

In a small bowl, combine the eggs and milk. Set aside.

In another larger bowl, combine the flour with 1 tablespoon of garlic salt and 1 tablespoon of lemon pepper. Set aside.

Pour oil to a depth of 1 inch into a heavy frying pan or electric skillet; heat to 350°F (medium to medium-high).

Season chicken with salt, pepper, garlic salt, and lemon pepper.

  1. Dip into the egg mixture and dredge in flour mixture, coating chicken well.

  2. Fry chicken in hot oil over medium heat for 15 to 20 minutes or until golden, turning occasionally.

  3. Remove and drain on paper towels.

'Keep your eyes on Jesus, who both began and finished this race we're in.' Hebrews 12:2 (The Message)

Main Course |

Fried Rice

Ingredients

  • 2 cups water
  • 2 cups Minute Rice
  • 1 egg
  • 1 TB Olive Oil
  • 4 green onions, optional

Additional vegetable suggestions:

  • Zucchini

  • Broccoli

  • Carrots

  • Bell peppers

  • 3 TB Butter

  • 2 TB Sesame Seeds

  • Soy Sauce

  • Salt

  • Pepper

Instructions

  1. Bring 2 cups of water to a boil. (Leave lid on pan to bring to a boil faster.) Turn off heat and add 2 cups rice. Place lid on pan and let sit covered for 5 minutes.

  2. Heat olive oil in a skillet over medium heat. Crack egg and scramble. Season with salt and pepper. Chop green onions and any other vegetables; add to egg. Cook for 3-4 minutes, allowing veggies to soften.

  3. Add rice, butter, soy sauce, and sesame seeds. Mix together and season with salt and pepper. Cover pan with lid and cook for 3 minutes. Remove lid and stir. Cover pan and let cook for another 3 minutes.

  4. Rice is finished when it starts to turn a golden brown. Serve with egg rolls and enjoy!

This recipe serves 4.

"Don't let anyone look down on you because you are young, but set an example for the believers in speech, in life, in love, in faith and in purity." 1 Timothy 4:12 NIV

Greek Chicken Pasta

Ingredients

  • 8 oz uncooked pasta
  • 1 TB olive oil
  • 3-4 garlic cloves, minced
  • ½ red onion, chopped
  • 4 chicken breasts, cut into bite-size pieces
  • Garlic salt and coarse black pepper, to taste
  • 14 oz can marinated artichoke hearts, drained and chopped
  • 2 large tomatoes, chopped or 1 can chopped tomatoes
  • 2 pkgs crumbled feta cheese
  • 3 TB fresh parsley, chopped
  • 2 TB lemon juice, fresh squeezed
  • 1 TB dried basil

Instructions

  1. Bring a large pot of lightly salted water to a boil. Cook pasta according to directions on package.

  2. While pasta is cooking; heat olive oil in a large skillet over medium-high heat. Add onion and garlic, and sauté for 2 minutes.

  3. Season chicken pieces with garlic salt and black pepper, add chicken to the skillet. Cook, stirring occasionally, until chicken is no longer pink, about 6 minutes.

  4. Reduce heat to medium-low, and add the artichoke hearts, tomato, feta cheese, parsley, lemon juice, basil, and cooked pasta. Stir until heated through, about 2-3 minutes. Remove from heat; serve and enjoy!

Healthy Baked Linguine

Ingredients

  • 1 lb ground beef
  • 1 tsp minced garlic
  • Pam cooking spray
  • 1 (28 oz) can crushed tomatoes
  • 1 (8 oz) can tomato sauce
  • 1 (6 oz) can tomato paste
  • 2 tsp sugar
  • 1/2 tsp salt
  • 8 oz uncooked whole wheat linguine
  • 16 oz nonfat sour cream
  • 8 oz fat-free cream cheese, softened
  • 1 bunch green onions
  • 1 1/2 cups shredded low-fat mozzarella cheese

Instructions

Preheat oven to 350 degrees.

Coat a large skillet with Pam cooking spray. Brown ground beef and garlic, cooking until beef crumbles and is no longer pink.

Drain beef mixture and stir in crushed tomatoes, tomato sauce, tomato paste, sugar, and salt.

Let simmer for 30 minutes.

Cook pasta according to package directions.

Place pasta in a 13x9 baking dish coated with Pam cooking spray.

In a medium mixing bowl, combine sour cream, cream cheese, and green onions.

Spread over linguine.

Top with meat sauce.

Bake at 350 degrees for 20-25 minutes or until bubbly on sides.

Sprinkle with mozzarella cheese, and bake 5 more minutes or until cheese melts.

Let stand 5 minutes before serving.

Enjoy!

Indian Tacos

Ingredients

  • 1 lb ground beef
  • 1 tsp salt
  • 1 tsp pepper
  • 1 TB dried minced onion flakes
  • 1 TB chili powder
  • 1 tsp garlic powder
  • 1 pkg taco seasoning
  • 1 can Rotel
  • 15 oz can spicy chili beans with liquid
  • Small jar picante sauce or as much as you prefer
  • 8 oz cheddar cheese, shredded
  • 2 cups iceberg lettuce, shredded
  • Additional toppings you might like:
    • Guacamole
    • Sour cream
    • Green onions

Fry Bread:

  • 2 cups all-purpose flour
  • 1 TB baking powder
  • 1 tsp salt
  • 1 cup milk
  • 4 cups oil for frying or as much as needed

Instructions

In a large skillet over medium-high heat, brown the ground beef.

Add the salt, pepper, onion flakes, chili powder, and garlic powder while browning the meat.

Once the meat is brown, add the taco seasoning, pinto beans, and Rotel.

Cover and simmer while you prepare the fry bread.

Fry Bread:
In a medium bowl, stir together the flour, baking powder, and salt.

Stir in the milk and mix until dough comes together.

Add more flour if necessary to be able to handle the dough.

On a floured surface, knead the dough until smooth, about 5 minutes.

Let dough rest for 5 minutes.

Heat oil in a large deep skillet to 365 degrees.

Oil should be about 1 1/2 inches deep.

Cut dough into 6 sections, or you can make them smaller for little appetizers!

Shape each section into a round disc about 1/4 inch thick, making the center area thinner.

Place dough into hot oil until golden on both sides, turning only once.

Drain on paper towels.

Place bread on a plate and top with meat mixture, lettuce, and cheese.

Top with picante sauce and any other toppings you prefer.

Enjoy!!

(Amp) Eph. 2:10

Maple Glazed Pork Tenderloin

Ingredients

  • 2 pork tenderloins, about 2-3 lbs total
  • 2 tsp garlic, minced
  • 1 TB Cajun seasoning
  • 1 TB garlic salt
  • 1 TB black pepper
  • 2 TB butter
  • 2 TB olive oil
  • 6 TB pure maple syrup
  • ¼ cup apple cider vinegar
  • 2 tsp Dijon mustard
  • 2 tsp apple cider vinegar

Instructions

  1. Trim pork of any visible fat. Season pork tenderloins with garlic, Cajun seasoning, garlic salt, and black pepper.

  2. In a small mixing bowl, combine 6 TB maple syrup, ¼ cup apple cider vinegar, and the Dijon mustard. Mix thoroughly.

  3. In a large skillet, melt butter with olive oil over medium heat until hot and foamy. Add pork tenderloins and cook, turning, until brown on all sides, about 6-8 minutes. Reduce heat to medium-low, cover, and cook until inside meat temperature is 150 degrees. This should take about 15 minutes.

  4. Transfer pork to a platter; cover and keep warm. Add 2 TB vinegar to skillet and bring to boil, scraping up any brown bits. Reduce heat to medium-low.

  5. Return pork to skillet; turn pork in glaze to coat well, about 2 minutes. Remove from heat. Cut pork into slices. Spoon glaze over pork and serve with rice. Enjoy!

Marinara Sauce

Ingredients

  • 1 small onion, chopped
  • 5 cloves garlic, chopped
  • 1/4 cup olive oil
  • 2 (28oz) cans diced tomatoes
  • 1/4 cup chopped fresh basil
  • 1/4 cup chopped fresh parsley
  • 2 tbsp lemon juice
  • 1/2 tsp sugar

Instructions

  1. Sauté onion and garlic in hot olive oil in a large saucepan, stirring constantly.
  2. Add tomatoes, basil, parsley, lemon juice, and sugar; bring mixture to a boil.
  3. Reduce heat to medium, and cook, uncovered, until mixture thickens (about 30 minutes).
  4. Serve over your favorite pasta.

ENJOY!

Mexican Goulash

Ingredients

  • 1 lb ground beef
  • 1 small yellow onion, chopped
  • 14.5 oz can Rotel, undrained
  • 8 oz can tomato sauce
  • 1 can corn, drained
  • 1 can black beans, drained
  • 3/4 cup water
  • 1 1/2 tsp chili powder
  • 1 tsp cumin
  • 1/2 tsp garlic powder
  • 1/2 tsp salt
  • 1 cup uncooked elbow macaroni
  • 1 cup Mexican cheese, shredded

Instructions

In a large nonstick skillet, season ground beef with chili powder, cumin, garlic powder, and salt. Cook ground beef and onion over medium heat until meat is no longer pink.

Drain excess grease from the pan.

Stir in the Rotel, tomato sauce, corn, black beans, and water.

Bring to a boil.

Add macaroni.

Reduce heat; cover and simmer for 20 minutes or until macaroni is tender.

Sprinkle with cheese.

Cover and cook for 2-3 minutes longer or until cheese is melted.

Enjoy!

Main Course |

Oodles of Noodles

Ingredients

  • 4 slices of bacon
  • 3 TB butter
  • 1 TB olive oil
  • 1 cup bread crumbs
  • 2 TB finely chopped parsley
  • 1 tsp lemon zest
  • 1 tsp crushed red pepper
  • 1 tsp minced onion
  • 1 tsp minced garlic
  • 1 ¼ cup whipping cream
  • ¾ cup grated Parmesan cheese
  • 1/3 cup goat cheese
  • 1 TB minced green onions
  • Salt and pepper to taste
  • 1 lb penne pasta

Instructions

  1. Bring a large pot of water to a boil. In a small skillet, cook the bacon until crisp. Transfer to paper towels to drain; let cool. Crumble the bacon.

  2. In a small saucepan, melt 1 TB butter in 1 TB of olive oil until foamy. In a bowl, toss the bacon with the bread crumbs, parsley, lemon zest, and crushed red pepper. Add the melted butter; mix well. Set aside for topping at the end.

  3. Melt the remaining 2 TB of butter in a saucepan. Add the onion and cook over medium heat for about 3 minutes. Add the garlic and cook for about 30 seconds or until you can smell the garlic. Reduce heat to medium-low, add the cream and simmer, stirring, until reduced by 1/3, about 5 minutes. Remove from heat and stir in the Parmesan, goat cheese, and green onions. Season with salt and pepper.

  4. Add the penne pasta to the boiling water and cook as directed. Drain and transfer to bowl. Add the cheese sauce and toss. Sprinkle with bread crumbs and serve. Enjoy!

Main Course |

Orange Chicken

Ingredients

  • 4 chicken breasts, cut in 1-inch pieces
  • 1/4 cup vegetable oil
  • 1 cup orange juice
  • 1/4 cup soy sauce
  • 1/2 tsp salt
  • 3 cloves of garlic, minced
  • 1 tbsp brown sugar
  • 1/2 tsp red pepper flakes
  • 2 tbsp cornstarch
  • 1 onion, cut in quarters
  • 1 head of broccoli florets

Instructions

In a small bowl, combine orange juice, soy sauce, salt, garlic, brown sugar, red pepper flakes, and cornstarch. Mix well and set aside.

Heat oil in a large skillet over medium-high heat. When oil is hot, add chicken. Sauté until no longer pink inside (about 7-10 minutes).

(While chicken is cooking, in a saucepan cook minute rice following the directions on the package.)

Add orange sauce mixture to chicken and cook for 5 minutes or until sauce thickens. Add broccoli florets and onion, cover and cook 2 minutes.

Serve over steamed rice.

Psalm 31:16
'Let your face shine on your servant; save me in your unfailing love.'

Main Course |

Pasta Tacos

Ingredients

  • 1 lb lean ground beef
  • 4 oz cream cheese
  • 1 pkg taco seasoning
  • 1/2 cup water
  • 15 oz can Ranch Style Beans
  • 18 jumbo pasta shells
  • 2 tbsp butter, melted
  • 1 bottle taco sauce
  • 2 cups shredded Colby/Jack cheese
  • 1 ½ cups crushed tortilla chips

Toppings:

  • Lettuce
  • Sour cream
  • Salsa

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 350 degrees.

  2. In a large skillet, brown beef over medium heat until no longer pink; drain.

  3. Add water, Ranch Style Beans, and taco seasoning; mix well. Add cream cheese; mix, cover, and simmer for 5 minutes.

  4. Bring a large pot of lightly salted water to a boil. Add pasta and cook for 8-10 minutes; drain.

  5. Toss cooked shells in butter.

  6. Fill shells with beef mixture and arrange in a greased 9x13 baking dish; pour taco sauce over shells.

  7. Cover with foil and bake for 15 minutes.

  8. Remove from oven and top with cheese and tortilla chips.

  9. Return to oven and bake uncovered for 15 minutes more.

  10. Remove from oven. Top with lettuce, sour cream, and salsa. Enjoy!

Pasta with Pesto Cream Sauce

Ingredients

  • 1 jar Pesto Sauce
  • 1/2 cup Heavy Cream
  • 2 tablespoons Butter
  • 1/4 cup fresh Parmesan, grated
  • 12 oz Pasta, your choice
  • 2 whole Tomatoes, diced

Instructions

  1. Cook pasta according to package directions.

  2. Heat cream and butter in a small saucepan over medium-low heat.

  3. Add pesto and stir.

  4. Drain pasta and place in serving bowl.

  5. Pour pesto cream sauce over the top and toss to combine.

  6. Add diced tomatoes, toss quickly, and serve.

Enjoy!

Pepper Steak

Ingredients

  • 1 lb sirloin steak, cut into thin strips
  • 1/3 cup red wine vinegar
  • 1/3 cup honey
  • 1/3 cup soy sauce
  • 2 tsp minced garlic
  • 1 yellow onion, chopped
  • 1 green bell pepper, sliced into thin strips
  • 2 tbsp olive oil
  • 1 red bell pepper, sliced into thin strips
  • 1 head broccoli, chopped
  • Brown rice

Instructions

  1. Heat olive oil in skillet over medium-high heat.
  2. Pour soy sauce, honey, and red wine vinegar in pan, then add steak and garlic. Cook steak, stirring frequently until done, about 10-15 minutes (no pink showing).
  3. Cook brown rice according to directions.
  4. Add onion and bell pepper, cook until tender, about 10 minutes.
  5. Serve over brown rice and enjoy!

Pesto Chicken Florentine

Ingredients

  • 2 tablespoons Olive Oil
  • 2 cloves Garlic, minced
  • 1 teaspoon Lemon Pepper
  • 1 teaspoon Garlic Salt
  • 4 Chicken Breasts, cut into strips
  • 2 cups Fresh Spinach
  • 1 jar Alfredo Sauce
  • 2 tablespoons Pesto
  • 8 oz package Penne Pasta
  • 1 tablespoon grated Romano Cheese

Instructions

  1. Season chicken with lemon pepper and garlic salt.

  2. Heat oil in a large skillet over medium-high heat. Add garlic, sauté for 1 minute; then add chicken and cook for 7-8 minutes on each side.

  3. When chicken is no longer pink inside, add spinach to pan and sauté all together for 3-4 minutes.

  4. In a saucepan, heat alfredo sauce. Add pesto and set aside.

  5. Cook pasta according to package directions. Rinse under cold water and drain. Add pasta to chicken/spinach mixture; stir in pesto/alfredo sauce. Mix well. Serve and top with Romano cheese. Enjoy!

Main Course |

Pesto Chicken Spaghetti

Ingredients

  • 1 lb Thin Spaghetti
  • 1 cup prepared Pesto
  • 1 cup Milk
  • 1 Rotisserie Chicken
  • 1/2 tsp Salt
  • 1/2 tsp Garlic Salt
  • 1/2 tsp Black Pepper
  • 16 oz Fresh Mozzarella, cubed
  • 1 cup shredded Mozzarella

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 350 degrees.

  2. Spray 9x13 baking dish with cooking spray.

  3. Cook spaghetti according to package directions.

  4. Drain and run under cold water.

  5. Place noodles back into pot.

  6. While spaghetti is cooking, remove and shred meat from rotisserie chicken. Set aside.

  7. Cut fresh mozzarella into cubes and grate the other mozzarella.

  8. After noodles are cooked and placed back in pot, add milk, chicken, salt, garlic salt, and pepper.

  9. Stir to combine.

  10. Transfer half of the spaghetti mixture into baking dish and add mozzarella cubes scattered about.

  11. Top with remaining spaghetti mixture and then top with grated mozzarella cheese.

  12. Bake for 20-25 minutes or until cheese is melted.

  13. Serve and enjoy!

Main Course |

Pesto Lasagna Rolls

Ingredients

  • 10 Lasagna Noodles (not the oven-ready kind)
  • 28 oz jar Marinara Sauce
  • 2 tsp minced Garlic
  • 15 oz container Ricotta Cheese
  • 10 oz package frozen chopped spinach, thawed and squeezed dry
  • 3 1/2 oz jar Pesto
  • 1 egg
  • 1 cup grated Parmesan Cheese
  • 1/2 tsp Salt
  • 1/2 tsp Pepper
  • 1 cup shredded Mozzarella Cheese

Instructions

Preheat oven to 350 degrees

Cook lasagna noodles according to package directions; remove from water and cool on dish towel.

While lasagna noodles are cooking, spread 2 cups of marinara sauce into the bottom of a 9x13 baking dish.

In a large mixing bowl, combine garlic, ricotta, spinach, pesto, egg, parmesan cheese, salt, and pepper.

On cooled lasagna noodles, spread 1/4 to 1/3 cup of ricotta mixture on the noodle.

Roll noodle up; place seam side down in pan.
Repeat with remaining noodles.

Pour remaining marinara sauce over noodles; sprinkle with mozzarella cheese.

Cover with foil and bake for 20 minutes. Uncover and cook until mozzarella is golden brown; 10-15 minutes.

Serve with salad and bread and enjoy!

“Serve wholeheartedly, as if you were serving the Lord, not men…” Ephesians 6:7

Main Course |

Philly Cheesesteak

Ingredients

  • 3 lbs sirloin, very thinly sliced
  • Garlic salt and pepper to season meat
  • 2 tbsp olive oil
  • 1 cup beef broth
  • 2 tbsp Worcestershire sauce
  • 1 yellow onion, thinly sliced
  • 1 green bell pepper, thinly sliced
  • 1 tbsp minced garlic
  • 1 tbsp olive oil
  • 6-count package hoagie buns, toasted
  • Shredded pepper jack cheese or any kind of white cheese

Instructions

  1. Season meat with garlic salt and pepper.
  2. In a large skillet, heat olive oil over medium-high heat.
  3. Add beef, and cook until cooked through, about 7-10 minutes.
  4. Add broth and Worcestershire sauce, and bring to a boil; reduce heat and simmer, stirring occasionally, for 15-25 minutes.
  5. In another skillet, heat olive oil over medium-high heat.
  6. Add onion, bell pepper, and garlic, stirring occasionally.
  7. Cook until tender.
  8. While meat is simmering and vegetables are cooking, grate cheese.
  9. Toast buns in oven.
  10. When meat and veggies are done, assemble sandwiches.
  11. Top sandwiches with grated cheese and return to oven until cheese is melted.
  12. Serve and enjoy!

Poppy Seed Chicken

Ingredients

  • 4 Chicken Breasts, cooked
  • 1 can Cream of Celery Soup
  • 1 cup Sour Cream
  • 1 1/2 cups Saltine Crackers, crushed
  • 1/2 cup Butter, melted
  • 1 TB Poppy Seed

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 350 degrees F. Grease a 9x13 baking dish.

  2. Cut or shred chicken into bite-sized pieces; place in baking dish.

  3. In a small mixing bowl, mix the soup and sour cream. Spread over chicken.

  4. In another small mixing bowl, combine cracker crumbs, poppy seed, and butter; sprinkle over casserole.

  5. Bake uncovered for 30 minutes or until golden brown.

  6. Enjoy!

Main Course |

Queso Taco Pasta

Ingredients

  • 4 tablespoons butter

  • 1/4 cup all-purpose flour

  • 1/2 teaspoon kosher salt

  • 1/4 teaspoon freshly ground black pepper

  • 1 1/2 cups reduced sodium chicken broth

  • 1 1/2 cups shredded cheddar cheese

  • 1/2 cup prepared mild salsa

  • 2 tablespoons extra virgin olive oil

  • 1 cup finely chopped onions

  • 1 tablespoon minced garlic

  • 2 lbs lean ground beef Substitute 2 cans black beans, drained and rinsed for meatless version

  • 1/2 teaspoon kosher salt

  • 1/4 teaspoon freshly ground black pepper

  • One 10-ounce can Rotel tomatoes, mild

  • 1 tablespoon ground cumin

  • 1 tablespoon fresh lime juice

  • 3/4 pound small pasta of your choice

  • 2 cups shredded cheddar cheese

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 350 degrees F and lightly spray a 9x13 inch baking dish with cooking spray.

  2. Place butter into a medium saucepan over medium heat. Stir to melt. Whisk in flour, salt, and pepper for about 30 seconds until bubbly. Slowly whisk in chicken broth until smooth. Increase heat to high while whisking until thickened, about 3-5 minutes. Stir in cheese and salsa, turn off heat, and set aside.

  3. Place 2 tablespoons olive oil in a medium skillet over medium heat. Sauté onions until softened, about 5 minutes. Add garlic and cook for an additional minute. Add ground beef, salt, and pepper, cooking until browned. Drain fat if necessary. Add Rotel, cumin, and lime juice; stir to combine. If using black beans, drain and rinse them. Add them to the onion mixture when you add the Rotel, cumin, and lime juice. Simmer for about 5-10 minutes then add to queso.

  4. Cook pasta according to package directions, drain and run under cold water.

  5. Transfer beef, noodles, and queso to a large bowl and mix all ingredients together. Taste and season with additional salt and pepper to your liking. Transfer to prepared baking dish and top with shredded cheese. Bake for 25-30 minutes until cheese is melted. Serve warm.

Show me your ways, Lord, teach me your paths. Guide me in your truth and teach me, for you are God my Savior, and my hope is in you all day long.
Psalm 25:4-5

Sesame Chicken

Ingredients

  • 1 cup flour
  • 1/2 cup sesame seeds
  • 1 tsp pepper
  • 1 tsp Chinese five-spice powder or 1 tsp ground ginger
  • 1 tsp crushed red pepper flakes (optional)
  • 4 chicken breasts, cut into bite-size pieces
  • 5 tbsp vegetable oil
  • 1/2 yellow onion, cut into wedges
  • 1/2 cup green bell pepper, thinly sliced
  • 6 tbsp teriyaki sauce
  • 6 tbsp honey

Instructions

In a large resealable plastic bag, combine flour, 1/4 cup sesame seeds, pepper, five-spice powder, and red pepper flakes.

Place a few pieces of chicken at a time into the bag.

Shake to coat. Remove, set aside on platter, and repeat until all chicken is coated.

Heat oil in a large skillet over medium-high heat.

Place chicken into skillet and brown on both sides, about 5 minutes.

Remove chicken, set aside.

Place onion wedges and bell pepper in skillet, cook until slightly browned, about 2 minutes.

Return chicken to skillet, reduce heat to low.

Combine teriyaki sauce, remaining 1/4 cup sesame seeds, and honey.

Pour into skillet with chicken, onions, and peppers; stir until sauce thickens.

Serve and enjoy!

When my heart is overwhelmed; Lead me to the rock that is higher than I. For You have been a shelter for me, A strong tower from the enemy. I will abide in Your tabernacle forever; I will trust in the shelter of Your wings.
Psalm 61:2-4

Shrimp Scampi

Ingredients

  • 10 oz pkg Thin Spaghetti
  • 1-2 lbs unpeeled large fresh shrimp
  • 8 cloves garlic, minced
  • 1 cup Butter, melted
  • 1 cup cooking White wine
  • 1/4 tsp salt
  • 1/4 tsp pepper
  • 1/4 cup fresh chopped parsley

Instructions

To thaw shrimp:

  • Open package and rinse with cold water.
  • Place in ziplock baggie and squeeze the juice of 1 lemon on top. Seal bag and let defrost in refrigerator.
  1. Peel shrimp.
  2. Cook pasta according to package directions.
  3. In a large skillet over medium heat, combine butter, wine, and garlic. Stirring constantly, cook 4 minutes or until garlic begins to brown.
  4. Add shrimp and cook over medium heat 3-5 minutes or until shrimp turn pink.
  5. Add salt, pepper, and parsley.
  6. Serve over cooked spaghetti.

Enjoy!

Skillet Soft Tacos

Ingredients

  • 1 lb boneless top sirloin or 2 chicken breasts
  • 1 TB vegetable oil
  • 2 cups water
  • 1 cup salsa
  • 1 pkg taco seasoning
  • 2 cups white or brown minute rice, uncooked
  • 1 can black beans
  • Flour tortillas

Optional Toppings:

  • Grated cheese
  • Sour cream
  • Salsa

Instructions

  1. Heat oil in a large skillet.
  2. Add steak or chicken, cook until done, about 6-8 minutes. No pink showing for the chicken.
  3. Add water, salsa, and taco seasoning.
  4. Heat to a boil.
  5. Add rice and black beans.
  6. Remove from heat and let stand. (5 minutes for white rice - 10 minutes for brown rice).
  7. Spoon into flour tortillas, top with your favorite toppings, and enjoy!

Ingredients

  • 1 can Rotel, drained

  • 1/2 cup BBQ sauce (your favorite)

Main Course |

Spinach & Black Bean Enchiladas

Ingredients

  • 4 Poblano Peppers, halved lengthwise and seeded
  • 2 (15oz) cans Black Beans, drained
  • 1 (16oz) can Pinto Beans, drained
  • 1 (14.5oz) can Fire-roasted diced Tomatoes, drained
  • 1 (8oz) pkg Cream Cheese, softened
  • 1 (8oz) ctn Sour Cream
  • 1 (6oz) bag Baby Spinach
  • 30 (6") corn tortillas, torn into 2" pieces
  • 1 (10oz) can Enchilada Sauce
  • 2 1/2 cups shredded Colby/Jack Cheese

Instructions

  1. Preheat broiler.

  2. Line a rimmed baking sheet with aluminum foil.

  3. Place peppers, cut side down, on prepared baking sheet.

  4. Broil 5 inches away from heat for 8-10 minutes or until skins are charred.

  5. Place hot peppers in a ziplock bag, seal bag and let stand for 10 minutes.

  6. Remove peppers from bag and peel off charred skin, discarding skin.

  7. Finely chop peppers.

  8. In a large bowl, combine peppers, beans, tomatoes, cream cheese, sour cream and spinach.

  9. Reduce oven temperature to 350 degrees.

  10. Lightly grease a 9x13 baking dish.

  11. Evenly layer half of corn tortillas over the bottom of the prepared baking dish.

  12. Spread half of the pepper mixture evenly over tortillas.

  13. Pour half of enchilada sauce over pepper mixture.

  14. Sprinkle evenly with half of the cheeses.

  15. Repeat layers, beginning with tortillas and ending with cheeses.

  16. Bake for 35-40 minutes or until hot and bubbly.

  17. Let stand for 10 minutes before serving.

  18. Enjoy!

Main Course |

Spinach Artichoke Pasta

Ingredients

  • 14 oz of Orecchiette Pasta (or short pasta)
  • 1 Tbsp Olive Oil
  • 1 Cup of Chopped Onion
  • 3 Cloves of Garlic (minced)
  • 1 Cup of Sour Cream
  • 4 oz of Cream Cheese (room temperature)
  • 3/4 Cup of Parmesan Cheese
  • 1 teaspoon Lemon Juice + Zest
  • 10 oz of Spinach (make sure to squeeze excess moisture, after it has been thawed of course)
  • 13.5 oz Can of Artichoke Hearts rinsed and chopped
  • 1 Cup of Mozzarella
  • 1/2 Teaspoon of Salt
  • 1 Teaspoon of Pepper

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 425°F.
  2. Cook pasta according to the box. Keep 1/4 cup of the pasta water before you drain the cooked pasta.
  3. Heat oil in a pan and cook onions for 4-5 minutes. Add garlic and cook for one minute.
  4. In a large bowl, mix sour cream, cream cheese, Parmesan, lemon (juice and zest), onions, and garlic. Add pasta and mix.
  5. Stir in spinach, artichokes, 1/4 cup of cooking liquid from the pasta, salt, pepper, and 1/2 cup of mozzarella.
  6. Place mixture into a greased 2.5 – 3 qt casserole dish and place the rest of the mozzarella cheese on top.
  7. Place in the oven until golden brown on top, about 10 – 15 minutes.
  8. Enjoy!
Main Course |

Taco Lasagna

Ingredients

  • 1 lb lean ground beef
  • 1 pkg taco seasoning
  • 1 can yellow corn with red & green peppers, drained
  • 1/2 cup water
  • 1 (8 oz) container onion & chive cream cheese
  • 2 cups shredded cheddar/jack cheese, divided
  • 4 1/2 cups salsa
  • 9 no-boil lasagna noodles

Instructions

  1. Cook ground beef in a large skillet over medium-high heat, stirring until it crumbles and is no longer pink; drain and return to skillet.

  2. Stir in taco seasoning, corn, and 1/2 cup water. Cook, uncovered, stirring occasionally until thickened, about 5 minutes.

  3. Add cream cheese, stirring until melted. Remove from heat and stir in 1 cup of the shredded cheese.

  4. In a lightly greased 9x13 baking dish, spread 1 cup salsa. Layer with 3 lasagna noodles (don't let noodles touch each other or side of dish), then add 1/2 of the ground beef mixture and 3/4 cup of salsa.

  5. Repeat layers starting with the 3 lasagna noodles, remaining ground beef, and 3/4 cup salsa.

  6. Top with remaining 3 noodles and salsa, covering noodles completely. Sprinkle evenly with remaining 1 cup of cheese.

  7. Bake covered at 375°F for 30 minutes. Uncover and bake an additional 10 minutes or until cheese is melted.

  8. Let stand 5 minutes before serving. Serve topped with sour cream, cilantro, and salsa. Enjoy!

Taco Pie

Ingredients

  • 1 can Crescent Creations (crescent rolls without perforations)
  • 1 lb Ground Beef
  • 1 Pkg Taco Seasoning
  • 1 can Refried Beans
  • 8 oz Shredded Colby/Jack Cheese
  • Snack Size pkg. Nacho Doritos (crushed)

Toppings:

  • Sour Cream
  • Salsa

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 350 degrees.

  2. In a pie pan, lay crescent dough to fit pan. Trim edges and fill in areas where needed.

  3. Bake for 10-15 minutes or until golden brown.

  4. While dough is baking, brown hamburger in large skillet over medium-high heat.

  5. Add the taco seasoning and mix well.

  6. Remove pie pan from oven. Let cool slightly.

  7. Spread 3/4 can of refried beans on top of baked crescent dough.

  8. Layer ground beef on top of refried beans.

  9. Layer shredded cheese on top of ground beef.

  10. Top with crushed Doritos.

  11. Return to oven and bake for 10 minutes or until cheese is melted.

  12. Serve with sour cream and salsa – Enjoy!

Main Course |

Teriyaki Chicken

Ingredients

  • 3 chicken breasts
  • 2 T olive oil
  • Salt and pepper to taste
  • 1 carrot
  • 1 red pepper
  • 1 cup snap peas
  • 1/2 a red onion

The vegetables are suggestions - add in what you like.

  • 16 oz package angel hair pasta

Homemade Teriyaki Sauce:

  • 1 1/2 C cold water
  • 3 T corn starch
  • 3/4 C packed brown sugar
  • 1/2 C soy sauce
  • 1 t garlic powder (or granulated garlic)
  • 1 t sesame oil

Instructions

  1. Cut your chicken breasts into bite-sized pieces. Place in a ziplock bag and pound into thin pieces.

  2. Heat a large skillet over medium-high heat.

  3. Add your oil and chicken breast pieces.

  4. Sprinkle with salt and pepper.

  5. Let them cook for about 10 minutes, stirring frequently.

  6. While your chicken is cooking, chop up your vegetables. Small bite-sized pieces work best.

  7. Start the water boiling for your noodles, cook them according to package directions.

  8. When the chicken is cooked through, remove it from the pan and set it aside on a plate.

  9. Add the other tablespoon of oil to your hot pan.

  10. Add the vegetables and cook until they are crisp-tender.

  11. While your veggies are cooking, mix all Teriyaki Sauce ingredients together in a medium-sized bowl. Combine the cold water and corn starch first, then add the rest of the ingredients.

  12. Once your vegetables are cooked, remove them from the pan and set them aside with the chicken.

  13. Pour your Teriyaki Sauce into the hot pan. Whisk it around until it thickens up. This should only take about 3 minutes.

  14. Place hot noodles in a bowl, top with the chicken and vegetables. Pour the teriyaki sauce on top and enjoy!

Tex-Mex Beef & Potatoes

Ingredients

  • 1 lb Ground Beef
  • 1 Red Bell Pepper, chopped
  • ½ Yellow Onion, chopped
  • 1 pkg. Taco Seasoning Mix
  • ½ cup Water
  • 4 cups Frozen cubed Southern Style Hash Browns, thawed
  • 15 oz can Corn, drained
  • 8 oz Velveeta, cut into cubes

Instructions

  1. In a large skillet on medium heat, brown meat with peppers and onions.
  2. Drain grease.
  3. Add taco seasoning, water, potatoes, and corn. Mix well and cover.
  4. Cook for 7-10 minutes or until potatoes are tender.
  5. Stir in Velveeta; cook uncovered, until melted.
  6. Serve and enjoy!

Main Course |

Vegetable Lasagna

Ingredients

  • Alfredo Sauce:

    • 1 stick Butter
    • 1 1/2 cups Whipping Cream
    • 1 1/2 cups freshly grated Parmesan Cheese
    • 1/2 tsp. White Pepper

  • Ricotta Mixture:

    • 2 cups Ricotta Cheese
    • 1/3 cup freshly grated Parmesan Cheese
    • 3/4 cup Mozzarella Cheese, shredded
    • 2 Green Onions, chopped
    • 1/2 tsp. Salt
    • 1/2 tsp. Black Pepper
    • 1/4 tsp. dried Basil
    • 1 1/2 cups of the prepared Alfredo Sauce

  • Vegetables:

    • 2 cups Chopped Carrots
    • 1 small Yellow Onion, chopped
    • 1 Red Bell Pepper, chopped
    • 1 large Zucchini, chopped
    • 1 large head Broccoli, chopped
    • 1 TB Butter

  • 18 slices Mozzarella Cheese

  • 1 pkg. Lasagna Noodles

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 375 degrees.

  2. Cook lasagna noodles according to package directions. Drain and set aside.

  3. In a heavy skillet, melt butter. Add cream and pepper and combine.

  4. Stir in parmesan cheese and cook until melted and blended.

  5. Remove from heat, set aside and let cool.

  6. In a large mixing bowl, combine ricotta cheese, 1/3 cup parmesan cheese, 3/4 cup shredded mozzarella cheese, green onions, salt, pepper and basil. Add 1 1/2 cups of the cooled alfredo sauce. Mix well and set aside.

  7. In a heavy skillet, melt 1 TB butter. Add carrots and onions. Cook until tender. Add remaining vegetables, mix well. Remove from heat.

  8. Grease a 9x13 baking dish.

  9. Spoon some of the leftover alfredo sauce into the bottom of the baking dish.

  10. Top with Lasagna noodles.

  11. Spread 1 1/4 cup of the ricotta mixture over the noodles.

  12. Top with half of the vegetable mixture and spread out evenly.

  13. Cover the vegetable layer with 9 slices of mozzarella cheese.

  14. Repeat layering - noodles, ricotta, vegetables and mozzarella.

  15. Finish by layering with lasagna noodles, the remaining ricotta mixture and topping with the remaining alfredo sauce.

  16. Cover with nonstick foil. Bake for about 1 hour.

  17. Remove from the oven and let stand for 5 minutes, covered, before cutting and serving.

  18. Enjoy!

Main Course |

White Mac and Cheese

Ingredients

  • 16 oz macaroni pasta (any shape you like)
  • 1/2 cup butter
  • 1 tsp salt
  • 1/2 tsp pepper
  • 1/2 cup flour
  • 3 1/2 cups milk, warmed
  • 1/4 cup chicken broth
  • 1/2 lb White Queso Velveeta cheese, cubed
  • 1/2 lb cheddar, cubed
  • 1/2 cup panko bread crumbs
  • 1 tbsp parsley
  • 2 tbsp butter, melted

Instructions

  1. Preheat the oven to 350°F.

  2. Spray a 9x13 baking dish with cooking spray.

  3. Cook pasta for about 2-3 minutes less than the package instructions, drain, and set aside.

  4. In a saucepan, melt 1/2 cup butter with salt and pepper. Once melted, add flour and stir until smooth.

  5. Let cook for 1 minute. Add milk and chicken broth, bring to a boil while whisking. Add cheeses and stir until melted.

  6. Pour over cooked pasta and add to greased dish.

  7. Combine panko, parsley, and melted butter in a small bowl.

  8. Sprinkle macaroni with panko topping, and bake uncovered for 30 minutes.

Pies

Pies |

Basic Pastry for 10" Pie

Ingredients

  • 1 1/2 cups all-purpose flour
  • 3/4 tsp salt
  • 1/2 cup shortening (Crisco)
  • 4 – 5 TB ice water

Instructions

In large mixing bowl, combine flour and salt; cut in shortening with a pastry blender until mixture is crumbly. Sprinkle ice water, 1 TB at a time, evenly over surface; stir with a fork until dry ingredients are moistened. Shape into a ball; cover and chill until ready to use.

To roll out pastry:
Roll pastry to 1/8-inch thickness on a lightly floured surface. Place in pie plate; trim off excess pastry along edges. Fold edges under, and crimp. Chill.

For baked pastry shell:
Prick bottom and sides of pastry shell with a fork. Chill pastry until ready to bake. Brush with egg wash. Bake at 450 degrees for 10 – 12 minutes or until golden.

To make egg wash:
Mix 1 egg and 1/4 cup of water.

Pies |

Caramel Apple Pie

Ingredients

  • 2 Pillsbury Pie Crusts (in the Dairy Section)

Filling:

  • 6 Granny Smith Apples
  • 1 TB Lemon Juice
  • 1/2 cup Brown Sugar
  • 1/2 cup Sugar
  • 1/4 cup Flour
  • 1 tsp Cinnamon
  • 1/4 tsp Nutmeg
  • 1/4 tsp Salt
  • 1 tsp Vanilla
  • 4 TB Whipping Cream
  • 4 TB Butter

Streusel Topping:

  • 1/2 cup Flour

  • 3 TB Sugar

  • 1 TB Butter

  • 2 Toffee Bars (scrape off chocolate and crush)

  • 1 beaten egg white

  • 1 TB Sugar

Instructions

Preheat oven to 450 degrees.

Pie Crust:
Follow directions for 2 crust pie.

Filling:

  1. Peel, core, and slice apples. Place in bowl and sprinkle with lemon juice. Set aside.

  2. In a large mixing bowl, combine brown sugar, sugar, flour, cinnamon, nutmeg, and salt. Add apples and toss. Add vanilla and whipping cream and toss.

  3. In a large skillet, melt butter over medium heat. Add apple mixture and cook for 8 minutes, to soften apples. Pour filling into pie shell.

Streusel Topping:

  1. In a small mixing bowl, combine flour and sugar. Mix in butter with fork until coarse crumbs, stir in toffee chips. Sprinkle over pie.

  2. Add top crust. Seal edges and vent top. Brush with beaten egg white and sprinkle with sugar. Bake for 15 minutes. Reduce heat to 350 degrees and bake an additional 45 minutes.

'Now may the God of hope fill you with all joy and peace in believing, that you may abound in hope by the power of the Holy Spirit' Romans 15:13

Pies |

Choc Chip Pie Mix in a Jar

Ingredients

  • 1 cup sugar
  • ½ cup flour
  • 3/4 cup chocolate chips
  • ½ cup coconut
  • ½ cup chopped pecans
  • 1 quart jar – wide mouth
  • 1/4 cup melted butter
  • 2 large eggs
  • 9" pie crust

Instructions

  1. Layer sugar then flour in jar.

  2. Place a plastic bag inside the jar and fill with chocolate chips, pecans, and coconut.

  3. Tie the bag and place the lid on the jar.

  4. Attach the following instructions on a gift tag:

    Chocolate Chip Pie

    In a large mixing bowl, combine ¼ cup melted butter and 2 large eggs with the sugar and flour from the jar. Stir until moist. Add the chocolate chip package and mix well. Spoon into a 9” unbaked pie shell and bake at 350 degrees for 35-40 minutes. Serve with ice cream or whipping cream. Enjoy!

Be joyful always; pray continually; give thanks in all circumstances, for this is God's will for you in Christ Jesus
I Thessalonians 5:16-18

Pies |

Chocolate Banana Cream Pie

Ingredients

  • 8 oz Cream Cheese, softened
  • 1 cup Powdered Sugar
  • 8 oz Cool Whip
  • 2 Bananas, sliced
  • 1 small pkg. Instant Chocolate Pudding
  • 1 ½ cups Cold Milk
  • ½ cup chopped Pecans
  • 1 9” Baked Pie Crust

Instructions

  1. In a medium mixing bowl, beat together cream cheese and powdered sugar, until smooth.

  2. Spread into the bottom of the pie shell.

  3. In a medium mixing bowl, beat together chocolate pudding mix and cold milk. Beat for 2 minutes until it thickens. Set aside.

  4. Spread a layer of Cool Whip onto cream cheese mixture.

  5. Lay sliced bananas on top of the Cool Whip.

  6. Spread the chocolate pudding on top of the banana layer.

  7. Top with Cool Whip and sprinkle with chopped pecans.

  8. Refrigerate at least 3 hours before serving.

Enjoy!

“I will praise You, O LORD with my whole heart; I will tell of all Your marvelous works. I will be glad and rejoice in Your name, O Most High.”
Psalm 9:1-2

Pies |

Fresh Fruit Tart

Ingredients

Crust:

  • 2 cups crushed graham crackers or ginger snaps
  • 2 tablespoons sugar
  • 1/3 cup melted butter

Filling:

  • 8 oz cream cheese, softened
  • 1/4 cup sugar
  • 2 teaspoons lemon juice
  • 1/2 cup whipping cream

Topping:

  • Assorted fruit: strawberries, blueberries, raspberries, grapes, kiwi
  • 1/4 cup apricot preserves
  • 1 tablespoon water

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 350 degrees.

For crust:

  • In a medium bowl, combine ground crumbs, sugar, and melted butter. Mix well until a crumbly mixture forms.
  • Press into a pizza pan or 6 individual tart pans.
  • Bake for 8 minutes. Let cool.

For filling:

  • Combine cream cheese, sugar, and lemon juice with an electric mixer.
  • Add whipping cream and beat until light and fluffy.
  • Spread into cooled crust and chill for several hours.

For topping:

  • Arrange sliced fruit on top of filling.
  • Combine apricot preserves and water, and brush over top of fruit.
  • Enjoy!
Pies |

Key Lime Pie

Ingredients

  • 2 cans sweetened condensed milk
  • ¾ cup key lime juice (or lime juice)
  • ½ cup sour cream
  • 1 TB lime zest
  • 1 prepared pie crust

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 350 degrees.

  2. In a medium mixing bowl, combine condensed milk, sour cream, lime juice, and zest.

  3. Mix well and pour into pie crust.

  4. Bake for 8-12 minutes, until tiny pinhole bubbles burst on the surface of the pie.

  5. Do not brown.

  6. Chill pie thoroughly before serving.

  7. Garnish with lime slices and whipped cream. Enjoy!

Psalm 9:1-2

Pies |

Pecan Pie Tartlets

Ingredients

  • 12 oz cream cheese, softened

  • 2 cups butter, softened

  • 3 1/2 cups flour

  • 2 TB butter, softened

  • 1 1/2 cup brown sugar

  • 1 tsp vanilla

  • Dash of salt

  • 2 eggs

  • 1 1/2 cups crushed pecans

Instructions

Preheat oven to 350 degrees.

  1. In a large mixing bowl, combine cream cheese, 2 cups butter, and flour. Blend until mixed well. Roll into 1" balls. Press into mini muffin tins. (Do not grease or use muffin liners)

  2. In another large mixing bowl, mix 2 TB butter, brown sugar, vanilla, salt, eggs, and crushed pecans. Using a tsp, fill each muffin tin.

  3. Bake 15-20 minutes or until edges are golden brown.

  4. Let cool for 2 minutes and remove from muffin tins. Enjoy!

Pies |

Pumpkin Pie

Ingredients

  • 15 oz can Pumpkin
  • 1 can sweetened condensed milk
  • 2 large eggs
  • 1 tsp cinnamon
  • ½ tsp ginger
  • ½ tsp nutmeg
  • ½ tsp salt
  • 1 pie crust

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 425 degrees.

  2. In a large mixing bowl, combine pumpkin, condensed milk, eggs, cinnamon, ginger, nutmeg and salt.

  3. Beat until smooth.

  4. Pour into pie crust.

  5. Bake 15 minutes.

  6. Reduce oven temperature to 350 degrees and continue baking 35-40 minutes or until knife inserted 1 inch from crust comes out clean.

  7. Cool. Top with whipped cream and enjoy!

  8. Store leftovers in refrigerator.

“I will praise You, O LORD with my whole heart; I will tell of all Your marvelous works. I will be glad and rejoice in Your name, O Most High.” Psalm 9:1-2

Salads |

Autumn Salad

Ingredients

  • 1 pkg Leafy Romaine Lettuce
  • 1 granny smith Apple, peeled, cored and sliced
  • 1 small Red Onion, thinly sliced
  • 1 pkg Blue Cheese, crumbled
  • Sugared Walnuts*
  • Maple Cider Vinaigrette**

*Sugared Walnuts: Ingredients-

  • 6 oz Walnut pieces
  • 2 tbsp melted Butter
  • 3 tbsp Sugar
  • 1/4 tsp ground Ginger
  • Pinch Curry Powder
  • 1/4 tsp Cinnamon
  • Pinch Salt
  • Pinch ground Red Pepper

**Maple Cider Vinaigrette: Ingredients-

  • 1/3 cup Apple Cider Vinegar
  • 2 tbsp Pure Maple Syrup
  • 1 tbsp Dijon Mustard
  • 1/4 tsp Salt
  • 1/4 tsp Pepper
  • 2/3 cup Olive or Vegetable Oil

Instructions

*Sugared Walnuts-
In a medium mixing bowl, toss walnuts in melted butter. In a small mixing bowl, stir together sugar, ginger, curry, cinnamon, salt, and red pepper. Sprinkle over walnuts; toss to coat.
Spread in a single layer on baking sheet. Bake at 350 degrees for 10 minutes. Cool pan on wire rack. Store in airtight container for up to 1 week.

**Maple Cider Vinaigrette-
In a medium mixing bowl, whisk together apple cider vinegar, maple syrup, mustard, salt and pepper. Gradually whisk in oil until completely blended. Cover and refrigerate for up to 3 days.

Salad -
Combine lettuce, apple slices, onion slices, blue cheese and walnuts. Toss with dressing. Serve and enjoy!

'Salt is good, but if it loses its saltiness, how can you make it salty again? Have salt in yourselves, and be at peace with each other.' Mark 9:50

BBQ Ranch Salad

Ingredients

  • 2 bags of your favorite prepared salad mix or make your own
  • 1/2 bunch green onion, chopped
  • 1 red pepper, chopped
  • 1 can black beans, drained
  • 1 can corn, drained
  • 2 cups cheddar cheese, grated
  • 1 small bag Frito corn chips

Dressing:

  • 1 1/2 cups ranch dressing
  • 3/4 cup your favorite BBQ sauce

Instructions

In a large bowl, combine lettuce, green onions, red pepper, black beans, and corn.

If you are serving immediately, go ahead and add the cheese and Fritos.

In a medium mixing bowl, combine ranch dressing and BBQ sauce.

Pour over salad and toss.

Serve and enjoy!

If you have grilled chicken, cut it into bite-size pieces and add when you add the cheese and Fritos!

Salads |

Broccoli & Tortellini Salad

Ingredients

  • 6 slices of bacon
  • 3 heads fresh broccoli, chopped
  • 20 oz fresh cheese-filled tortellini
  • 1 cup mayonnaise
  • 1/2 cup sugar
  • 2 tsp apple cider vinegar
  • 1 cup raisins
  • 1 cup sunflower seeds
  • 1 red onion, diced

Instructions

In a skillet or microwave, cook bacon until evenly brown. Drain, crumble and set aside.

Bring a large pot of slightly salted water to a boil. Cook tortellini according to package directions (about 8-10 minutes). Drain and rinse under cold water.

In a small bowl, combine mayonnaise, sugar, and vinegar to make the dressing.

In a large bowl, combine broccoli, tortellini, bacon, raisins, sunflower seeds, and red onion. Pour dressing over salad and toss. Serve and enjoy. Tastes even better if made at least 1 hour ahead!

For the joy of the Lord is your strength.
Nehemiah 8:10

Chicken Pesto Pasta Salad

Ingredients

  • 16 oz pkg Bow Tie Pasta
  • 2 (4.5 oz) jars Pesto
  • 5 oz pkg Shredded Parmesan Cheese
  • 10 oz pkg Butterball Grilled Chicken Strips

Instructions

  1. Cook pasta according to package directions.
  2. While pasta is cooking, cut chicken strips into bite-size pieces.
  3. When pasta is ready, rinse and add pesto, chicken, and Parmesan cheese. Mix all together.
  4. Serve and enjoy!
  5. Super easy and delicious!
