Salads |

Chinese Chicken Salad

Ingredients

Ingredients for Dressing:

  • 3 TB hoisin sauce
  • 1 TB peanut butter
  • 2 teaspoons brown sugar
  • 3/4 teaspoon hot chile sauce
  • 1 teaspoon ginger
  • 3 tablespoons rice wine vinegar
  • 1 tablespoon sesame oil

Ingredients for Salad:

  • 2 boneless chicken breast halves, pounded flat
  • 16 (3.5 inch square) wonton wrappers, cut in small slices
  • 1 head Romaine lettuce - torn, washed, and dried
  • 2 carrots, shredded or chopped
  • 1 bunch green onions, chopped
  • 1 pkg. cole slaw (broccoli slaw works well too)
  • 16oz can mandarin oranges, drained (reserve some juice to thin the dressing)
  • 1 cup slivered almonds

Instructions

  1. To prepare the dressing, whisk together the hoisin sauce, peanut butter, brown sugar, chili paste, ginger, vinegar, and sesame oil.

  2. Add juice from mandarin oranges to thin if needed.

  3. Season chicken breasts with salt and pepper.

  4. In a large skillet, add 2 TB olive oil.

  5. Place chicken breasts in the pan. Cook until done (about 7 minutes on each side).

  6. Preheat oven to 350 degrees F (175 C). Spray a large shallow pan with nonstick vegetable spray, arrange sliced wontons in a single layer and bake until golden brown (about 7-10 minutes). Cool.

  7. In a large bowl, combine the chicken, wontons, lettuce, carrots, green onions, oranges, and almonds.

  8. Toss with dressing and serve. Enjoy!

"Why do you call me, ‘Lord, Lord,’ and do not do what I say? As for everyone who comes to me and hears my words and puts them into practice, I will show you what they are like. They are like a man building a house, who dug down deep and laid the foundation on rock. When a flood came, the torrent struck that house but could not shake it, because it was well built." Luke 6:46-48

Salads |

Crunchy Broccoli Romaine

Ingredients

  • 1 Head Romaine Lettuce
  • 2 small heads of Broccoli, chopped
  • 4 green onions, chopped
  • 1/2 cup chopped walnuts
  • 1 pkg. Ramen Noodles, broken (don't use the seasoning package)
  • 2 TB Butter
  • 1/2 cup Sugar
  • 1/2 cup Vegetable Oil
  • 1/4 cup Apple Cider Vinegar
  • 2 tsp Soy Sauce
  • Salt and pepper to taste

Instructions

In a skillet over medium heat, melt butter and add broken ramen noodles. Toss and stir until golden. Remove from heat and let cool.

In a jar with a tight fitting lid, combine the sugar, oil, vinegar, soy sauce, salt, and pepper. Shake well. Refrigerate until ready to serve.

In a large bowl, toss broccoli, romaine, onions, walnuts, and ramen noodles.

Toss with dressing before serving.

Enjoy!

Salads |

Crunchy Broccoli Salad

Ingredients

  • 1 head Romaine Lettuce, chopped
  • 1 bunch Green Onions, chopped
  • 1 large head of Broccoli, cut into small pieces
  • 1 lb bacon, cooked and cut into small pieces
  • 2-3 oz pkgs Ramen Noodles, crushed (discard seasoning packet)
  • 4 TB Butter, melted
  • 1 cup Pecans, chopped or sliced almonds

Dressing:

  • 1 cup Vegetable Oil
  • 1/2 cup Red Wine Vinegar
  • 1 cup Sugar
  • 1 1/2 TB Soy Sauce

Instructions

  1. In a large mixing bowl, combine melted butter, nuts, and crushed noodles.

  2. Spread on a cookie sheet and bake at 350 degrees until lightly browned, stirring occasionally - about 10 minutes.

  3. Set aside to cool completely.

  4. Cut bacon into small pieces and fry in a large skillet.

  5. Remove from pan and place on paper towel to remove extra grease.

  6. To make dressing: Using a large mason jar, combine oil, vinegar, sugar, and soy sauce. Shake to combine. Refrigerate until ready to use.

  7. In a large salad bowl, combine broccoli, onions, romaine, bacon, and nut mixture. Toss with dressing right before serving.

  8. Enjoy!

Salads |

Crunchy Broccoli Slaw

Ingredients

  • 2 Bags Ramen Noodle Oriental flavor
  • ¾ stick Butter
  • ½ cup Slivered Almonds
  • ½ cup Sunflower Seeds
  • 4 Green Onions, chopped (optional)
  • 1 bag Craisins

Dressing:

  • ½ cup Canola Oil
  • ½ cup Brown Sugar
  • 1/3 cup apple cider Vinegar
  • 2 packets ramen noodle seasoning

Instructions

Crush the ramen noodles while they are in the bag. Melt butter over medium heat in a large skillet. Add the crushed noodles and slivered almonds and sauté until golden. (Watch carefully because they go from not done to burned really quickly.) Remove from heat.

In a mixing bowl, whisk all the ingredients of the dressing together.

In a large serving bowl, combine broccoli slaw, noodles, almonds, sunflower seeds, craisins, and onions.

Toss with dressing and serve. Enjoy! (Once you toss with dressing, you will want to serve it. The noodles get soggy if left overnight. The flavor is still good but you might not get the crunch you were expecting!)

Salads |

Crunchy Romaine Toss and Sweet & Sour Dressing

Ingredients

Salad:

  • 1 cup chopped pecans
  • 1 pkg Ramen noodles, broken up (discard flavor packet)
  • 4 Tbsp butter
  • 4 green onions, chopped
  • 1 bunch broccoli, chopped
  • 2 bunches romaine lettuce, torn apart

Sweet and Sour Dressing:

  • 1 cup vegetable oil
  • 1/2 cup wine vinegar
  • 3 tsp soy sauce
  • 1 cup sugar
  • Salt and pepper to taste

Instructions

Directions for Salad:

  1. In a large skillet, brown noodles and pecans in butter (noodles take longer).
  2. Cool on paper towel.
  3. Chop onions and broccoli.
  4. In a large bowl, combine onions, broccoli, romaine lettuce, cooled pecans, and noodles.
  5. Toss with dressing and serve. Enjoy!

Directions for Dressing:

  1. In a medium mixing bowl, combine wine vinegar, soy sauce, and sugar; whisk together.
  2. Add oil and whisk vigorously.
  3. Add salt and pepper to taste if desired.
  4. Toss with salad and enjoy!

Salads |

Fall Fruits Salad with Lemon Poppyseed Dressing

Ingredients

  • ½ cup sugar
  • ½ cup lemon juice
  • 2 tsp diced onion
  • 1 tsp Dijon mustard
  • ½ tsp salt
  • 2/3 cup vegetable oil
  • 1 tbsp poppy seeds

Salad Ingredients:

  • 1 head romaine lettuce, washed and torn into bite-sized pieces
  • 1 ½ cup shredded mozzarella cheese
  • 1 cup chopped walnuts (or any other nut)
  • ¼ cup dried cranberries
  • 1 apple, peeled, cored and diced
  • 1 pear, peeled, cored and diced

Instructions

Dressing Instructions:
In a food processor or blender, combine sugar, lemon juice, onion, mustard, and salt. Process until well blended. With machine running, add oil in a slow steady stream until mixture is thick and smooth. Add poppy seeds and process a few seconds more.

Salad Instructions:
In a large serving bowl, combine the romaine lettuce, shredded cheese, nuts, cranberries, apples, and pears. Toss to mix then pour dressing over salad just before serving and toss to coat.

Psalm 118:8

Salads |

Festive Slaw

Ingredients

  • 1 pkg Cole Slaw
  • 1 1/2 cups dried Cranberries
  • 1 bunch Green Onion, chopped

Dressing:

  • 1/2 cup Mayonnaise
  • 1 TB sweet Relish
  • 1 TB Dijon Mustard
  • 2 TB Honey
  • 1/2 tsp Salt
  • 1 tsp Pepper

Instructions

  1. In a large bowl, combine the slaw, cranberries, and green onion.

  2. In a smaller bowl, combine the dressing ingredients.

  3. Mix well and toss over slaw mixture.

  4. Refrigerate at least 6 hours before serving.

Enjoy!

Fruit Salad

Ingredients

  • 1 can pineapple chunks, drained but save the juice
  • 2 apples, cored, peeled, and cut into bite-size pieces
  • 1 can mandarin oranges, drained
  • 1 pint strawberries, cut into bite-size pieces
  • 2 bananas, sliced into bite-size pieces
  • 1 small box sugar-free instant vanilla pudding

Instructions

  1. Drain and save juice from canned pineapple.

  2. Combine all fruit.

  3. In a small bowl, add pineapple juice to pudding until thickened.

  4. Pour over fruit.

  5. Serve and enjoy!

Salads |

Fruit Salad with Honey Dressing

Ingredients

  • 1/3 cup Honey
  • 1/4 cup Orange Juice
  • 1/4 cup Canola Oil
  • 1/2 tsp fresh Lemon Juice, plus the juice of 1 Lemon
  • 1 1/2 tsp Poppy seeds
  • 1/4 tsp Salt
  • 1/4 tsp Mustard
  • 2 Apples, 1 green – 1 red, cored and diced
  • 1 banana, sliced
  • 11 oz can Mandarin Oranges, drained
  • 1/4 cup dried cranberries
  • 1/4 cup chopped Walnuts or Pecans

(You can add other fruits) we’ll add raspberries tonight!

Instructions

  1. Combine honey, orange juice, oil, 1/2 tsp lemon juice, poppy seeds, salt, and mustard in a bowl with a whisk. Set aside.

  2. In a large bowl, toss the apples and bananas with the juice of 1 lemon to prevent the fruit from turning brown.

  3. Combine the fruit, cranberries, and nuts. Add the dressing and stir gently.

  4. Serve and enjoy!

“And so we know and rely on the love God has for us. God is love. Whoever lives in love lives in God and God in him.” 1 John 4:16

Grape Salad

Ingredients

  • 1 large bunch of red grapes (sliced)
  • 1 8 oz package cream cheese (softened)
  • 1 7 oz jar marshmallow cream
  • 1 6 oz package walnut pieces

Instructions

  1. Wash and slice grapes.

  2. In a large bowl, mix cream cheese and marshmallow cream.

  3. Add grapes and walnut pieces. Mix well.

  4. Refrigerate before serving.

Serves many and will keep for several days.

My niece, Megan, made this but added apples with the grapes and said it was wonderful! Be creative and add other fruits but remember they may not keep as well as the grapes.

Salads |

Jenny's Orzo Salad with Feta Cheese

Ingredients

  • 1 cup uncooked orzo
  • 1/2 cup thinly sliced green onions
  • 1/2 cup (2 ounces) crumbled feta cheese
  • 1/4 cup chopped fresh dill
  • 1 (19 ounce) can chickpeas (garbanzo beans), drained
  • 3 tablespoons fresh lemon juice
  • 1 1/2 tablespoons extra-virgin olive oil
  • 1 tablespoon cold water
  • 1/8 teaspoon salt
  • 2-3 teaspoons minced garlic

Instructions

  1. Cook pasta according to package directions, omitting salt and fat. Drain and rinse with cold water, then drain again.

  2. Combine pasta, onions, cheese, dill, and chickpeas in a large bowl, tossing gently to combine.

  3. Combine juice and remaining ingredients in a small bowl, stirring with a whisk. Drizzle over pasta mixture and toss gently to coat.

Yield: 4 servings (serving size 1 1/4 cups)

You can serve immediately, but it tastes best when it's been in the refrigerator for a couple of hours or a day to get really cold and marinate. You can serve with pita bread (I like wheat pita) and extra feta cheese crumbled on top—however you like. Also, using fresh dill tastes much better than dried dill.

ENJOY THE SUNSHINE AND THE ORZO!

Thank you, Jenny!

'My salvation and honor depend on God; He is my rock, my refuge.' Psalm 62:7

Salads |

Lori's Potato Salad

Ingredients

  • 4 Baking Potatoes
  • 1 bunch Green Onions, diced
  • 1 cup Dill Pickle relish
  • 1 ½ cups Hellmann’s Mayonnaise
  • 2 pkgs Hidden Valley Ranch Dressing Mix
  • 6 Eggs, hard-boiled, peeled and chopped

To boil eggs – place in a saucepan and cover with water. Bring to a boil, turn off heat and place lid on pan. Let sit for 15 minutes. Eggs will be done.

Instructions

  1. Peel and cut up potatoes. Place in a saucepan and cover with water. Bring to a boil and cook until tender when pierced with a fork. Pour off water and place back on burner for a couple of minutes (this makes sure all water is gone).

  2. In a large bowl, combine potatoes, eggs, green onions, dill relish, and Ranch dressing mix. Add mayonnaise and mix well.

  3. Chill before serving. Enjoy!

Salads |

Mexi Chicken & Black Bean Salad

Ingredients

  • 4 skinless, boneless chicken breast halves
  • 2 Tbsp olive oil
  • 1 ½ cups salsa
  • 1 (1 ounce) packet taco seasoning
  • 1 (15.5 ounce) can black beans, drained
  • 1 can Mexican corn, drained
  • 4 cups baby spinach leaves or any salad mixture

Toppings:

  • Sour cream
  • Shredded Colby Jack cheese
  • Avocado slices
  • Red onion, chopped
  • Salsa

Instructions

  1. Cut chicken into strips.
  2. Heat olive oil in a skillet over medium-high heat.
  3. Add chicken; season with taco seasoning mix and 1/2 cup of salsa.
  4. Cook until chicken is done; about 8 minutes.
  5. Add 1 cup salsa, black beans, and corn.
  6. Reduce heat to medium and cook for about 5 minutes or until mixture is heated.
  7. While mixture is cooking, prepare salad base.
  8. Remove chicken and add to salad.
  9. Top with your favorite toppings - Enjoy!

Salads |

No-Mayo Pasta Salad

Ingredients

  • 16 oz linguini noodles (you can use any pasta but spaghetti and linguini seem to be preferred)
  • 5 cups assorted vegetables, chopped (I recommend):
    • 1 red onion
    • 1 red bell pepper
    • 1 zucchini
    • 1 head of broccoli
    • 1 box grape tomatoes, cut in half (add the tomatoes right before serving)
  • 1 8 oz bottle Wish-Bone Italian Dressing
  • 1 2.6 oz bottle of McCormick Salad Supreme Dressing

Instructions

  1. Cook pasta according to package directions.

  2. Chop vegetables into small bite-size pieces, except for the tomatoes - those are added at the end right before serving.

  3. Drain pasta after cooked and rinse with cold water.

  4. Place pasta and vegetables in a large mixing bowl.

  5. Add salad dressing and seasoning.

  6. Toss well to coat.

  7. Refrigerate.

Can be served immediately or made a day ahead.

Enjoy!

Salads |

Orange Poppy Seed Salad

Ingredients

Sugared Almonds:

  • 1 egg white
  • 1/4 cup sugar
  • 1 cup sliced almonds
  • 2 TB butter

Salad Mix:

  • 1 head Bibb lettuce, torn
  • 1 head red or green leaf lettuce, torn
  • 11 oz can mandarin oranges, drained
  • 16 oz container strawberries, sliced thin
  • 1 bunch green onions, chopped

Salad Dressing:

  • 3/4 cup olive oil
  • 1/4 cup red wine vinegar
  • 1 tsp grated orange rind
  • 1 TB orange juice
  • 1/2 tsp poppy seeds
  • 1/8 tsp salt
  • 1/8 tsp pepper

Instructions

Sugared Almonds:

Beat egg white at high speed with an electric mixer until foamy. Add sugar, 1 TB at a time, beating 2-4 minutes or until stiff peaks form and sugar dissolves. Fold in almonds.

Melt butter in a 9" baking dish in a 325-degree oven. Add almonds to pan and bake, stirring every 5 minutes, 20-25 minutes or until lightly browned and dry. Cool in pan on a wire rack.

Salad Mix:

Toss together Bibb lettuce and next 4 ingredients in a large bowl.

Salad Dressing:

In a small mixing bowl, combine vinegar, orange rind, orange juice, poppy seeds, salt, and pepper. Whisk in olive oil.

Drizzle dressing over salad, tossing gently. Sprinkle with sugared almonds and serve immediately.

To make ahead:

Keep almonds, dressing, and salad separate. Combine when ready to eat - will keep up to 3 days.

Enjoy!

Blessed are they who keep his statutes and seek him with all their heart.
Psalm 119:2 NIV

Salads |

Orzo Pasta Salad

Ingredients

  • 4 cups Chicken Broth
  • 1 pkg Orzo (1 1/2 cups)
  • 15oz can Garbanzo beans, drained and rinsed
  • 1 pint grape tomatoes or red/yellow teardrop tomatoes, quartered
  • 1 red onion, chopped
  • 5oz pkg Parmesan Cheese, grated

Red wine vinaigrette:

  • 1/2 cup red wine vinegar
  • 1/4 cup fresh lemon juice
  • 2 tsp Honey
  • 2 tsp salt
  • 1 tsp pepper
  • 3/4 cup olive oil

Instructions

  1. Pour broth into large saucepan. Cover the pan and bring the broth to a boil over high heat. Stir in the Orzo. Cover partially and cook until the orzo is tender but still firm, about 7 minutes.

  2. Pour Orzo into a large wide bowl and toss until the orzo cools slightly. Set aside to cool completely.

  3. Toss the orzo with the beans, tomatoes, onion, cheese, and vinaigrette. Season with salt and pepper. Refrigerate until ready to serve. I think it tastes better made ahead of time. Set it out about 10-15 minutes before serving.

  4. You can add vegetables if you like – zucchini, broccoli, red bell pepper – add them to the hot orzo. It will soften them just a bit. Then add the cheese, beans, onions and toss with the vinaigrette.

To make vinaigrette:
Combine all ingredients. I make mine in a dressing bottle - once the ingredients are combined - I close the lid and shake to mix.

Salads |

Pasta Salad

Ingredients

  • 1 (12 oz) pkg pasta (any kind)
  • 1 small head broccoli florets (cut into bite-size pieces)
  • 1/4 cup chopped red onion
  • 1/2 cup grated parmesan cheese
  • 1 cup Italian salad dressing
  • 1 (6.5 oz) jar artichoke hearts (drained and chopped)

Instructions

  1. Bring a large pot of lightly salted water to a boil. Add pasta and cook following package directions.

  2. Wash and cut up broccoli while pasta is cooking. Add broccoli to pasta water for the final 2 minutes of cooking time.

  3. Drain pasta and broccoli and rinse with cold water until cool.

  4. Drain and chop the artichoke hearts. Chop the red onion.

  5. In a large bowl, combine artichoke hearts, parmesan cheese, salad dressing, and red onion. Add pasta and broccoli.

  6. Refrigerate or serve immediately. Serves 6.

'I will praise you, O Lord, with all my heart; I will tell of all your wonders. I will be glad and rejoice in you; I will sing praise to your name, O Most High.'
Psalm 9:1-2

Salads |

Sesame Chicken Pasta Salad

Ingredients

  • 1/2 cup (2 oz) sesame seeds
  • 16 oz bow tie pasta
  • 1/4 cup olive oil
  • 1/3 cup soy sauce
  • 1/3 cup rice vinegar
  • 3 tbsp white sugar
  • 1/2 tsp ground ginger
  • 1/4 tsp black pepper
  • 1 rotisserie chicken or 4 cooked chicken breasts, shredded
  • 2 carrots, shredded
  • 1 head of broccoli, chopped
  • 1/2 red bell pepper, chopped
  • 1/3 cup green onion, chopped
  • 1/4 cup fresh cilantro, chopped (optional)

Instructions

  1. Heat a skillet over medium-high heat. Add sesame seeds, cook stirring frequently until lightly toasted. (watch because they burn quickly!)

  2. Remove from heat, and set aside.

  3. Bring a large pot of lightly salted water to a boil. Add pasta, and cook for 8-10 minutes or until al dente. Drain pasta, and rinse under cold water until cool. Transfer to a large bowl.

  4. In a jar with a tight-fitting lid, combine olive oil, soy sauce, vinegar, sugar, sesame seeds, ginger and pepper. Shake well.

  5. Pour sesame dressing over pasta and toss to coat evenly.

  6. Gently mix in chicken, carrots, broccoli, red bell pepper, green onions, and cilantro.

  7. Serve and enjoy!

For great is your love, reaching to the heavens; your faithfulness reaches to the skies.
Psalm 57:10 NIV

Seven Layer Salad with a Twist

Ingredients

  • ½ cup Plain Yogurt
  • ½ cup Buttermilk
  • ½ cup Mayonnaise
  • 4 oz Crumbled Feta Cheese
  • 1 tsp Sugar
  • ½ tsp Dill Weed
  • 9 oz pkg Cheese Tortellini
  • 1 pkg Baby Spinach, chopped
  • 2 cups Red Cabbage, chopped
  • 8 oz Havarti Cheese, cubed
  • 2 cups Grape Tomatoes
  • 8 slices cooked Bacon
  • 1/2 cup Parmesan Cheese, freshly grated

Instructions

In a mixing bowl, combine yogurt, buttermilk and mayonnaise. Beat well. Stir in feta cheese, sugar and dill weed. Refrigerate.

Cook tortellini as directed on package. Drain tortellini and stir into dressing. Set aside.

In a large glass bowl, layer spinach and then cabbage.

Next, layer tortellini.

Next, Havarti cheese cubes.

Next, grape tomatoes.

Next, bacon.

Top with parmesan cheese.

Chill overnight.

Enjoy!

"Call to me and I will answer you and tell you great and unsearchable things you do not know." Jeremiah 33:3

Salads |

Southwest Caesar Salad

Ingredients

  • 4 skinless, boneless chicken breasts
  • 1 pkg. dry fajita mix
  • 1 cup mayonnaise
  • ½ cup sour cream
  • 2/3 pkg. Parmesan cheese, cut in cubes
  • 4 green onions
  • 1 TB Dijon mustard
  • 2 chipotle peppers, from a can
  • 1 ½ tsp. minced garlic
  • 1 tsp. Worcestershire sauce
  • 1 head Romaine lettuce, rinsed
  • 1/3 pkg. Parmesan cheese, shredded
  • Tortilla chips, broken into bite-size pieces
  • Red bell pepper, sliced into thin rings
  • 1 can black beans, drained and rinsed
  • 1 can Mexicorn or regular corn, drained

Instructions

  1. Flatten chicken breasts between 2 sheets of freezer paper.

  2. Season with dry fajita seasoning mix.

  3. Spray a skillet with Pam and heat pan to medium high.

  4. Cook chicken on each side until no longer pink (about 4 minutes on each side).

  5. In a blender or food processor, combine mayonnaise, sour cream, parmesan cheese, green onions, Dijon mustard, chipotle peppers, garlic, and Worcestershire sauce. Blend until smooth.

  6. Tear romaine into bite-size pieces.

  7. Place into serving bowl or individual servings.

  8. Top with shredded parmesan cheese, tortilla chips, bell pepper, black beans, and corn.

  9. Arrange chicken on top.

  10. Top with dressing. Enjoy!

“Train yourself to be godly. For physical training is of some value, but godliness has value for all things, holding promise for both the present life and the life to come.” 1 Timothy 4:7-8

Spring Salad Citrus Dressing

Ingredients

  • ¾ cup Walnut pieces
  • 1 Bag mixed Salad Greens
  • 1 can Mandarin Oranges, drained or 2 Large Navel Oranges, peeled and sectioned
  • ½ Red Onion, sliced
  • ½ cup Vegetable Oil
  • 2/3 cup Orange Juice
  • ¼ cup Sugar
  • 2 TB Balsamic Vinegar
  • 2 tsp. Dijon Mustard
  • ¼ tsp. Dried Oregano
  • ¼ tsp. Black Pepper

Instructions

  1. Place walnuts in a skillet. Over medium heat, cook for 5 minutes, stirring constantly until lightly browned.

  2. In a jar with a tight fitting lid, combine orange juice, sugar, vinegar, mustard, oregano, pepper, and oil.

  3. In a large bowl, toss the toasted walnuts, salad greens, oranges, and red onion. Sprinkle feta cheese on top.

  4. Drizzle dressing over the salad and serve.

Salads |

Strawberry Jello Salad

Ingredients

  • 2 cups crushed pretzels

  • 3/4 cup butter, melted

  • 1 tablespoon sugar

  • 1 (8 oz) package cream cheese, softened

  • 1 cup sugar

  • 1 (9 oz) carton Cool Whip, softened

  • 1 large box strawberry Jello

  • 2 cups boiling water

  • 2 frozen packages sliced strawberries

Instructions

Crust:

  1. In a large bowl, combine pretzels, butter, and sugar.
  2. Press into a 9x13 baking dish.
  3. Bake at 400 degrees for 8 minutes.
  4. Remove and chill.

Filling:

  1. In a large mixing bowl, combine cream cheese, 1 cup sugar, and Cool Whip.
  2. Spread onto crust.

Topping:

  1. Bring 2 cups of water to a boil.
  2. Add jello, mix until dissolved.
  3. Add frozen strawberries, chill until it starts to thicken.
  4. Pour over cream cheese filling and then refrigerate.

Enjoy!

'And let us consider how we may spur one another on toward love and good deeds.'
Hebrews 10:24

Strawberry Spinach Salad

Ingredients

  • 1 pkg fresh spinach
  • 1 carton strawberries, washed and sliced
  • 1/2 cup sugar
  • 1/4 cup white wine vinegar (I like champagne wine vinegar)
  • 1/2 cup vegetable oil
  • pinch of paprika
  • 1 TB poppy seeds
  • 2 TB sesame seeds
  • 1 cup sliced almonds

Instructions

Wash spinach and place in a bowl. Wash and slice strawberries and sprinkle on top of spinach. Sprinkle almonds on top of spinach and strawberries. In a bowl, combine all ingredients using a whisk or place in a mason jar and shake well. Refrigerate until ready to use. Pour dressing over salad and toss. Enjoy!

Sides |

Creamy Mashed Potatoes

Ingredients

  • 5 lbs russet potatoes
  • 1/2 cup sour cream
  • 3/4 cup butter
  • 8 oz pkg cream cheese, softened
  • 1/4 cup of heavy cream (half and half or milk will work)
  • salt and pepper to taste

Instructions

  1. Bring a large pot of water to a boil. (you want a big enough pot so that all the potatoes are covered with water)

  2. Peel and cut potatoes into chunks, trying to keep them the same size.

  3. Add 1 TB salt to water, then carefully add potatoes. (do not cover pot with a lid)

  4. Boil until soft, (pierce with a fork to check) about 25 minutes.

  5. Drain and return potatoes to pot

  6. Using a potato masher,mash potatoes in the pot.

  7. Add butter, sour cream and cream cheese and continue to mash with potato masher.

  8. Add milk* slowly while mashing. They can get too runny easily.

  9. Season with salt and pepper to taste.

9.Serve immediately

**If making ahead of time, let cool to room temperature, slice 5 tb Butter and place on top of potatoes, cover with foil and refrigerate for up to 3 days.
To reheat: let set out for 1-2 hours. Reheat, covered, in oven at 350 degrees for 30 minutes.

"Give thanks to the Lord, for He is good. His love endures forever."
Psalm 136:1

BBQ Baked Beans

Ingredients

  • 1 lb bacon, cut into pieces and cooked
  • 1 can black beans, drained
  • 1 can dark red kidney beans, drained
  • 3 (15 oz) cans pork and beans
  • 1 red onion, finely chopped
  • 1 red bell pepper, finely chopped
  • 4 tsp minced garlic
  • 3 jalapeño peppers, finely chopped (seeds removed, leave seeds if you like it hot)
  • 16 oz BBQ sauce (we like Sweet Baby Ray's)
  • 1 1/2 cup brown sugar
  • 1/2 cup Dijon mustard
  • 2 tsp liquid smoke

Instructions

Preheat oven to 325 degrees.

In a large oven-safe pot, combine all ingredients.

I cut my bacon into pieces and then cook it. Saving a tablespoon of the grease, I add it to the beans for flavor.

Stir well and place in oven.

Bake 1 1/2 hours to 2 hours, stirring occasionally.

Our friends make this on their grill. Hope you enjoy!

Sides |

Bacon Wrapped Asparagus with Dipping Sauce

Ingredients

  • 1 bundle of asparagus, trimmed
  • 10 slices thinly sliced bacon
  • ¼ cup extra virgin olive oil
  • Coarse salt and ground pepper
  • ½ cup mayonnaise
  • 2 tsp Asian chili sauce
  • Juice of 1 freshly squeezed lime
  • 1 tbsp cilantro, finely chopped

Instructions

Preheat oven to 450 degrees.

Trim asparagus (to trim: hold asparagus stalk in the middle and at the end, bend in half. It will break where it needs to be trimmed. Rinse with cool water).

Separate asparagus into bundles of 3 stalks each. Wrap a piece of bacon around each bundle, starting ¼ inch from the bottom; secure with a toothpick.

Place bundles on a rimmed baking sheet. Drizzle with oil and sprinkle with salt and pepper.

Roast, flipping halfway through, until asparagus begin to wilt and bacon browns, about 20–22 minutes.

While asparagus is roasting, in a medium mixing bowl, combine mayonnaise, chili sauce, lime juice, cilantro, ½ tsp salt, and ¼ tsp pepper. Refrigerate up to one week until ready to serve.

Transfer bundles to a platter and serve with the dipping sauce. Enjoy!

Delight yourself in the Lord; And He will give you the desires of your heart
Psalm 37:4

Baked Corn and Wild Rice

Ingredients

  • 2 cups cooked Wild Rice (I use 1 package of Uncle Ben's quick cooking wild rice)
  • 4 Fresh Corn on the Cob, scrape the kernels off the cob
  • 1 cup Cream
  • 6 TB Butter, cubed
  • 2 eggs
  • 1 1/2 tsp Salt
  • 1/4 tsp Cayenne Pepper
  • 1/2 cup Milk (for thinning)

Instructions

Preheat oven to 350 degrees.
Cook wild rice in chicken broth according to package directions. Drain rice and set aside.
While rice is cooking, scrape kernels off corn cob and place in a large mixing bowl.
Add cream, butter, eggs, salt, and cayenne pepper. Stir together.
Add wild rice and stir gently.
If thinning is needed, add milk so that the mixture is stirrable, but not too runny.
Grease a 9" square baking dish with Pam or butter.
Bake for 30-45 minutes or until golden brown on top.
Enjoy!

This recipe is based on one from "The Pioneer Woman."
I tweaked it to make it cook quicker for bible study. Check out her website for the real deal. :)
She has a wonderful, helpful website filled with recipes and more.

"The LORD is the one who goes ahead of you; He will be with you. He will not fail you or forsake you. Do not fear or be dismayed."
Deuteronomy 31:8

Sides |

Baked Sweet Potato & Carrots

Ingredients

  • 2 large carrots, peeled and sliced
  • 2 large sweet potatoes, peeled and cut into 1" cubes
  • 3 tbsp olive oil
  • 1/2 cup dried apricots, cut into strips
  • 1/4 cup golden raisins
  • 1/2 cup brown sugar
  • 1/4 tsp salt
  • 1/4 tsp black pepper
  • 1/2 cup ginger ale
  • 1/4 cup cranberry juice
  • 2 tbsp orange juice

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 350 degrees.

  2. Place carrot slices and sweet potato cubes into a 9x13 baking dish.

  3. Drizzle with olive oil and toss to coat.

  4. Sprinkle salt, pepper, apricots, raisins, and brown sugar on top.

  5. Toss to coat.

  6. Combine ginger ale, cranberry juice, and orange juice, drizzle over the potato mixture.

  7. Cover and bake in a 350-degree oven for 75 minutes or until potatoes are tender, stirring once at 45 minutes.

  8. Uncover after baking for 75 minutes and bake an additional 15 minutes or until the liquid is thick.

  9. Serve and enjoy!

Tips...

  • This can be made ahead - may take an additional 30-45 minutes to cook.

Fried Corn

Ingredients

  • 6 ears of frozen corn, thawed
  • 1 stick of butter
  • 1/3 cup sugar
  • 1 cup milk
  • 1/2 tsp salt

Instructions

  1. Let corn thaw in refrigerator overnight or rinse with warm water to thaw quickly.

  2. With a large knife, cut corn off cob. Scrape edge of cob with knife blade to clean the cob of all kernels.

  3. In a large saucepan, add corn, butter, milk, sugar, and salt. Cook over medium-high heat, stirring often.

  4. When corn begins to boil, lower temperature and let cook for about 30 minutes.

  5. Serve and enjoy!

Kathy's Baked Beans

Ingredients

  • 1 onion, diced
  • 1 lb hamburger meat
  • 1 ¼ tsp salt
  • ½ tsp pepper
  • 1 TB mustard
  • 2 cups ketchup
  • 2 cups brown sugar
  • 1 tsp Worcestershire sauce
  • 15 oz and 31 oz can pork & beans
    • (some pork & beans have excess juice – if so, pour off the excess)

Instructions

In a large pot, brown hamburger meat with onions, salt, and pepper. Drain excess grease off. Add ketchup, mustard, Worcestershire sauce, and brown sugar and cook on medium heat for about 15 minutes, stirring often because the sugar tends to burn.

When the mixture begins to thicken and looks like syrup, add the pork & beans and turn the heat down to low. Cook for 15 more minutes. Serve and enjoy!

Kathy's Fruit & Wild Rice Dressing

Ingredients

  • 1 box chicken wild rice (Uncle Ben's)

  • 1 cup of white or brown rice

  • Water according to rice instructions

  • 1 tbsp butter

  • 2 tsp salt

  • 1 lb bacon

  • 1 cup celery

  • 1/2 cup minced green onion

  • 1 medium onion, diced

  • 4 cups bread cubes or crumbs

  • 2 cups chopped Granny Smith apples

  • 2 cups chicken broth

  • 1 15 oz can cream of chicken

  • 6 oz dried cranberries

  • 1 cup chopped pecans

  • 2 large eggs

  • 1 tsp sage

  • 1 tsp poultry seasoning

  • 1 tsp pepper

  • Salt to taste

Instructions

In a large saucepan, combine wild rice, brown or white rice (Kathy prefers white), water, salt, and butter, and cook according to directions.

Remove from heat and let sit for 10 minutes.

Cook bacon until crispy, remove, and lay on paper towels.

Reserve 2 tbsp of drippings in the pan.
Add celery, onions, and apples to bacon drippings. Cook over medium heat for 5 to 6 minutes or until tender.

Then add crumbled bacon to the pan with onions, celery, and apples. Turn off heat.

In a large bowl, combine rice, bacon, celery, onions, apples, bread crumbs, cranberries, and seasonings.

In a separate bowl, combine chicken broth, cream of chicken, and eggs.

Beat the egg mixture and then pour over the rice mixture. Be sure to toss lightly!

Now pour rice dressing into a 13x9 deep dish pan. Bake in a preheated oven at 350 degrees for 45 minutes.

Scalloped Parmesan Potatoes

Ingredients

  • 3 baking potatoes, peeled and thinly sliced
  • Salt and pepper for seasoning
  • 3 cups whipping cream
  • 1 tablespoon chopped garlic
  • 2 teaspoons salt
  • 1 teaspoon coarsely ground black pepper
  • 5 oz freshly grated Parmesan cheese

Instructions

Preheat oven to 400 degrees.

Spray a 9x13 baking dish with non-stick spray.

Layer potatoes in baking dish, season with salt and pepper.

In a large mixing bowl, combine whipping cream, garlic, 2 teaspoons salt, 1 teaspoon black pepper.

Pour over potatoes.

Bake at 400 degrees for 30 minutes, stirring gently every 10 minutes.

Remove from oven, sprinkle Parmesan cheese on top and return to oven.

Bake for 15-20 minutes or until bubbly and golden brown.

Let stand 10 minutes before serving.

Enjoy!

Sesame Green Beans

Ingredients

  • 1 pound fresh green beans washed and cleaned
  • 1 tablespoon olive oil
  • 3 tablespoons soy sauce
  • 2 teaspoons sesame seeds
  • 2 1/2 teaspoons garlic cloves, minced
  • 4 tablespoons brown sugar
  • 3 tablespoons peanut butter

Instructions

Heat a large skillet on high heat.

When it's hot, add your olive oil.

When the oil is hot, add the green beans.

Turn down heat to medium and cook until the beans are crisp-tender. About 10 minutes. Stir your beans every once in a while to help them cook evenly.

In a medium size mixing bowl, combine the soy sauce, sesame seeds, garlic cloves, brown sugar, and peanut butter.

When the beans are done cooking, pour the sauce on top of the beans and mix it in well.

Cook until the sauce is heated through.

Serve immediately.

Enjoy!

Smashed Garlic Potatoes

Ingredients

  • 18 small red potatoes
  • 1/4 cup extra virgin olive oil
  • 1 stick butter, melted
  • Garlic salt and coarse ground pepper to season

Instructions

Preheat oven to 450 degrees.

Wash red potatoes.
Place in a large pot and cover with water.
Add 1 tablespoon salt and bring water to a boil.
Cook until fork tender.

On a baking sheet with sides, drizzle olive oil.
Place tender potatoes on the baking sheet leaving plenty of room between potatoes.

With a potato masher or fork, gently press down each potato until smashed; lift and press down again in the opposite direction.

Brush the tops of each potato generously with the melted butter.

Sprinkle the potatoes with garlic salt and coarse ground pepper.

Bake in a 450-degree oven for 20-25 minutes or until golden brown.

Serve and enjoy!

The Pioneer Woman says you can add grated cheese to the top...I would agree!

Southwest Black Bean and Rice

Ingredients

  • 2 cups rice
  • 1 can Black Beans, drained and rinsed
  • 1 can Mexican-style Corn, drained
  • 1 can Rotel, slightly drained
  • 1 tsp Cumin
  • ½ tsp Garlic Powder
  • 1 TB Chili Powder
  • ¼ tsp – ½ tsp Ground Red Pepper (according to your taste)
  • 2 cans Cream of Chicken Soup
  • 1 cup Cheddar Jack Cheese, shredded

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 350 degrees.

  2. Spray 9x13 baking dish with cooking spray.

  3. In a large bowl, combine rice, beans, corn, Rotel, cumin, garlic powder, chili powder, red pepper, and cream of chicken soup. Stir to blend.

  4. Pour into baking dish. Bake at 350 for 25-30 minutes or until bubbly.

  5. Sprinkle with cheese 5-8 minutes before done. Serve with sour cream, chopped green onions, and tomatoes. Enjoy!

Sides |

Stir Fry Spaghetti Squash

Ingredients

  • 1/4 cup Olive Oil
  • 1 egg
  • 1 small Spaghetti Squash
  • 1 bunch green onions, chopped
  • 1 head of Broccoli, chopped
  • 2 carrots, peeled and chopped
  • 2 TB Sesame Seeds
  • 1 TB Butter
  • 1/4 - 1/2 cup Soy Sauce
  • Salt and pepper to taste

You can add chicken or pork to this if you want.
You can also add additional vegetables: bell peppers, mushrooms...

Instructions

To prepare Spaghetti Squash:

  1. Cut in half and scoop out seeds.
  2. Wrap each half with plastic wrap and place in microwave for 10 minutes. Poke a fork in the edge close to the skin to see if it's done - if not, continue microwaving for a couple more minutes.
  3. Remove from oven. If you don't want to use it right then, place it in the fridge for no more than 2 days, leaving plastic wrap on.
  4. If you are ready to use, remove plastic wrap and start scraping out with a fork - it will look like spaghetti.

Set aside.

  1. In a large skillet, heat olive oil over medium-high heat.
  2. Add egg and season with salt and pepper. Scramble and cook until done.
  3. Add spaghetti squash, green onions, broccoli, carrots, and sesame seeds. Mix together.
  4. Add butter and season with salt and pepper.
  5. When butter has melted, add soy sauce.
  6. Continue to cook over medium-high to high heat, stirring frequently.

Once vegetables are tender, it's done.

Serve and enjoy!

"So whoever knows the right thing to do and fails to do it, for him it is sin" James 4:17 ESV

Twice Baked Potatoes

Ingredients

  • 7 Baking Potatoes
  • 2 sticks Butter, cut into slices
  • 8 oz Sour Cream
  • 1 pint Heavy Cream
  • 2 tsp (or more) Garlic Salt
  • 2 tsp (or more) Pepper
  • Season these the way you like
  • 1 bunch Green Onions Optional (can be added in the potatoes or used for topping or not at all)
  • 1 pkg Real Bacon Crumbles again Optional
  • 3 cups Shredded Colby Jack Cheese (divided 2 cups and 1 cup)

Instructions

  1. Bake potatoes in oven or microwave until done.

  2. Spray a 9x13 baking pan with cooking spray.

  3. Slice in half and carefully scoop the potato out of its peel. You'll use the shell of the peel to stuff the potato.

  4. Combine the butter, sour cream, and a little of the heavy cream. Mash with potato masher or use an electric mixer to combine.

  5. Add seasonings and mix well.

  6. Stir in 2 cups of grated cheese and onions if you desire.

  7. Start stuffing your potato shells and place them on a prepared baking pan.

  8. Sprinkle remaining cheese on top and add bacon bits and green onions if desired.

  9. Bake in a 350 degree oven for 15-20 minutes.

  10. These can be made ahead up to 3 days or frozen. If you freeze, don't add the green onions. Green onions do not freeze well according to the Pioneer Woman - and I BELIEVE HER :D

  11. Serve and enjoy!

Twice-Baked Sweet Potato

Ingredients

  • 4 Sweet Potatoes
  • 4 TB Brown Sugar
  • 4 TB Butter, room temp
  • 4 oz Cream Cheese, room temp
  • 1/2 tsp Cinnamon
  • 1/4 tsp Nutmeg
  • 1/4 tsp Ginger
  • Pinch of salt and pepper
  • 1 cup Mini Marshmallows

Streusel Topping:

  • 3 TB Flour
  • 1/4 tsp Cinnamon
  • 1/4 cup chopped Walnuts
  • 2 TB Butter
  • 3 TB Brown Sugar

Instructions

  1. Preheat oven to 400 degrees.
  2. Place sweet potatoes on a baking sheet and place in oven.
  3. Bake for 1 hour or until soft.
  4. Let cool and cut in half.
  5. Carefully remove pulp from skin and place pulp in a large mixing bowl.
  6. Place skin of potato on baking sheet and set aside.
  7. In another mixing bowl, combine brown sugar, butter, cream cheese, cinnamon, nutmeg, ginger, salt, and pepper.
  8. Mix well.
  9. Add butter mixture to potato pulp and fold in completely.
  10. Place 5-7 mini marshmallows in potato skin.
  11. Add the potato mixture to the top of the marshmallows.
  12. In another mixing bowl, combine streusel topping ingredients and sprinkle on top of potato.
  13. Place in oven and bake 10-12 minutes or until golden brown.
  14. Serve and enjoy!

Soups |

Baked Potato Soup

Ingredients

  • 4 large baked potatoes
  • 2/3 cup butter
  • 2/3 cup flour
  • 6 cups milk (for a richer flavor, use 3 cups heavy cream and 3 cups milk)
  • 1 tsp salt
  • 1 tsp pepper
  • 1 1/2 cups cheddar cheese, shredded
  • 12 slices cooked bacon, crumbled
  • 4 green onions, chopped and divided
  • 1 (8 oz) ctn sour cream

Instructions

  1. Wash potatoes; prick several times with a fork. Bake at 400 degrees for 1 hour or until done; cool.

  2. Cut potatoes in half lengthwise; scoop out pulp, and reserve. Discard shells.

  3. Melt butter in large pan over low heat; add flour, stirring until smooth. Cook 1 minute, stirring constantly. Gradually add milk; cook over medium heat, stirring constantly, until thickened and bubbly.

  4. Stir in potato, salt, pepper, 1-cup cheese, half of the crumbled bacon, and half of the green onions; cook until heated (DO NOT BOIL). Stir in sour cream; cook until just heated (DO NOT BOIL).

  5. Serve. Sprinkle servings with remaining cheese, green onions, and bacon.

"But now abide faith, hope, and love, these three; but the greatest of these is love."
1 Corinthians 13:13

Beef and Bean Chili

Ingredients

  • 4 stalks celery, chopped
  • 3 green onions, chopped
  • 2 cloves garlic, minced
  • 1 yellow onion, chopped
  • 1 green bell pepper, chopped
  • 1 tablespoon vegetable oil
  • 2 lbs ground beef
  • 15 oz can tomato sauce
  • 6 oz can tomato paste
  • 16oz beef broth
  • 6 tablespoons chili powder
  • 1 teaspoon salt
  • 1 teaspoon pepper
  • 16 oz can kidney beans, undrained

Extras:

  • Fritos
  • Cheddar cheese
  • Sour cream

Instructions

In a large stock pot, heat oil over medium-high heat.

Add celery, green onions, garlic, yellow onion, and bell pepper. Cook for 5 minutes or until tender.

Add ground beef and cook until browned and crumbled. Drain grease.

Add tomato sauce, tomato paste,beef broth, chili powder, salt, and pepper. Stir well and bring to a boil; reduce heat, and simmer, uncovered for 30 minutes or to desired consistency.

Add beans during the last 15 minutes. Serve and enjoy!

Top with grated cheese and sour cream.

Make individual Frito pies - using snack-size Fritos, spoon chili on top of Fritos, top with sour cream and grated cheese.

"See, I am doing a new thing! Now it springs up; do you not perceive it? I am making a way in the wilderness and streams in the wasteland." Isaiah 43:19

Black Bean Soup

Ingredients

  • 2 TB olive oil
  • 1 large yellow onion, chopped
  • 3 ribs celery, chopped
  • 4 tsp garlic, chopped
  • 1 jalapeno pepper, seeded and chopped (leave the seeds in if you like heat)
  • 1 red bell pepper, seeded and chopped
  • 3 (15oz) cans black beans
  • 2 TB cumin
  • Salt and pepper
  • 2-3 TB hot sauce
  • 32oz chicken or vegetable broth
  • 15oz can Rotel (you can use diced tomatoes or fire-roasted tomatoes)

Instructions

  1. Heat a soup pan over medium-high heat.

  2. Add 2 TB olive oil to hot pan.

  3. Add onion, celery, garlic, and jalapeno.

  4. Stir and cook for 3-4 minutes, being careful not to burn garlic.

  5. Add red peppers and continue to cook.

  6. Drain 2 cans of the black beans and add them to the pan.

  7. Pour the remaining can of black beans into a bowl, juice and all. Mash with a fork and add to the pot.

  8. Stir well and season with cumin, salt, pepper, and hot sauce.

  9. Add broth and Rotel, stir, and bring to a boil.

  10. Reduce heat and simmer for 15 minutes over low heat.

  11. Can be served topped with sour cream and green onions. Enjoy!

“Holy, Holy, Holy is the Lord Almighty: the whole earth is full of His glory.” Isaiah 6:3

Broccoli Cheese Soup "Quick and Easy"

Ingredients

  • 1 can cream of chicken soup
  • 1 1/2 cups of milk
  • 16 oz Velveeta cheese
  • 10 oz pkg frozen broccoli, thawed and rinsed

Instructions

In medium saucepan, add cream of chicken soup, milk, Velveeta cheese (cut up in small pieces), and broccoli.
Cook on medium heat until all melted. Stir and be careful not to burn.
Serve and enjoy!

Soups |

Cheesy Chicken Chowder

Ingredients

  • 3 cans chicken broth
  • 3 potatoes, peeled and diced
  • 4 celery stalks, diced
  • 4 carrots, peeled and diced
  • 1 yellow onion, diced
  • 1 can corn, drained
  • 1/3 cup butter
  • 1/3 cup flour
  • 2 tbsp corn starch
  • 3 cups milk
  • 1 tbsp soy sauce
  • 1 tsp red pepper
  • 1 lb Velveeta, cubed
  • 3 chicken breasts, cooked and shredded OR
  • 1 cooked rotisserie chicken, deboned and shredded

Instructions

In a large pot combine chicken broth, potatoes, celery, carrots, onion, and corn. Cover and bring to a slow boil for 15 minutes or until vegetables are tender when pricked with a fork.

In a medium saucepan, melt butter over low heat. Add flour and corn starch. Let simmer for 1 minute and then increase temperature to medium. Add milk gradually, stirring constantly. Continue to stir until thick and bubbly. (This happens suddenly)

Add flour mixture to vegetables. Add soy sauce, cheese, and chicken. Continue to stir until cheese is melted.

Serve and enjoy!

Chicken Enchilada Soup

Ingredients

  • 6 chicken breasts (cooked and shredded) I like to use the meat from 2 Rotisserie Chickens
  • 4 cans chicken broth
  • 1 cup butter
  • 1 1/2 cups flour
  • 1 onion. finely chopped
  • 2 garlic cloves, minced
  • 1 tsp salt
  • 2 tsp paprika
  • 1 tsp cumin
  • 1 can chopped green chilies
  • 2 pints sour cream

Instructions

In a large saucepan or Dutch oven, melt butter and sauté onions and garlic.
Stir in 1 can of chicken broth and flour.
Mix well and cook for 2 minutes.
Add the rest of the chicken broth, salt, paprika, and cumin.
Stir constantly till thick and bubbly.
Add chicken, sour cream, and chilis.
Heat till warm but DO NOT boil.

Serve topped with shredded cheese, tortilla chips, and chopped green onions.

Enjoy!

Soups |

Chicken Noodle Soup

Ingredients

  • 32 oz Chicken Broth
  • 4 chicken breasts
  • 1 chicken bouillon cube
  • 1 carrot
  • 1 celery stalk
  • 1/2 yellow onion
  • 1 tsp salt
  • 1 tsp pepper
  • 1 15 oz can cream of chicken soup
  • 1 1/2 cups milk
  • 1 (12 oz) pkg egg noodles

Instructions

  1. Cook egg noodles according to package directions.

  2. Clean and cut up chicken breasts into bite-sized pieces.

  3. In a large stock pot, bring chicken broth to a boil.

  4. While water is starting to boil, cut up carrot, celery, and onion.

  5. To boiling water, add bouillon package, carrot, celery, onion, and chicken. Cook until chicken turns done (about 6 minutes).

  6. Add cream of chicken soup and milk. Cook on medium heat until it starts to boil (about 5 minutes).

  7. Add cooked egg noodles. Cook a couple more minutes.

  8. Serve and enjoy!

"May the Lord direct your hearts into God's love and Christ's perseverance."
2 Thessalonians 3:5

Soups |

Chicken and Wild Rice Soup

Ingredients

  • 2 cans chicken broth
  • 3 cooked chicken breasts, chopped or shredded (i like to use a rotisserie chicken)
  • 2 cups water
  • 1 (4.5 oz) quick cooking wild rice and seasoning packet
  • 1/2 tsp salt
  • 1/2 tsp pepper
  • 3/4 cup flour
  • 1/2 cup butter
  • 2 cups whipping cream

Instructions

In a large pot over medium heat, combine chicken broth, water, and cooked chicken breasts.
Bring to a boil, then stir in the rice, reserving the seasoning packet for later.
Cover and remove from heat.

In a small bowl, combine salt, pepper, and flour. In a medium saucepan, melt butter over medium heat.
Add seasoning packet and stir until bubbly.
Reduce heat to low, then whisk in flour mixture by tablespoons, to form a roux.
Whisk in whipping cream, 1/2 cup at a time, until mixed and smooth.
Cook until thickened, 2-5 minutes.
Remove from heat.
Return large pot to medium heat, stir in cream mixture into broth and rice.
Cook until heated about 10-15 minutes, stirring occasionally.
Enjoy!

"Now faith is being sure of what we hope for and certain of what we do not see" Hebrews 11:1

Soups |

Classic Corn Chowder

Ingredients

  • 4 oz Butter

  • 1 Small Onion, finely chopped

  • 10-12 oz frozen corn kernels or 5 ears of shucked fresh corn

  • 2 small baking potatoes, peeled and diced

  • 1/2 tsp garlic powder

  • 1/4 tsp cumin

  • 1/2 tsp Salt

  • 2 cups chicken or vegetable broth

  • 1/2 cup half-and-half

    Use cream instead of half-and-half for a thicker, richer soup

    Use 1/8 tsp Turmeric for a bright yellow chowder

Instructions

  1. Sauté onions and butter in a large stock pot over medium low heat for 4-5 minutes.

  2. Add corn, potatoes, garlic powder, cumin, and salt and continue cooking for 5-6 minutes.

  3. Add broth, bring to a boil, and simmer 18-20 minutes until all the vegetables are soft.

  4. Add half-and-half and simmer until hot.

  5. Garnish with chives, sour cream, and bacon.

  6. Enjoy!

"Don't expect anyone else to fully understand both the bitterness and the joys of all you experience in your life" Proverbs 14:10 TPT

Soups |

Friendship Soup in a Jar

Ingredients

To layer in pint jar:

  • 1/3 cup uncooked long-grain white rice
  • 1/3 cup dry split peas
  • 1/4 cup pearl barley
  • 1/4 cup dried onion flakes
  • 1/3 cup beef bouillon granules
  • 1/3 cup dry lentils
  • 2 teaspoons dried Italian seasoning
  • 1/3 cup uncooked alphabet pasta

Additional ingredients:

  • 1 lb ground beef
  • Salt, pepper, and garlic salt to season meat with
  • 28 oz can diced tomatoes, undrained
  • 6 oz can tomato paste
  • 3 quarts of water (12 cups)

Instructions

  1. In a pint jar, layer the rice, split peas, pearl barley, onion flakes, bouillon, lentils, Italian seasoning, and pasta. Seal tightly.
  2. Attach a label to the jar with the following instructions:

Friendship Soup

ADDITIONAL INGREDIENTS: 1 pound ground beef, black pepper to taste, garlic powder to taste, 1 (28 ounce) can diced tomatoes - undrained, 1 (6 ounce) can tomato paste, and 3 quarts water.

TO PREPARE SOUP:

In a large pot over medium heat, brown beef with salt, pepper, and garlic salt. Add diced tomatoes, tomato paste, water, and soup mix. Bring to a boil, then reduce heat to low. Cover, and simmer for 45 minutes or until the pasta, peas, lentils, and barley are tender.

Larry's Stew Chili

Ingredients

  • 1 tbsp vegetable oil
  • 1 lb stew meat
  • 1 lb hamburger meat
  • Salt
  • Pepper
  • 2 tsp chili powder
  • 1 can pinto beans, drained and rinsed
  • 1 can corn, drained and rinsed
  • 16 oz jar thick and chunky salsa
  • 1/4 cup BBQ sauce
  • 1 pkg gravy mix
  • 16 oz can fire-roasted diced tomatoes

Instructions

  1. Cut stew meat into bite-size pieces.
  2. In a Dutch oven, heat oil over medium heat.
  3. Add stew meat, hamburger meat, and chili powder.
  4. Season with salt and pepper.
  5. Cook until no longer pink.
  6. Add remaining ingredients.
  7. Bring to a boil, then simmer.
  8. Serve with cornbread and potato skins.

Enjoy!

a large stock pot, melt butter over medium heat.

Spicy Black Bean Chili

Ingredients

  • 1 small yellow onion, chopped
  • 1 lb ground beef
  • 3 (15 oz) cans black beans, drain and rinse 2 of the 3 cans
  • 2 (14.5 oz) cans diced fire roasted tomatoes
  • 2 tablespoons chili powder
  • 1 teaspoon cumin
  • 1 teaspoon salt
  • 1 teaspoon pepper
  • 1 can chicken broth
  • 1 can chipotle peppers in adobo sauce, chopped

Instructions

  1. Rinse and drain 2 cans of black beans.

  2. Chop onion.

  3. Chop chipotle peppers.

  4. In a large stock pot over medium heat, add ground beef and chopped onions.

  5. Cook until meat is no longer pink, about 6 minutes.

  6. Stir in chili powder, cumin, salt, and pepper.

  7. Add drained and undrained black beans, chicken broth, fire roasted tomatoes, and chipotle peppers.

  8. Bring to a boil; cover, reduce heat to low, and simmer at least 10 minutes.

  9. Top with cheese and sour cream.

  10. Serve with pepper jack cornbread muffins* (recipe on website)

  11. Enjoy!

Soups |

Spicy Chicken Chili

Ingredients

  • 4 tbsp olive oil
  • 1 medium yellow onion, chopped
  • 4 cloves garlic, chopped
  • 1 1/2 tsp cumin
  • 1 tsp salt
  • 1 tsp paprika
  • 1 large chipotle chile in adobo sauce, finely chopped, sauce reserved
  • or 1 poblano pepper, finely chopped
    • If using poblano pepper, you'll need 1 small can of chipotle sauce
  • 3 chicken breasts, thinly sliced and chopped
  • 2 (14.5 oz) cans diced tomatoes with juice
  • 2 (15 oz) cans chili beans with juice
  • 15 oz can black beans, drained and rinsed

Instructions

  1. In a large pot, heat the olive oil over medium heat.

  2. Add the onions, garlic, cumin, salt, paprika and chipotle or poblano pepper.

  3. Cook, stirring occasionally, until the onion is softened. About 7 minutes.

  4. Add the chicken and cook, stirring, until the meat begins to change color, about 5-7 minutes.

  5. Stir in the tomatoes, beans, and 1 tbsp of adobo sauce or 1 can of chipotle sauce.

  6. Bring to a boil.

  7. Lower the heat and simmer the chili, stirring often, until thickened, about 25-30 minutes.

  8. Add more of the adobo sauce to taste, if desired.

  9. Serve with grated cheese and sour cream.

ENJOY!

Psalm 139:1-4

Spicy Chicken Soup

Ingredients

  • The meat of 1 rotisserie chicken, shredded
  • 3 TB olive oil
  • 1 yellow onion, chopped
  • 3 cloves garlic, chopped (or 3 tsp.)
  • 16oz jar chunky salsa
  • 1 can Rotel
  • 14.5oz canned diced tomatoes
  • 14.5oz can whole peeled tomatoes
  • 1 15oz can tomato soup
  • 3 TB chili powder
  • 15oz can corn, drained
  • 15oz can black beans, drained
  • 16oz can chili beans, undrained
  • 2 ½ cans chicken broth (5 cups)

Topping:

  • Sour cream
  • Shredded cheddar jack cheese

Instructions

  1. In a large stock pot, heat olive oil on medium-high heat.

  2. Add onions and garlic and cook until softened.

  3. Add shredded chicken and the rest of the ingredients.

  4. Simmer for 30 minutes – do not boil.

  5. Serve topped with a dollop of sour cream and cheese!

Enjoy!

Ingredients

  • 1 tsp salt

White Chicken Chili

Ingredients

  • 3 TB Vegetable Oil
  • 1 Yellow Onion, chopped
  • Meat from 1 rotisserie chicken
  • 2 cans Chicken Broth
  • 2 (4oz) cans chopped Green Chilis
  • 2 (15oz) cans Great Northern Beans, undrained
  • 1 can Cream of Chicken Soup
  • 1 TB Garlic Powder
  • 2 tsp ground Red Pepper
  • 2 tsp ground Cumin
  • 1 tsp Oregano

Optional:
flour tortillas, sour cream mozzarella cheese

Instructions

In a large pot over medium-high heat, heat vegetable oil.
Add onions and cook until onions are clear.
Add chicken and saute for 2-3 minutes.
Add remaining ingredients - chicken broth, green chilis, great northern beans, cream of chicken soup, garlic powder, cumin, oregano, and red pepper.
Bring to a boil, then reduce heat and simmer for at least 30 minutes.
Cut flour tortilla into 1/4 inch strips, place in the bottom of bowl.
Ladle chicken chili on top.
Garnish with sour cream and grated mozzarella cheese.
Enjoy!
